Nature Communications · 2026

一种磁性软、力学强且塑性好的无钽 CoFeNi 高熵合金（添加 Al 与 Ti）A magnetically soft yet mechanically strong and ductile Ta free CoFeNi high entropy alloy with Al and Ti additions

Nat. Commun. (2026) 17:2890 · doi:10.1038/s41467-026-68891-6 · Sudip Kumar Sarkar, Nachiket Keskar, Li Ping Tan, Tirthesh Ingale, Advika Chesetti, Sriswaroop Dasari, Karl P. Davidson, V. Chaudhary, Narendra Dahotre, R. V. Ramanujan, Rajarshi Banerjee
本页为「摘要 + 正文」中文翻译；已省略 Methods（方法）与参考文献。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Next generation high speed, large torque, rotating electrical machines demand materials with an excellent balance of magnetic and mechanical properties. High entropy alloys (HEAs) can exhibit this unique combination of properties, e.g., CoFeNi based HEAs containing Ta. The high Ta content makes these alloys prohibitively expensive for industrial applications. Hence, we develop Ta free HEA using a thermodynamic approach. The addition of Al and Ti to the base CoFeNi alloy is studied. We investigate two compositions: Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 and Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi. In their solution treated and quenched condition these HEAs exhibit a low coercivity Hc - 1.8–2.4 Oe, high saturation magnetization Ms - 96.5-137.3 emu/g, coupled with a high yield strength, YS - 780-850 MPa, ultimate tensile strength, UTS - 1200 MPa, large tensile elongation, ε - 29-35%, and with desired high electrical resistivity ( - 269.5-308.3 μΩ·cm), making them attractive for high frequency electrical machines. Importantly, the latter HEA is further strengthened to a very high YS - 1140-1200 MPa (ε - 18-25%) and electrical resistivity (296.1-459.3 μΩ·cm), but at the cost of higher Hc values - 8.8-48.6 Oe.
下一代高速、大扭矩旋转电机要求材料在磁性能与力学性能之间取得优异平衡。高熵合金（HEA）能够呈现这种独特的性能组合，例如含 Ta 的 CoFeNi 基高熵合金。但高 Ta 含量使这类合金在工业应用中过于昂贵。为此，我们采用热力学方法开发无钽（Ta-free）高熵合金，研究在基础 CoFeNi 合金中添加 Al 和 Ti 的效果。我们考察了两种成分：Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 和 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi。在固溶处理并淬火的状态下，这些高熵合金表现出低矫顽力（Hc 约 1.8–2.4 Oe）、高饱和磁化强度（Ms 约 96.5–137.3 emu/g），同时具有高屈服强度（YS 约 780–850 MPa）、抗拉强度（UTS 约 1200 MPa）、大拉伸延伸率（ε 约 29–35%），以及理想的高电阻率（约 269.5–308.3 μΩ·cm），使其对高频电机颇具吸引力。值得注意的是，后一种高熵合金可进一步强化到很高的 YS（1140–1200 MPa，ε 约 18–25%）和电阻率（296.1–459.3 μΩ·cm），但代价是矫顽力升高（Hc 约 8.8–48.6 Oe）。
Soft magnetic materials (SMMs) play a pivotal role in rotating electrical machines, which underpin a wide range of modern energy conversion and mobility technologies. The rising global emphasis on reducing fossil fuel consumption and greenhouse gas emissions has accelerated the adoption of electric machines in electric vehicles, aircraft, and other technological sectors. According to the U.S. Energy Information Administration's Monthly Energy Review (April 2024), nearly 20% of the electrical energy generated in the U.S. power sector is lost before reaching its end use, underscoring the urgent need for improved efficiency in electrical systems. Enhancing efficiency requires materials that combine optimal magnetic, electrical, and mechanical properties, or materials with tunable multifunctional characteristics, to meet the demands of next-generation motors, e.g., for electric vehicles, designed for large torque, high-frequency operation, and more compact architectures. The essential requirements for SMMs include: (i) low coercivity (Hc) to minimize hysteresis losses during rapid magnetization–demagnetization cycles, (ii) high saturation magnetization (Ms) for maximizing magnetic flux density and torque, and (iii) elevated electrical resistivity (ρ) to reduce eddy current losses. Equally critical are superior mechanical properties, particularly high yield strength to prevent plastic deformation under cyclic stresses and adequate ductility for manufacturing flexibility and damage tolerance. Meeting this complex set of requirements would substantially expand the application envelope of SMMs and reduce energy losses across electrical machines.
软磁材料（SMM）在旋转电机中发挥着关键作用，而旋转电机支撑着现代众多的能量转换与出行技术。全球日益重视减少化石燃料消耗和温室气体排放，加速了电机在电动汽车、飞行器及其他技术领域的应用。据美国能源信息署《月度能源评论》（2024 年 4 月），美国电力部门发出的电能中有近 20% 在到达终端用途之前就已损失，凸显了提升电气系统效率的迫切需求。提高效率需要在磁、电、力学性能上均达到最优的材料，或具有可调多功能特性的材料，以满足下一代电机（如电动汽车用电机）对大扭矩、高频运行和更紧凑结构的要求。软磁材料的基本要求包括：(i) 低矫顽力（Hc），以最大限度减少快速磁化–退磁循环中的磁滞损耗；(ii) 高饱和磁化强度（Ms），以最大化磁通密度和扭矩；(iii) 较高的电阻率（ρ），以降低涡流损耗。同等关键的还有优异的力学性能，尤其是能在循环应力下防止塑性变形的高屈服强度，以及能提供加工灵活性和损伤容限的足够塑性。满足这一复杂的综合要求，将大幅拓展软磁材料的应用范围，并降低各类电机的能量损耗。
Despite decades of progress, simultaneously achieving this balance of properties remains a formidable challenge. Conventional SMMs—including Fe-Si silicon steels (3–6 wt.% Si), Fe–Ni permalloys, Fe–Ni–Mo supermalloys, and Fe–Co alloys such as Hiperco 50 and Hiperco 50HS—are widely used in electrical machines. These alloys exhibit low Hc (0.01–5.0 Oe) and high Ms (64–235 emu/g) values, yet they fall short in mechanical strength (maximum ~624–810 MPa in Hiperco 50HS) and electrical resistivity (27–90 μΩ·cm). Such limitations are particularly acute in high-torque, high-frequency motor applications in automotive and aviation use cases, where premature mechanical failure and excessive eddy current losses are prevalent. Therefore, future progress in electrical machine design hinges on engineering single-composition soft magnetic materials that possess the appropriate magnetic, electrical, and mechanical property set.
尽管已有数十年进展，同时实现上述性能平衡仍是一项艰巨挑战。传统软磁材料——包括 Fe-Si 硅钢（3–6 wt.% Si）、Fe–Ni 坡莫合金、Fe–Ni–Mo 超坡莫合金，以及 Hiperco 50、Hiperco 50HS 等 Fe–Co 合金——被广泛用于电机。这些合金具有低 Hc（0.01–5.0 Oe）和高 Ms（64–235 emu/g），但在力学强度（Hiperco 50HS 最高约 624–810 MPa）和电阻率（27–90 μΩ·cm）方面存在不足。这些局限在汽车和航空领域的高扭矩、高频电机应用中尤为突出，那里普遍存在过早的机械失效和过大的涡流损耗。因此，电机设计的未来进展有赖于开发出兼具适当磁、电、力学性能组合的单一成分软磁材料。
Recent advances in high-entropy alloys (HEAs) and complex concentrated alloys (CCAs) have created new opportunities for designing alloys with enhanced multifunctional properties. By combining multiple principal magnetic elements, HEAs offer a platform to simultaneously tailor magnetic, structural, and mechanical characteristics. The alloying elements and processing conditions are decisive: they influence phase stability, microstructural evolution, and interfacial characteristics, which, in turn, can affect the relevant properties. HEAs containing concentrated amounts of ferromagnetic elements, such as Co, Fe, and Ni, can exhibit promising soft magnetic properties. Further, the addition of atoms such as Al and Ti, which exhibit large negative enthalpies of mixing with these ferromagnetic elements, promotes chemical ordering within face-centered cubic (FCC) solid solutions, often through the formation of intermetallic nanoprecipitates such as the L12 or the B2 phases.
高熵合金（HEA）和复杂浓缩合金（CCA）的最新进展，为设计具有增强多功能性能的合金创造了新机遇。通过组合多种主量级磁性元素，高熵合金提供了同时调控磁、结构和力学特性的平台。合金元素与工艺条件起决定作用：它们影响相稳定性、组织演化和界面特征，进而影响相关性能。含有浓缩铁磁性元素（如 Co、Fe、Ni）的高熵合金可表现出优良的软磁性能。此外，加入 Al、Ti 等与这些铁磁性元素具有较大负混合焓的原子，会促进面心立方（FCC）固溶体内的化学有序化，通常通过形成 L12 或 B2 相等金属间化合物纳米析出相来实现。
The ternary CoFeNi alloy has emerged as a promising base for soft magnetic HEAs. It exhibits excellent magnetic properties, and the interaction energies of the three binary pairs (Fe–Ni, Fe–Co, Co–Ni) are nearly zero, yielding a near-ideal random solid solution. Equiatomic CoFeNi forms a single-phase FCC structure, which offers excellent ductility but insufficient mechanical strength. Various strengthening mechanisms have been applied, including solid solution strengthening, grain refinement, dislocation strengthening, and multiphase strengthening. Of these, nanoprecipitation strengthening via coherent L12-type precipitates is especially effective, as established in Ni-based superalloys (γ + γ′ structure), Co-based alloys (e.g., Co84Ti8V8), and Fe-based systems. Building on this principle, Al or Ti additions to CoFeNi can induce coherent L12 nanoprecipitates in the FCC matrix, yielding significant strengthening without severely compromising ductility. For instance, Han et al. reported a CoFeNi–Ta/Al HEA containing >42 vol.% ordered L12 nanoprecipitates, which achieved a high yield strength (~1202 MPa) and good tensile ductility (~15%), but exhibited relatively high coercivity (~10.7 Oe). Similarly, Xu et al. studied (FeCoNi)90–TixAlx alloys, with (FeCoNi)90–Ti5Al5 composition as optimal. This alloy contained <1 nm nanoprecipitates with ~9.8% volume fraction, yielding a balanced property set: YS = 463 MPa, ductility = 50.7%, Ms = 121.1 emu/g, and Hc = 6.5 Oe.
三元 CoFeNi 合金已成为软磁高熵合金颇具前景的基体。它具有优异的磁性能，且三个二元对（Fe–Ni、Fe–Co、Co–Ni）的相互作用能几乎为零，形成近乎理想的随机固溶体。等原子比 CoFeNi 形成单相 FCC 结构，塑性极佳但力学强度不足。人们已应用多种强化机制，包括固溶强化、晶粒细化、位错强化和多相强化。其中，通过共格 L12 型析出相实现的纳米析出强化尤为有效，这一点在镍基高温合金（γ + γ′ 结构）、钴基合金（如 Co84Ti8V8）和铁基体系中都已确立。基于这一原理，向 CoFeNi 中添加 Al 或 Ti 可在 FCC 基体中诱导出共格的 L12 纳米析出相，在不严重损害塑性的前提下带来显著强化。例如，Han 等报道了一种含 >42 vol.% 有序 L12 纳米析出相的 CoFeNi–Ta/Al 高熵合金，实现了高屈服强度（约 1202 MPa）和良好拉伸塑性（约 15%），但矫顽力相对较高（约 10.7 Oe）。类似地，Xu 等研究了 (FeCoNi)90–TixAlx 合金，以 (FeCoNi)90–Ti5Al5 成分为最优。该合金含有体积分数约 9.8%、尺寸 <1 nm 的纳米析出相，性能均衡：YS = 463 MPa、塑性 = 50.7%、Ms = 121.1 emu/g、Hc = 6.5 Oe。
Phase stability in Co- and Ni-based HEAs is highly sensitive to Al and Ti content. Prior work on Al-containing CoCrFeNi CCAs revealed compositional transitions from FCC/L12 to FCC/L12 + BCC/B2, and finally to BCC/B2 with increasing Al content. In FCC-based HEAs, Al often stabilizes metastable/stable L12 and/or B2 nanoprecipitates. The simultaneous addition of Ti has proven effective in stabilizing and increasing the mass fraction of the L12 phase. For example, a CoCrFeNi base alloy with 4 at.% Al + 2 at.% Ti achieved strength ≥1 GPa and ductility ~17%, while a CoFeNi base alloy with 8.5 at.% Al + 5.7 at.% Ti reached even higher ductility (~50%) alongside excellent strength.
Co 基和 Ni 基高熵合金的相稳定性对 Al、Ti 含量高度敏感。此前对含 Al 的 CoCrFeNi 复杂浓缩合金的研究表明，随 Al 含量增加，成分演化依次经历 FCC/L12 → FCC/L12 + BCC/B2，最终到 BCC/B2。在 FCC 基高熵合金中，Al 常常稳定亚稳/稳定的 L12 和/或 B2 纳米析出相。同时加入 Ti 已被证明能有效稳定并提高 L12 相的质量分数。例如，添加 4 at.% Al + 2 at.% Ti 的 CoCrFeNi 基合金实现了 ≥1 GPa 的强度和约 17% 的塑性；而添加 8.5 at.% Al + 5.7 at.% Ti 的 CoFeNi 基合金则在优异强度之外达到了更高的塑性（约 50%）。
Prior investigations into an Al0.3CoFeNi HEA underscored the pivotal role of Al in inducing ordered tendencies. This alloy exhibits a combination of long-range ordered (LRO) phases and a disordered FCC solid solution. At 500 °C, L12 nanoprecipitates enhanced hardness and tensile yield stress, while further thermo-mechanical processing yielded a hierarchical four-phase microstructure (FCC + L12 + BCC + B2). Depending on the morphology, this complex microstructure delivered exceptional mechanical performance, with the microstructure with multi-scale hierarchical precipitates achieving yield stresses up to ~1490 MPa, ultimate tensile strengths of ~1663 MPa, and ductility of ~12%. However, these mechanical gains were offset by high coercivity (~160 Oe), presenting an opportunity for further microstructure engineering and alloy development.
此前对 Al0.3CoFeNi 高熵合金的研究强调了 Al 在诱导有序化倾向中的关键作用。该合金呈现长程有序（LRO）相与无序 FCC 固溶体的组合。在 500 °C 时，L12 纳米析出相提高了硬度和拉伸屈服应力；进一步的热机械加工则产生了分级的四相组织（FCC + L12 + BCC + B2）。视形貌不同，这种复杂组织带来了出众的力学性能——具有多尺度分级析出相的组织实现了高达约 1490 MPa 的屈服应力、约 1663 MPa 的抗拉强度和约 12% 的塑性。然而，这些力学增益被高矫顽力（约 160 Oe）所抵消，这也为进一步的组织工程和合金开发留下了空间。
These findings highlight the central challenge: while introducing ordered nanoprecipitates significantly enhances strength, it often increases coercivity. Achieving a low-coercivity, high-strength microstructure thus requires careful stabilization of nanoscale L12 phases while avoiding large mass fractions of the magnetically deleterious B2 phase or coarse lamellar morphologies.
这些发现凸显了核心难题：引入有序纳米析出相在显著提高强度的同时，往往也会增大矫顽力。因此，要获得低矫顽力、高强度的组织，就必须精心稳定纳米级 L12 相，同时避免出现大量对磁性有害的 B2 相或粗大的片层形貌。
In this study, we build upon the previous work on CoFeNi and Al0.3CoFeNi alloys by introducing Ti as an additional alloying element to enhance the stability and volume fraction of ordered L12 nanoprecipitates within an FCC matrix. Solution thermodynamic modeling guided our alloy design. Our central hypothesis is that increasing the fraction of fully coherent nanoscale L12 precipitates will simultaneously provide high yield strength and preserve low coercivity. As established in superalloy design, Ti is a potent L12 stabilizer, and we hypothesized that its addition to CoFeNi and Al0.3CoFeNi-based alloys would yield HEAs with a unique combination of properties: high strength, high ductility, low coercivity, high saturation magnetization, and elevated electrical resistivity. Such alloys would represent a significant advance toward next-generation SMMs for high-frequency, high-torque electrical machines.
本研究在此前 CoFeNi 和 Al0.3CoFeNi 合金工作的基础上，引入 Ti 作为额外的合金元素，以增强 FCC 基体内有序 L12 纳米析出相的稳定性和体积分数。溶液热力学建模为我们的合金设计提供指导。我们的核心假设是：提高完全共格的纳米级 L12 析出相的比例，可同时提供高屈服强度并保持低矫顽力。正如高温合金设计中所确立的，Ti 是强效的 L12 稳定剂；我们据此假设，将其加入 CoFeNi 和 Al0.3CoFeNi 基合金，可获得具有独特性能组合的高熵合金：高强度、高塑性、低矫顽力、高饱和磁化强度和较高电阻率。此类合金将代表着面向高频、高扭矩电机的下一代软磁材料的重要进步。

结果 —— 热力学建模Results — Thermodynamic modeling

Pseudo-binary sections of the TixAl0.3CoFeNi and Ti–CoFeNi systems, calculated using Thermo-Calc ® with the TCHEA6 database, are shown in Figs. 1a and 1b, respectively. In both cases, alloy compositions were chosen to maximize Ti incorporation while preserving a stable FCC single-phase field at high temperatures: Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi (Al8.5Ti5.7Co28.5Fe28.5Ni28.5 in atom %) for the quinary system and Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 (Ti8Co33Fe33Ni26 in atom %) for the quaternary system. The corresponding phase fraction diagrams (Figs. 1b, d) indicate that both alloys transform from a high-temperature FCC phase into multiphase fields containing ordered intermetallics upon cooling. Specifically, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy is predicted to exhibit FCC + L12 + B2 stability between 800 and 1150 °C, while the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy is expected to form FCC + L12 phases within this temperature range.
图 1a 和 1b 分别给出了用 Thermo-Calc ® 配合 TCHEA6 数据库计算的 TixAl0.3CoFeNi 与 Ti–CoFeNi 体系的伪二元截面。两种情形下，合金成分的选取都以在保持高温稳定 FCC 单相区的前提下最大化 Ti 的加入量为原则：五元体系取 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi（原子分数 Al8.5Ti5.7Co28.5Fe28.5Ni28.5），四元体系取 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78（原子分数 Ti8Co33Fe33Ni26）。相应的相分数图（图 1b、d）表明，两种合金在冷却时都从高温 FCC 相转变为含有序金属间化合物的多相区。具体而言，预测 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金在 800–1150 °C 之间稳定为 FCC + L12 + B2；而 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金在此温度区间内预期形成 FCC + L12 相。
To experimentally validate these predictions, homogenized and cold-rolled (90%) samples of both alloys were solutionized in the high-temperature FCC field (1200 °C for Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi, 1000 °C for Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78) and water-quenched. Subsequent annealing treatments were performed within the predicted multiphase regions: the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy was annealed at 800 °C for 4 h after solutionizing or, alternatively, annealed directly at 900 °C for 4 h from the cold-rolled state; the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy was annealed at 1000 °C for 15 min. These conditions were selected to promote the evolution of ordered L12 and B2 phases from the disordered FCC matrix.
为在实验上验证这些预测，将两种合金经均匀化并冷轧（90%）的样品在高温 FCC 相区固溶（Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 为 1200 °C，Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 为 1000 °C）后水淬。随后在预测的多相区内进行退火处理：Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金在固溶后于 800 °C 退火 4 h，或从冷轧态直接在 900 °C 退火 4 h；Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金在 1000 °C 退火 15 min。选取这些条件旨在促进有序 L12 和 B2 相从无序 FCC 基体中的演化。

拉伸性能Tensile properties

The tensile behavior of Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 and Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi HEAs at room temperature under three different conditions is summarized in Fig. 2a. In the solutionized and water-quenched state (CRSA), the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy exhibited a yield strength (YS) of ~850 MPa, ultimate tensile strength (UTS) of ~1200 MPa, and elongation to failure of 29%. Under the same condition, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy showed slightly lower strength (YS ~ 780 MPa, UTS - 1110 MPa) but superior ductility (35%). Subsequent isothermal annealing of the solutionized Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy at 800 °C for 4 h (CRSA800) markedly enhanced strength, with YS ~ 1200 MPa and UTS ~ 1420 MPa, while maintaining 25% ductility. In the cold-rolled (90%) plus directly annealed (900 °C/4 h) condition (CR900), the alloy displayed YS ~ 1140 MPa, UTS ~ 1500 MPa, and 18% elongation.
图 2a 汇总了 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 和 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 高熵合金在三种不同状态下的室温拉伸行为。在固溶并水淬的状态（CRSA）下，Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金的屈服强度（YS）约 850 MPa，抗拉强度（UTS）约 1200 MPa，断裂延伸率 29%。在相同状态下，Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金强度略低（YS 约 780 MPa，UTS 约 1110 MPa），但塑性更佳（35%）。随后将固溶态 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金在 800 °C 等温退火 4 h（CRSA800），强度显著提升，YS 约 1200 MPa、UTS 约 1420 MPa，同时保持 25% 的塑性。在冷轧（90%）加直接退火（900 °C/4 h）状态（CR900）下，合金呈现 YS 约 1140 MPa、UTS 约 1500 MPa、延伸率 18%。
For context, the tensile curve of a solutionized CoFeNi alloy is also plotted in Fig. 2a. The comparison highlights the substantial strengthening imparted by Al and Ti additions. Relative to solutionized CoFeNi, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi HEA in the CRSA state exhibits nearly a threefold increase in YS and a twofold increase in UTS. Remarkably, this strengthening was achieved without significant loss of ductility, pointing to a modified deformation mechanism that mitigates the typical strength–ductility trade-off. Prior studies have shown that while CoFeNi deforms through homogeneous dislocation-mediated plasticity, the incorporation of Al and Ti in 3d transition-metal HEAs promotes planar slip, enabling sustained work hardening and enhanced ductility.
作为参照，图 2a 还绘出了固溶态 CoFeNi 合金的拉伸曲线。对比凸显了 Al、Ti 添加所带来的显著强化。相对于固溶态 CoFeNi，CRSA 态的 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 高熵合金 YS 提高近三倍、UTS 提高约两倍。值得注意的是，这种强化并未伴随明显的塑性损失，说明存在一种改变了的变形机制，缓解了典型的强度–塑性权衡。此前研究表明，CoFeNi 通过均匀的位错主导塑性变形，而在 3d 过渡金属高熵合金中加入 Al 和 Ti 会促进平面滑移，从而实现持续的加工硬化和增强的塑性。
The corresponding work-hardening responses for CoFeNi, Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78, and the three conditions of Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi (CRSA, CRSA800, CR900) are presented in Fig. 2b, further illustrating the role of compositional and processing modifications in tailoring the strength–ductility balance.
图 2b 给出了 CoFeNi、Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 以及 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 三种状态（CRSA、CRSA800、CR900）相应的加工硬化响应，进一步说明了成分与工艺调整在调控强度–塑性平衡中的作用。

磁性能与电阻率Magnetic properties and electrical resistivity

Figure 2c presents the room temperature magnetization (M) vs applied magnetic field (H) curves for the base CoFeNi alloy, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi HEA under three processing conditions (CRSA, CRSA800, CR900), and the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy in the CRSA condition. All samples display typical soft-ferromagnetic behavior. To highlight coercivity evolution, a magnified view of the curves near the origin (H = 0, M = 0) is shown as an inset. Ms and Hc values are extracted from the curves and summarized in Table 1.
图 2c 给出了基础 CoFeNi 合金、三种工艺状态（CRSA、CRSA800、CR900）的 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 高熵合金以及 CRSA 态 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金的室温磁化强度（M）随外加磁场（H）的变化曲线。所有样品都表现出典型的软铁磁行为。为凸显矫顽力的演化，插图放大显示了曲线在原点（H = 0，M = 0）附近的部分。Ms 和 Hc 值从曲线中提取并汇总于表 1。
Relative to CoFeNi, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi HEA exhibits a markedly reduced Ms of ~100 emu/g across all conditions, representing ~70% lower magnetization. While this value is significantly below conventional soft magnetic alloys like Fe–Si or Fe–Co (>180 emu/g), it is comparable to the values of Ni–Fe-based soft magnets (particularly when compared with commercial Ni-20 wt.% Fe permalloy (82 emu/g) and Ni-15 wt.% Fe-5wt.% Mo supermalloy (~70 emu/g)) and in line with other high-strength HEAs. Importantly, the newly developed Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy demonstrates a superior property balance, achieving Ms = 137.3 emu/g together with Hc = 1.8 Oe, thereby narrowing the gap toward conventional soft magnetic materials.
相对于 CoFeNi，Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 高熵合金在所有状态下的 Ms 均显著降低，约为 100 emu/g，磁化强度下降约 70%。虽然该值明显低于 Fe–Si、Fe–Co 等传统软磁合金（>180 emu/g），但与 Ni–Fe 基软磁体的值相当（尤其与商用 Ni-20 wt.% Fe 坡莫合金（82 emu/g）和 Ni-15 wt.% Fe-5 wt.% Mo 超坡莫合金（约 70 emu/g）相比），也与其他高强度高熵合金一致。重要的是，新开发的 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金展现出更优的性能平衡，Ms = 137.3 emu/g 且 Hc = 1.8 Oe，从而缩小了与传统软磁材料的差距。
The coercivity of Al0.3Ti5.7Co28.5Fe28.5Ni28.5 varies strongly with processing conditions. In the CRSA state, the alloy shows a promising Hc - 2.4 Oe, close to the 2.0 Oe of base CoFeNi. Upon annealing at 800 °C (CRSA800), Hc increases substantially to -48.8 Oe. The CR900 condition yields an intermediate Hc of -8.8 Oe. Thus, the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi offers better soft magnetic properties in the CRSA condition rather than in the CRSA800 condition.
Al0.3Ti5.7Co28.5Fe28.5Ni28.5 的矫顽力随工艺状态强烈变化。在 CRSA 态，合金呈现颇具前景的 Hc 约 2.4 Oe，接近基础 CoFeNi 的 2.0 Oe。在 800 °C 退火（CRSA800）后，Hc 大幅升高到约 48.8 Oe。CR900 状态给出居中的 Hc 约 8.8 Oe。因此，Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 在 CRSA 状态下的软磁性能优于 CRSA800 状态。
Figure 2d presents the corresponding electrical resistivity values. All alloy states— Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi (CRSA, CRSA800, CR900) and Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 (CRSA)—exhibit markedly higher resistivities compared to conventional soft magnetic alloys, a highly desirable feature for minimizing eddy current losses. Notably, alloying with Al and Ti increases resistivity by several fold relative to the base CoFeNi alloy (24.0 μΩ·cm). These properties are favorable compared to the recently developed Fe34.51Co30.32Ni30.23Ta4.94 alloy, which, despite exhibiting a lower coercivity of 4.52 Oe, possesses a significantly lower resistivity of 62.5 μΩ·cm. This combination of high resistivity and low-to-moderate coercivity provides a compelling pathway for developing HEA-based soft magnets that can simultaneously address eddy current suppression and the ability to experience higher stresses in service.
图 2d 给出了相应的电阻率值。所有合金状态——Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi（CRSA、CRSA800、CR900）和 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78（CRSA）——的电阻率都明显高于传统软磁合金，这是抑制涡流损耗非常理想的特性。值得注意的是，加入 Al 和 Ti 使电阻率相对基础 CoFeNi 合金（24.0 μΩ·cm）提高数倍。与近期开发的 Fe34.51Co30.32Ni30.23Ta4.94 合金相比，这些性能也颇具优势——后者虽矫顽力更低（4.52 Oe），但电阻率明显偏低（62.5 μΩ·cm）。这种高电阻率与中低矫顽力的组合，为开发既能抑制涡流、又能在服役中承受更高应力的高熵合金基软磁体提供了一条极具吸引力的路径。

组织表征：Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi —— 固溶淬火态Microstructure: Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi — solutionized & quenched

The nanoscale features of the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi in the CRSA condition were examined via TEM and APT. Figure 3a and b show selected area electron diffraction patterns (SAEDPs), recorded along the <001> and <112> FCC zone axes, respectively. In addition to the fundamental FCC reflections, extra spots were observed at {001} positions, providing clear evidence of L12-type ordering. A corresponding dark-field image (Fig. 3c) reveals a homogeneous distribution of -10 nm L12 precipitates embedded within the FCC matrix. High-resolution TEM (Fig. 3d) further confirms the atomic-scale structure of the FCC matrix and the ordered L12 phase.
采用 TEM 和 APT 考察了 CRSA 态 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 的纳米尺度特征。图 3a、b 分别给出了沿 <001> 和 <112> FCC 晶带轴记录的选区电子衍射花样（SAEDP）。除基本的 FCC 衍射斑外，还在 {001} 位置观察到额外斑点，为 L12 型有序化提供了清晰证据。相应的暗场像（图 3c）显示 FCC 基体中均匀弥散着约 10 nm 的 L12 析出相。高分辨 TEM（图 3d）进一步证实了 FCC 基体和有序 L12 相的原子尺度结构。
These observations establish that the alloy exhibits a two-phase FCC + L12 microstructure even after solution annealing and quenching. This is consistent with the thermodynamic predictions and suggests that L12 nanoprecipitates form during cooling, most likely driven by the large negative enthalpies of mixing of Al and Ti with Ni, Co, and Fe.
这些观察确立了该合金即使在固溶退火并淬火后仍呈现 FCC + L12 两相组织。这与热力学预测一致，表明 L12 纳米析出相在冷却过程中形成，最可能由 Al、Ti 与 Ni、Co、Fe 之间较大的负混合焓驱动。
Atom probe tomography (APT) analysis of the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy in the CRSA condition further confirmed the presence of nanoscale L12 precipitates within the FCC matrix. A Ni iso-concentration surface (green color surface in Fig. 3e) constructed at a 35 at.% threshold delineates the precipitates, revealing their nearly spherical morphology and uniform distribution within the Co–Fe–rich FCC matrix. For clarity, a 10 nm thick slice of the reconstructed volume is shown. The compositional partitioning across the FCC/L12 interface is highlighted in the proximity histogram profiles presented in Fig. 3f. The FCC matrix exhibits an average composition of 8.2Al–4.3Ti–30.4Co–33.8Fe–23.3Ni (at.%), whereas the L12 precipitates are enriched in Ni and Ti, with a representative composition of 9.6Al–13.9Ti–26.2Co–12Fe–38.3Ni (at.%). This strong Ni–Ti enrichment supports their identification as ordered L12 precipitates.
对 CRSA 态 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金的原子探针层析（APT）分析进一步证实了 FCC 基体内存在纳米级 L12 析出相。以 35 at.% 阈值构建的 Ni 等浓度面（图 3e 中绿色曲面）勾勒出析出相，显示其近球形形貌并均匀分布于富 Co–Fe 的 FCC 基体中。为清晰起见，图中展示了重构体积的一层 10 nm 厚切片。图 3f 的邻近直方图曲线突出显示了跨 FCC/L12 界面的成分分配：FCC 基体的平均成分为 8.2Al–4.3Ti–30.4Co–33.8Fe–23.3Ni（原子分数），而 L12 析出相富集 Ni 和 Ti，代表性成分为 9.6Al–13.9Ti–26.2Co–12Fe–38.3Ni（原子分数）。这种强烈的 Ni–Ti 富集支持将其判定为有序 L12 析出相。
Quantitative analysis yields an average spherical-equivalent precipitate diameter of 11.5 ± 2.2 nm and a number density of 1.24 × 10²³/m³. The size measurement, derived from the enclosed precipitate volume, may include minor uncertainties associated with iso-concentration threshold selection. Similarly, number density estimates were corrected by assigning half-weight to precipitates intersecting the dataset boundaries. Together with TEM, these APT observations establish that the solution-annealed and quenched Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy retains a finely dispersed FCC + L12 microstructure.
定量分析给出析出相的平均球等效直径为 11.5 ± 2.2 nm，数量密度为 1.24 × 10²³/m³。尺寸测量由所包围的析出相体积推算，可能含有与等浓度阈值选取相关的微小不确定度。类似地，对与数据集边界相交的析出相赋以半权重来校正数量密度估计。结合 TEM，这些 APT 观察确立了固溶退火并淬火的 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金保持着细弥散的 FCC + L12 组织。

Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi —— CRSA800 与 CR900 状态Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi — CRSA800 & CR900 conditions

A representative SEM backscattered electron (BSE) image of the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi CRSA800 sample is shown in Fig. 4a, while the corresponding EBSD phase and inverse pole figure (IPF) maps are presented in Figs. 4c and 4e. Based on EBSD analysis, the average FCC grain size of this sample was determined to be ~73 μm. A higher-magnification BSE image (Fig. 4b) reveals the presence of BCC/B2 precipitates, preferentially located at FCC grain boundaries and triple junctions. Further microstructural details are revealed in Fig. 4g, where a high-resolution SEM image from within the FCC grain clearly shows fine nanometer-scale precipitates. The presence of L12 ordering within the FCC matrix is confirmed by the selected area diffraction pattern in the inset in Fig. 4g, recorded along the [011] FCC zone axis, which exhibits distinct (100)-type superlattice reflections.
图 4a 给出了 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi CRSA800 样品的代表性 SEM 背散射电子（BSE）像，图 4c 和 4e 给出了相应的 EBSD 相图和反极图（IPF）。基于 EBSD 分析，该样品的平均 FCC 晶粒尺寸确定为约 73 μm。更高倍的 BSE 像（图 4b）显示存在优先位于 FCC 晶界和三叉点处的 BCC/B2 析出相。图 4g 揭示了更多组织细节——来自 FCC 晶粒内部的高分辨 SEM 像清晰显示了细小的纳米级析出相。图 4g 插图中沿 [011] FCC 晶带轴记录的选区衍射花样呈现出清晰的 (100) 型超晶格衍射斑，证实 FCC 基体内存在 L12 有序化。
APT results from the CRSA800 sample (Figs. 4h, i) show a Ni iso-concentration surface constructed at 28.5 at.% delineating the Ni-rich L12 precipitates within the Co–Fe–rich FCC matrix. Comparison with the CRSA state indicates that annealing at 800 °C for 4 h promoted significant growth and coarsening of ordered L12 precipitates. The average compositions of the FCC matrix and L12 precipitates were measured as 8.1Al–2.9Ti–31Co–38.9Fe–19.1Ni (at.%) and 10.1Al–14.5Ti–23.7Co–9.8Fe–41.9Ni (at.%), respectively. The average spherical-equivalent diameter and number density of the L12 precipitates were determined as 27 ± 5.1 nm and 5.76 × 10²²/m³. Interestingly, while the compositions of the L12 precipitates and the FCC matrix remain largely unchanged compared to the quenched condition, the precipitate size more than doubles, accompanied by a reduction in number density by nearly two orders of magnitude. This significant change in precipitate size and distribution is expected to strongly influence the mechanical response and Hc behavior of the HEA.
CRSA800 样品的 APT 结果（图 4h、i）显示，以 28.5 at.% 构建的 Ni 等浓度面勾勒出富 Co–Fe 的 FCC 基体内富 Ni 的 L12 析出相。与 CRSA 态相比表明，800 °C 退火 4 h 促进了有序 L12 析出相的显著长大和粗化。FCC 基体和 L12 析出相的平均成分分别测得为 8.1Al–2.9Ti–31Co–38.9Fe–19.1Ni（原子分数）和 10.1Al–14.5Ti–23.7Co–9.8Fe–41.9Ni（原子分数）。L12 析出相的平均球等效直径和数量密度确定为 27 ± 5.1 nm 和 5.76 × 10²²/m³。有趣的是，尽管与淬火态相比 L12 析出相和 FCC 基体的成分基本不变，析出相尺寸却增大一倍以上，同时数量密度下降近两个数量级。这种析出相尺寸与分布的显著变化预期将强烈影响高熵合金的力学响应和 Hc 行为。
To further tailor the microstructure, the CR900 route was applied: following homogenization at 1200 °C, the alloy was cold-rolled to 90% thickness reduction and directly annealed at 900 °C for 4 h. According to phase stability calculations, this composition is predicted to form a stable three-phase microstructure (FCC, B2, L12). However, as established for FCC-based HEAs, B2 nucleation is hindered by its high nucleation barrier, whereas ordered L12 nucleates more readily due to the low FCC/L12 interfacial energy. Consequently, B2 precipitates form only heterogeneously at defects such as grain boundaries and triple junctions, unlike the more uniformly distributed L12 precipitates. A low-magnification SEM image (Fig. 5a) with EBSD phase and IPF maps (Fig. 5b, c) reveal a refined FCC grain size of ~5 μm—a substantial reduction from the ~73 μm of the homogenized+quenched condition. A secondary population of BCC/B2 precipitates (~1 μm, area fraction ~5%) preferentially decorates FCC grain boundaries and triple junctions.
为进一步调控组织，采用 CR900 路线：在 1200 °C 均匀化后，将合金冷轧至 90% 减薄，并直接在 900 °C 退火 4 h。根据相稳定性计算，预测该成分形成稳定的三相组织（FCC、B2、L12）。然而，正如 FCC 基高熵合金所确立的，B2 因形核势垒高而形核受阻，而有序 L12 因 FCC/L12 界面能低而更易形核。因此，与分布较均匀的 L12 析出相不同，B2 析出相只在晶界、三叉点等缺陷处非均匀形核。低倍 SEM 像（图 5a）配合 EBSD 相图和 IPF 图（图 5b、c）显示 FCC 晶粒细化至约 5 μm——相较均匀化+淬火态的约 73 μm 大幅减小。次生的 BCC/B2 析出相（约 1 μm，面积分数约 5%）优先装点于 FCC 晶界和三叉点处。
Higher-magnification SEM-EBSD (Fig. 5d–f) confirmed that these boundary-associated precipitates were indexed as BCC, consistent with B2 ordering, while a finer dispersion of intragranular precipitates indexed as FCC but are likely L12-ordered (EBSD cannot differentiate ordered and disordered FCC). TEM (Fig. 6) verified B2 ordering via (010) superlattice reflections in the [001] BCC diffraction pattern, with EDS maps showing strong Al partitioning to the B2 phase, Ti enrichment, and Fe depletion. A [112] FCC-zone SAED pattern (Fig. 7a) displayed (110)-type superlattice reflections indicative of L12 ordering; APT (Fig. 7b) identified Ni-rich L12 precipitates (number density 3.4 × 10²³/m). Proximity histograms gave an FCC matrix of ~7.1Al–3.7Ti–31.6Co–37.4Fe–20.1Ni (at.%) and L12 precipitates of ~8.7Al–15.7Ti–24.6Co–9.9Fe–41.1Ni (at.%).
更高倍的 SEM-EBSD（图 5d–f）证实，这些与晶界相关的析出相被标定为 BCC，与 B2 有序一致；而更细弥散的晶内析出相虽标定为 FCC，但很可能是 L12 有序相（EBSD 无法区分有序与无序 FCC）。TEM（图 6）通过 [001] BCC 衍射花样中的 (010) 超晶格衍射斑证实了 B2 有序，EDS 图显示 Al 强烈分配到 B2 相、Ti 富集、Fe 贫化。沿 [112] FCC 晶带轴的 SAED 花样（图 7a）呈现 (110) 型超晶格衍射斑，指示 L12 有序；APT（图 7b）识别出富 Ni 的 L12 析出相（数量密度 3.4 × 10²³/m）。邻近直方图给出 FCC 基体约为 7.1Al–3.7Ti–31.6Co–37.4Fe–20.1Ni（原子分数），L12 析出相约为 8.7Al–15.7Ti–24.6Co–9.9Fe–41.1Ni（原子分数）。
These results establish that direct annealing at 900 °C after 90% cold rolling produces a refined FCC matrix with ~5 μm grains, decorated by B2 precipitates at boundaries and a uniform dispersion of Ni- and Ti-rich L12 precipitates within grains. The dual presence of boundary-associated B2 and intragranular L12 phases highlights the distinct nucleation pathways of the two ordered structures: heterogeneous for B2 and homogeneous for L12. This microstructural configuration is expected to significantly influence the mechanical response of the alloy by combining grain refinement with multi-scale precipitation strengthening.
这些结果确立了：在 90% 冷轧后于 900 °C 直接退火，可得到晶粒约 5 μm 的细化 FCC 基体，其晶界处装点 B2 析出相，晶内则均匀弥散富 Ni、Ti 的 L12 析出相。晶界相关的 B2 相与晶内 L12 相并存，凸显了两种有序结构不同的形核途径：B2 为非均匀形核，L12 为均匀形核。这种组织构型通过将晶粒细化与多尺度析出强化相结合，预期将显著影响合金的力学响应。

Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy

Figure 8 presents the microstructural characterization of the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy in its solution-annealed condition. EBSD analysis (Fig. 8a) revealed an equiaxed FCC grain structure with an average grain size of 100 ± 25.6 μm. High-resolution SEM imaging in the backscattered mode (Fig. 8b) showed the presence of nanoscale precipitates, which were further confirmed by dark-field TEM (Fig. 8c). SAEDPs recorded along the <011> BCC zone axis (Fig. 8d) displayed additional {001} and {011} reflections, unambiguously establishing the presence of L12 ordering within the FCC matrix. Despite undergoing solution annealing and subsequent quenching, the alloy retained a dual-phase FCC + L12 microstructure, indicating that L12 precipitates homogeneously nucleated during cooling, facilitated by the large negative enthalpy of mixing between Ti and Ni/Co/Fe.
图 8 给出了固溶退火态 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金的组织表征。EBSD 分析（图 8a）显示为等轴 FCC 晶粒组织，平均晶粒尺寸 100 ± 25.6 μm。背散射模式的高分辨 SEM 成像（图 8b）显示存在纳米级析出相，暗场 TEM（图 8c）进一步证实。沿 <011> BCC 晶带轴记录的 SAEDP（图 8d）呈现额外的 {001} 和 {011} 衍射斑，明确确立了 FCC 基体内存在 L12 有序。尽管经历了固溶退火及随后的淬火，该合金仍保持 FCC + L12 双相组织，表明 L12 析出相在冷却过程中均匀形核，这由 Ti 与 Ni/Co/Fe 之间较大的负混合焓所促成。
Compositional analysis across the FCC/L12 interface (Fig. 8f) revealed distinct partitioning: the FCC matrix was 2.5Ti–34.0Co–44.4Fe–19.0Ni (at.%), whereas the L12 precipitates were significantly enriched in Ti and Ni, with an average composition of 23.6Ti–27.6Co–7.3Fe–41.5Ni (at.%). Quantitative analysis showed an average spherical-equivalent diameter of 4.0 ± 0.8 nm and a number density of 6.29 × 10²³/m³. These results confirm that, even under solution-annealed and quenched conditions, the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy exhibits a stable two-phase FCC + L12 microstructure, driven by strong chemical partitioning and ordering tendencies.
跨 FCC/L12 界面的成分分析（图 8f）显示出明显的元素分配：FCC 基体为 2.5Ti–34.0Co–44.4Fe–19.0Ni（原子分数），而 L12 析出相显著富集 Ti 和 Ni，平均成分为 23.6Ti–27.6Co–7.3Fe–41.5Ni（原子分数）。定量分析给出平均球等效直径 4.0 ± 0.8 nm、数量密度 6.29 × 10²³/m³。这些结果证实，即使在固溶退火并淬火状态下，Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金也呈现稳定的 FCC + L12 两相组织，这由强烈的化学分配和有序化倾向所驱动。

讨论Discussion

The present study demonstrates a novel strategy for the design of high-strength, soft magnetic, and high resistivity HEAs by carefully tuning composition and processing pathways to control nanoscale precipitation behavior. Specifically, Ti and Al additions to the equiatomic CoFeNi alloy promote the formation of ordered L12 precipitates within an FCC matrix, thereby achieving a remarkable balance between strength, ductility, electrical resistivity, and soft magnetic properties.
本研究展示了一种设计高强度、软磁、高电阻率高熵合金的新策略——通过精心调控成分与工艺路径来控制纳米尺度析出行为。具体而言，向等原子比 CoFeNi 合金中加入 Ti 和 Al，可促进 FCC 基体内有序 L12 析出相的形成，从而在强度、塑性、电阻率和软磁性能之间取得显著平衡。
Two distinct HEA compositions were designed—Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi and Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78—to exploit the effectiveness of ordered L12 precipitates in enhancing mechanical properties while maintaining soft magnetic behavior. The formation of ordered L12 precipitates in the random solid-solution FCC parent matrix occurs via a nucleation and growth process. At the earliest transformation, nanometer-scale ordered L12 domains nucleate within the parent FCC matrix. These domains are likely fully coherent with the FCC matrix and thus show no substantial lattice-parameter difference; they are early stages of the second phase with a distinctly different composition and chemical ordering. This corresponds to the CRSA condition for both HEAs and is manifested as superlattice reflections in electron diffraction (Figs. 3a, 8d) and compositional differences in APT (Figs. 3e, f, 8e, f). However, XRD patterns under CRSA show no discernible splitting of the FCC peaks, because the lattice parameters of FCC and L12 are not substantially different at this early stage. Upon further annealing (CRSA800, CR900 of Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi), the ordered L12 precipitates grow and coarsen, causing lattice-parameter differences and eventual loss of coherency, so splits appear in the XRD peaks.
本研究设计了两种不同的高熵合金成分——Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 和 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78——以利用有序 L12 析出相在提升力学性能同时保持软磁行为方面的有效性。有序 L12 析出相在随机固溶体 FCC 母相基体中的形成，通过形核与长大过程进行。在最早期的转变中，纳米级有序 L12 畴在母相 FCC 基体内形核。这些畴很可能与 FCC 基体完全共格，因而不表现出明显的晶格常数差异；它们是第二相的早期阶段，具有与母相明显不同的成分和化学有序。这对应于两种高熵合金的 CRSA 状态，表现为电子衍射中的超晶格衍射斑（图 3a、8d）和 APT 中的成分差异（图 3e、f、8e、f）。然而，CRSA 状态下的 XRD 图谱未见 FCC 峰的明显劈裂，因为在此早期阶段 FCC 与 L12 的晶格常数差异不大。进一步退火后（Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 的 CRSA800、CR900），有序 L12 析出相长大并粗化，导致晶格常数出现差异并最终丧失共格，因而 XRD 峰出现劈裂。

析出相主导的强化Precipitation-mediated strengthening

The precipitation of nanoscale L12 phases is central to the superior mechanical and physical performance of the investigated alloys. In the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy, the formation of ultrafine (~4.0 nm), uniformly distributed L12 precipitates at extremely high number densities (6.29 × 10²³/m³) provides significant strengthening by interacting with dislocations during plastic deformation. Despite such dense precipitation, the small precipitate size ensures minimal pinning of magnetic domain walls, resulting in very low coercivity (~1.8 Oe). The concurrent enhancement in yield strength (YS - 850 MPa), UTS (~1200 MPa), ductility (~29%), and resistivity (308.3 μΩ·cm), while retaining a high saturation magnetization (137.3 emu/g), positions this alloy among the best-performing soft magnetic alloys reported to date. By contrast, in the Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy, larger L12 precipitates (~11.5 nm) form at lower number densities (1.24 × 10²³/m³). While the mechanical response remains impressive (YS - 780 MPa, UTS - 1110 MPa, elongation ~35%), the saturation magnetization decreases substantially (~96.5 emu/g) due to compositional partitioning, and the coercivity increases modestly (~2.4 Oe).
纳米级 L12 相的析出是所研究合金优异力学与物理性能的核心。在 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金中，形成超细（约 4.0 nm）、均匀分布、数量密度极高（6.29 × 10²³/m³）的 L12 析出相，通过在塑性变形中与位错相互作用提供显著强化。尽管析出如此密集，但小的析出相尺寸确保对磁畴壁的钉扎极小，从而带来很低的矫顽力（约 1.8 Oe）。在保持高饱和磁化强度（137.3 emu/g）的同时，屈服强度（YS 约 850 MPa）、UTS（约 1200 MPa）、塑性（约 29%）和电阻率（308.3 μΩ·cm）同步提升，使该合金跻身迄今报道的性能最佳软磁合金之列。相比之下，Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金中形成的 L12 析出相更大（约 11.5 nm）、数量密度更低（1.24 × 10²³/m³）。虽然其力学响应依旧出色（YS 约 780 MPa，UTS 约 1110 MPa，延伸率约 35%），但由于成分分配，饱和磁化强度明显下降（约 96.5 emu/g），矫顽力小幅升高（约 2.4 Oe）。
The L12 structure is an ordered FCC superlattice, analogous to the γ′ Ni3(Al, Ti) phase in Ni-based superalloys. The low lattice misfit between coherent γ′ precipitates and the disordered FCC matrix minimizes interfacial energy, yielding a low nucleation barrier and homogeneous nucleation, as observed here. γ′ precipitates strengthen the alloy by impeding dislocation motion via two mechanisms: (i) dislocation shearing—smaller precipitates (<50 nm) are sheared, impeded by anti-phase boundary formation which requires energy; (ii) Orowan bowing—for larger precipitates (>50 nm), dislocations bend and bypass them. Using Orowan's simplified model, YS ∝ √(Nv·D), where Nv is the number density and D the diameter: Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 has a higher number density and smaller diameter than Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi in the CRSA condition, which leads to its higher YS.
L12 结构是一种有序的 FCC 超晶格，类似于镍基高温合金中的 γ′ Ni3(Al, Ti) 相。共格 γ′ 析出相与无序 FCC 基体之间的低晶格错配使界面能最小化，从而形核势垒低、可均匀形核，正如本文所观察到的。γ′ 析出相通过两种机制阻碍位错运动来强化合金：(i) 位错剪切——较小的析出相（<50 nm）被剪切，而剪切过程中形成反相畴界需要能量，从而受阻；(ii) Orowan 弓弯——对较大的析出相（>50 nm），位错弯曲并绕过它们。用 Orowan 简化模型，YS ∝ √(Nv·D)，其中 Nv 为数量密度、D 为直径：在 CRSA 状态下，Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 的 L12 析出相数量密度更高、直径更小（相对 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi），因而屈服强度更高。

组织演化及其对磁、电性能的影响Microstructural evolution and its impact on magnetic/electrical properties

Annealing treatments further reveal how precipitate coarsening alters property balance. Growth of L12 precipitates to ~27 nm in the CRSA800 condition of Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi dramatically increases strength (YS ~ 1200 MPa) but also raises coercivity nearly 20-fold (~48.6 Oe) due to domain wall pinning. This illustrates the trade-off between mechanical properties and magnetic softness: coarser precipitates act as effective barriers to dislocation motion but simultaneously increase magnetostatic energy and coercivity, impede domain wall mobility, and compromise soft magnetic performance.
退火处理进一步揭示了析出相粗化如何改变性能平衡。在 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 的 CRSA800 状态下，L12 析出相长大到约 27 nm，使强度大幅提高（YS 约 1200 MPa），但也因畴壁钉扎使矫顽力升高近 20 倍（约 48.6 Oe）。这体现了力学性能与磁软性之间的权衡：更粗的析出相能有效阻碍位错运动，但同时会增大静磁能和矫顽力、阻碍畴壁移动，损害软磁性能。
Thermo-Calc predictions and experiments provide additional insight. At higher annealing temperatures, the alloy stabilizes a minor fraction of B2 precipitates, nucleating heterogeneously at grain boundaries and triple junctions. These coarse precipitates alter solute partitioning and reduce supersaturation within FCC grains, leading to fewer intragranular L12 precipitates. This is evident in CR900 of Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi, where direct annealing after cold rolling produces a three-phase microstructure: fine FCC grains (~5 μm), ~5% B2 precipitates (~1 μm), and a small fraction of coarse L12 precipitates (~30–50 nm). This yields a superior combination: high YS (~1140 MPa), UTS (~1500 MPa), low coercivity (~8.8 Oe), and moderate Ms (~107.6 emu/g). Although strength is comparable to CRSA800, the coercivity is nearly six times lower, underscoring the critical role of microstructural pathway engineering. The enhanced resistivity of these alloys arises from high inelastic electron scattering in the distorted lattice due to nanoscale precipitates; the CRSA Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy shows slightly higher resistivity than CRSA Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi owing to its higher L12 number density (more scattering sites).
Thermo-Calc 预测与实验提供了进一步的洞见。在较高退火温度下，合金稳定出少量 B2 析出相，在晶界和三叉点处非均匀形核。这些粗大析出相改变溶质分配、降低 FCC 晶内的过饱和度，从而使晶内 L12 析出相减少。这在 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 的 CR900 中很明显——冷轧后直接退火产生三相组织：细 FCC 晶粒（约 5 μm）、约 5% 的 B2 析出相（约 1 μm）和少量粗大 L12 析出相（约 30–50 nm）。由此获得优异组合：高 YS（约 1140 MPa）、UTS（约 1500 MPa）、低矫顽力（约 8.8 Oe）和中等 Ms（约 107.6 emu/g）。尽管强度与 CRSA800 相当，矫顽力却低近六倍，凸显了组织路径工程的关键作用。这些合金电阻率的提升源于纳米析出相造成的畸变晶格中较强的非弹性电子散射；CRSA 态 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 的电阻率略高于 CRSA 态 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi，是因为前者 L12 数量密度更高（散射位点更多）。

强化机制与磁畴相互作用Mechanistic insights & magnetic domain interactions

The contrasting strengthening mechanisms in CRSA800 and CR900 show how different microstructural features govern deformation resistance. In CRSA800, strengthening arises from a dense population of coherent, shearable L12 precipitates (~27 nm), akin to γ′ in Ni/Co-based superalloys—cutting coherent ordered precipitates contributes to both strength and ductility. In CR900, strengthening is dominated by fine FCC grain size (~5 μm, Hall–Petch), with secondary contributions from coarse B2 boundary precipitates and a small fraction of intragranular L12. Both achieve high strength, but their differing impacts on coercivity reveal distinct trade-offs: precipitate shearing versus grain-boundary strengthening carry different penalties for soft magnetic properties.
CRSA800 与 CR900 中截然不同的强化机制，展示了不同组织特征如何主导变形抗力。在 CRSA800 中，强化来自密集的共格、可剪切 L12 析出相（约 27 nm），类似镍/钴基高温合金中的 γ′——切过共格有序析出相同时贡献强度和塑性。在 CR900 中，强化以细小 FCC 晶粒（约 5 μm，Hall–Petch）为主导，粗大 B2 晶界析出相和少量晶内 L12 提供次要贡献。两者都能达到高强度，但它们对矫顽力的不同影响揭示了不同的权衡：析出相剪切与晶界强化对软磁性能带来的"代价"各不相同。
The results also clarify how nanoscale precipitates influence magnetic softness. In CRSA Al0.3CoFeNi, L12 precipitates average ~11.5 nm, far smaller than the ~171 nm domain wall width reported for such FCC–L12 microstructures (Kovács et al.), so domain wall–precipitate interactions are minimized and coercivity stays low. The same argument explains the exceptionally low coercivity (1.8 Oe) of CRSA Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78, whose L12 precipitates are even finer (~4.0 nm). In contrast, coarsening to ~27 nm (CRSA800) produces stronger magnetostatic interactions and coherency stresses, pinning domain walls and drastically raising coercivity—more severe still because the domain wall width narrows (~35 nm) in FCC + L12 + BCC/B2 microstructures. CR900 is intermediate: grain boundaries contribute some pinning, but the absence of a high density of coarsened intragranular L12 prevents a severe coercivity increase.
这些结果还阐明了纳米析出相如何影响磁软性。在 CRSA 态 Al0.3CoFeNi 中，L12 析出相平均约 11.5 nm，远小于此类 FCC–L12 组织中报道的约 171 nm 畴壁宽度（Kovács 等），因此畴壁–析出相相互作用被最小化，矫顽力保持很低。同样的论据可解释 CRSA 态 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 极低的矫顽力（1.8 Oe），其 L12 析出相更为细小（约 4.0 nm）。相反，粗化至约 27 nm（CRSA800）会产生更强的静磁相互作用和共格应力，钉扎畴壁并大幅提高矫顽力——由于 FCC + L12 + BCC/B2 组织中畴壁宽度变窄（约 35 nm），这一效应更为严重。CR900 居于两者之间：晶界贡献一定钉扎，但由于不存在高密度的粗化晶内 L12，矫顽力未出现严重增大。

与其他合金对比、应用与可持续性Comparison, applications & sustainability

Benchmarking against commercial and literature soft magnetic alloys reveals the unique positioning of these Ta-free HEAs. Conventional alloys (Si steel, Ni–Fe permalloy, Ni–Fe–Mo supermalloy, Fe–Co–V Hiperco) exhibit low coercivity and high Ms but suffer from low strength and poor resistivity; e.g., Hiperco 50HS achieves YS ~415–624 MPa with ~10% ductility, but its resistivity (27–80 μΩ·cm) and coercivity (~1.8–8.0 Oe) are inadequate for emerging high-frequency, high-speed applications. Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 offers YS ~40% higher plus fourfold higher resistivity while keeping coercivity well below Hiperco. Recent Ta-containing CoFeNi HEAs reached YS up to 1930 MPa with low coercivity (0.97–10.7 Oe) and moderate Ms (~100–115 emu/g), but their resistivity (~106 μΩ·cm) and density are lower than desired, and Ta raises cost and limits scalability. In contrast, CRSA Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 combines a high specific YS (~105.5 kN·m/kg), low Hc (1.8 Oe), high Ms (137.3 emu/g), and exceptional resistivity (308.3 μΩ·cm); CR900 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi delivers even higher strength (YS ~1140 MPa, UTS ~1500 MPa) with low coercivity (8.8 Oe) and good ductility (18%).
与商用及文献报道的软磁合金进行基准对比，揭示了这些无钽高熵合金的独特定位。传统合金（硅钢、Ni–Fe 坡莫合金、Ni–Fe–Mo 超坡莫合金、Fe–Co–V Hiperco）具有低矫顽力和高 Ms，但强度低、电阻率差；例如 Hiperco 50HS 实现 YS 约 415–624 MPa、塑性约 10%，但其电阻率（27–80 μΩ·cm）和矫顽力（约 1.8–8.0 Oe）不足以应对新兴的高频、高速应用。Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 的 YS 高约 40%，电阻率高四倍，同时矫顽力远低于 Hiperco。近期含 Ta 的 CoFeNi 高熵合金 YS 高达 1930 MPa，矫顽力低（0.97–10.7 Oe）、Ms 中等（约 100–115 emu/g），但电阻率（约 106 μΩ·cm）和密度偏低，且 Ta 抬高成本、限制可规模化。相比之下，CRSA 态 Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 兼具高比屈服强度（约 105.5 kN·m/kg）、低 Hc（1.8 Oe）、高 Ms（137.3 emu/g）和出众的电阻率（308.3 μΩ·cm）；CR900 态 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 则提供更高强度（YS 约 1140 MPa，UTS 约 1500 MPa），且矫顽力低（8.8 Oe）、塑性良好（18%）。
These properties align with requirements for next-generation high-speed machines, flywheel energy storage, and advanced electromechanical devices. In high-speed motors, higher rotational velocity raises centrifugal stresses that demand higher yield strength in rotor laminations; Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78's YS ~850 MPa allows stable operation at higher rpm and power densities. High resistivity (>300 μΩ·cm) inhibits eddy-current losses—nearly an order-of-magnitude improvement over conventional alloys—reducing heat and improving efficiency; combined with low Hc and high Ms, hysteresis is minimized and induction improved. Ductility of 18–35% enables forming into complex shapes and damage tolerance, rarely met simultaneously with high strength and soft magnetic behavior. On sustainability: eliminating expensive, geopolitically constrained Ta reduces cost, supply risk, and embodied energy, while ~10% lower density reduces component mass. Although more expensive than conventional Fe–Si SMMs, the better strength, ductility, and resistivity are expected to extend service life and offset the higher processing cost.
这些性能契合下一代高速电机、飞轮储能和先进机电装置的要求。在高速电机中，更高的转速会增大离心应力，要求转子叠片具有更高的屈服强度；Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 约 850 MPa 的 YS 使其能在更高转速和功率密度下稳定运行。高电阻率（>300 μΩ·cm）抑制涡流损耗——相比传统合金提升近一个数量级——减少发热、提高效率；结合低 Hc 和高 Ms，磁滞被最小化、磁感应得到改善。18–35% 的塑性使其能成形为复杂形状并具有损伤容限，这在高强度与软磁行为同时具备的情况下十分罕见。在可持续性方面：去除昂贵且受地缘政治制约的 Ta，可降低成本、供应风险和隐含能耗，而约 10% 更低的密度可减轻部件质量。尽管比传统 Fe–Si 软磁材料更昂贵，但更好的强度、塑性和电阻率预期能延长服役寿命，抵消更高的加工成本。

两种合金的性能小结Summary of the two alloys

This study establishes a robust design pathway for high-performance soft magnetic HEAs through precipitation-mediated strengthening while retaining low Hc, appreciable Ms, and high resistivity in Ta-free CoFeNi-based systems. By judiciously adding Ti and Al, nanoscale ordering is harnessed to introduce a dense population of ultrafine L12 precipitates; if their length scale and distribution are appropriately controlled, mechanical strength and electrical resistivity are simultaneously enhanced while soft magnetic behavior is preserved. Two CoFeNi-based HEAs were designed: Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 and quinary Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi.
本研究确立了一条稳健的高性能软磁高熵合金设计路径——在无钽 CoFeNi 基体系中，通过析出相主导的强化，同时保持低 Hc、可观的 Ms 和高电阻率。通过合理添加 Ti 和 Al，利用纳米尺度有序化引入密集的超细 L12 析出相；若适当控制其尺度和分布，可在保持软磁行为的同时同步提升力学强度和电阻率。据此设计了两种 CoFeNi 基高熵合金：Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 和五元 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi。
The Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 alloy demonstrates a rare combination of low coercivity (~1.8 Oe), high saturation magnetization (~137.3 emu/g), and ultrahigh electrical resistivity (308.3 μΩ·cm), coupled with a yield strength of ~850 MPa, UTS of ~1200 MPa, and tensile elongation of ~29%. While state-of-the-art Ta-containing HEAs may exhibit marginally lower coercivity, they generally show lower Ms (~100 emu/g) and much lower resistivity (62–117 μΩ·cm). Therefore, the Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 composition offers clear advantages for reducing eddy current losses and meeting the magnetic requirements of future electrical machines.
Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 合金展现出罕见的性能组合：低矫顽力（约 1.8 Oe）、高饱和磁化强度（约 137.3 emu/g）和超高电阻率（308.3 μΩ·cm），并兼具约 850 MPa 的屈服强度、约 1200 MPa 的 UTS 和约 29% 的拉伸延伸率。虽然最先进的含 Ta 高熵合金可能矫顽力略低，但它们通常 Ms 更低（约 100 emu/g）、电阻率也低得多（62–117 μΩ·cm）。因此，Ti0.24CoFeNi0.78 成分在降低涡流损耗、满足未来电机磁性要求方面具有明显优势。
The quinary Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi alloy exhibits a marginally higher coercivity (~2.4 Oe) and Ms (96.5 emu/g) but provides more tunability. In the CRSA condition it combines high resistivity (~269.5 μΩ·cm) with very good mechanical properties (YS ~780 MPa, UTS ~1100 MPa, elongation ~30%). Controlled thermo-mechanical processing allows further optimization: precipitate coarsening at 800 °C boosts YS to ~1200 MPa and UTS to ~1420 MPa but substantially raises coercivity (~48.6 Oe); in contrast, direct annealing of the cold-rolled condition at 900 °C produces a refined three-phase microstructure with very high YS (~1140 MPa), UTS (~1500 MPa), elongation ~18%, high resistivity (296.1 μΩ·cm), and lower coercivity (~8.8 Oe). Therefore, these newly developed CoFeNi-based HEAs offer a competing alternative to Ta-containing HEAs and surpass conventional Fe-based soft magnetic alloys, especially in mechanical properties and electrical resistivity—providing a lightweight, cost-effective, and scalable alternative for next-generation high-speed electrical machines, flywheel energy storage, and other demanding applications.
五元 Al0.3Ti0.2CoFeNi 合金的矫顽力（约 2.4 Oe）和 Ms（96.5 emu/g）略高，但提供了更大的可调性。在 CRSA 状态下，它兼具高电阻率（约 269.5 μΩ·cm）与很好的力学性能（YS 约 780 MPa，UTS 约 1100 MPa，延伸率约 30%）。受控的热机械加工可进一步优化：800 °C 下析出相粗化使 YS 提升到约 1200 MPa、UTS 提升到约 1420 MPa，但矫顽力大幅升高（约 48.6 Oe）；相反，将冷轧态在 900 °C 直接退火可得到细化的三相组织，兼具很高的 YS（约 1140 MPa）、UTS（约 1500 MPa）、约 18% 的延伸率、高电阻率（296.1 μΩ·cm）和较低的矫顽力（约 8.8 Oe）。因此，这些新开发的 CoFeNi 基高熵合金为含 Ta 高熵合金提供了有力的替代方案，并在力学性能和电阻率方面超越传统铁基软磁合金——为下一代高速电机、飞轮储能及其他苛刻应用提供了一种轻质、经济、可规模化的替代材料。

Acta Materialia · 2023

异相界面对磁性 Al0.3CoFeNi 复杂浓缩合金局部矫顽力机制的作用Role of heterophase interfaces on local coercivity mechanisms in the magnetic Al0.3CoFeNi complex concentrated alloy

Acta Materialia 246 (2023) 118672 · doi:10.1016/j.actamat.2023.118672 · András Kovács, Nithin B. Venkataraman, Varun Chaudhary, Sriswaroop Dasari, Thibaud Denneulin, R.V. Ramanujan, Rajarshi Banerjee, Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski
本页为「摘要 + 正文」中文翻译（含引言、实验、结果、讨论、结论各节）；已省略参考文献与致谢。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Microstructural features across different length scales have a profound influence on the coercivity of magnetic alloys. Whereas the role of homophase boundaries on the pinning of magnetic domain walls is well established, the influence of heterophase interfaces on domain wall motion is complex and poorly understood. Here, we use state-of-the-art electron microscopy techniques to show that the magnetization reversal process in an Al0.3CoFeNi magnetic complex concentrated alloy (CCA), which is responsible for its coercivity, changes dramatically from a nucleation-type mechanism in the FCC+L12 state of the CCA, with a domain wall width of 171 nm, to a pinning type mechanism in the microstructure with colonies of FCC/L12 nanorods embedded in a BCC/B2 matrix, with a domain wall width of 35 nm. Our work reveals that heterophase FCC/BCC interfaces have a much stronger effect on coercivity than isostructural chemically ordered/disordered interfaces and provides a powerful guide to the rational design of microstructure to tune magnetic properties in both complex concentrated alloys and conventional magnetic alloys.
跨越不同尺度的组织特征对磁性合金的矫顽力有深刻影响。同相界（homophase boundary）对磁畴壁钉扎的作用已被充分认识，但异相界面（heterophase interface）对畴壁运动的影响却复杂而认识不足。本文采用最先进的电子显微技术表明：决定矫顽力的磁化反转过程在 Al0.3CoFeNi 磁性复杂浓缩合金（CCA）中发生了显著转变——从该 CCA 的 FCC+L12 状态下的形核型（nucleation-type）机制（畴壁宽度 171 nm），转变为 FCC/L12 纳米棒集束嵌于 BCC/B2 基体的组织中的钉扎型（pinning-type）机制（畴壁宽度 35 nm）。我们的工作揭示，异相 FCC/BCC 界面对矫顽力的作用远强于同结构的化学有序/无序界面，为通过合理设计组织来调控复杂浓缩合金与传统磁性合金的磁性能提供了有力指导。

1. 引言1. Introduction

Complex concentrated alloys (CCAs) or multi-principal-element alloys (MPEAs) or high entropy alloys (HEAs) are a novel class of materials that offer superior functional and structural performance in many applications. Their properties can be tuned over a very wide range as a result of the vast number of possible combinations of elements and processing strategies. Whereas most previous studies of CCAs have focused on their structural properties, much less effort has been paid to understanding their local functional properties.
复杂浓缩合金（CCA）、多主元合金（MPEA）或高熵合金（HEA）是一类新型材料，在许多应用中提供优异的功能与结构性能。由于元素组合与工艺策略数量极为庞大，其性能可在很宽范围内调控。此前对 CCA 的研究大多聚焦于结构性能，而对其局部功能性能的理解则投入不足。
Soft magnetic materials are key components in industrial applications that include electrical power generation, electrical machines, magnetic shielding and electromagnets. High frequency electrical motors require materials with high mechanical strength and superior magnetic properties. However, the low strength of conventional soft magnetic materials limits their use in such applications, particularly in high rotational frequency devices.
软磁材料是发电、电机、磁屏蔽和电磁铁等工业应用中的关键部件。高频电机需要兼具高力学强度和优异磁性能的材料。然而，传统软磁材料强度较低，限制了它们在此类应用中的使用，尤其是在高转速器件中。
Recent research has focused on identifying novel high-strength soft magnets in the HEA domain. Most magnetic CCAs contain Fe, Co and Ni, in combination with other alloying elements. Although some FeCoNi-X (X = Al, Si, Cu, AlSi, AlCu, AlMn, etc.) CCAs have promising combinations of magnetic and mechanical properties, their magnetic softness generally decreases with increasing mechanical strength, with their coercivity Hc depending sensitively on their composition and microstructure over various length scales. For example, the strength of the FeCoNi(CuAl)0.8Gax (0 < x < 0.08) HEA improved from 601 MPa for x = 0 to 1176 MPa for x = 0.08. However, its coercivity increased from 362 to 685.8 A/m. Another important property is saturation magnetization Ms, which increases with diamagnetic Ga content in FeCoNi(CuAl)0.8Gax HEAs. Such changes in magnetic and mechanical properties result from the heterogeneous microstructure of these alloys and are not yet understood experimentally.
近期研究着眼于在高熵合金领域寻找新型高强度软磁体。大多数磁性 CCA 含 Fe、Co、Ni，并配以其他合金元素。尽管一些 FeCoNi-X（X = Al、Si、Cu、AlSi、AlCu、AlMn 等）CCA 具有颇具前景的磁-力学性能组合，但它们的磁软性通常随力学强度提高而下降，其矫顽力 Hc 对成分及跨多个尺度的组织高度敏感。例如，FeCoNi(CuAl)0.8Gax（0 < x < 0.08）高熵合金的强度从 x = 0 时的 601 MPa 提高到 x = 0.08 时的 1176 MPa，但其矫顽力也从 362 增大到 685.8 A/m。另一重要性能是饱和磁化强度 Ms，它在 FeCoNi(CuAl)0.8Gax 高熵合金中随抗磁性 Ga 含量增加而升高。这些磁性与力学性能的变化源自这些合金的非均匀组织，实验上尚未得到理解。
Here, we study a model Al0.3CoFeNi CCA, whose magnetic and mechanical properties can be tuned by varying the processing conditions. The first sample was obtained by solutionizing the alloy at 1250 °C (cold rolling solution annealing or CRSA) to form an FCC+L12 microstructure. The second sample was obtained by annealing the solutionized alloy at 600 °C for 50 h to form a hierarchical microstructure (CRSA600). The magnetic and mechanical properties of the two alloys are given in Table 1. The large difference in properties (increase in Hc by a factor of >60 and increase in yield strength and hardness by a factor of >2) obtained by changing the heat treatment makes this alloy a model material to understand the influence of local variations in composition and microstructure on coercivity using quantitative in situ magnetic imaging techniques in the transmission electron microscope (TEM).
本文研究一种模型 Al0.3CoFeNi 复杂浓缩合金，其磁性与力学性能可通过改变工艺条件来调控。第一个样品通过在 1250 °C 固溶（冷轧固溶退火，CRSA）获得，形成 FCC+L12 组织；第二个样品通过将固溶态合金在 600 °C 退火 50 h 获得，形成分级组织（CRSA600）。两种合金的磁性与力学性能列于表 1。改变热处理所带来的性能巨大差异（Hc 增大 >60 倍，屈服强度和硬度提高 >2 倍），使该合金成为一种理想的模型材料，可借助透射电子显微镜（TEM）中的定量原位磁成像技术，理解成分与组织的局部变化对矫顽力的影响。

2. 实验方法2. Experimental

A plate-shaped arc-melted Al0.3CoFeNi ingot was procured from ACI Alloys Inc., CA, USA. The composition of the cast ingot was measured to be 9.4Al-30.1Co-31.8Fe-28.6Ni (at.%) using energy dispersive X-ray spectroscopy (EDXS) in a scanning electron microscope. The cast alloy was initially homogenized at 1250 °C for 1 h. Subsequently, all samples were cold rolled to 90%, encapsulated in quartz tubes backfilled with Ar and either solutionized at 1250 °C for 5 min (CRSA) or solutionized and annealed at 600 °C for 50 h (CRSA600), followed by water quenching. Magnetization (M) vs magnetic field (H) measurements obtained from the CRSA and CRSA600 samples are shown in Fig. S1 in the Supplementary Materials.
一块板状的电弧熔炼 Al0.3CoFeNi 铸锭购自美国加州 ACI Alloys 公司。经扫描电镜中的能谱（EDXS）测定，铸锭成分为 9.4Al-30.1Co-31.8Fe-28.6Ni（原子分数）。铸态合金先在 1250 °C 均匀化 1 h。随后，所有样品冷轧至 90%，封入充 Ar 的石英管中，或在 1250 °C 固溶 5 min（CRSA），或固溶后在 600 °C 退火 50 h（CRSA600），再水淬。CRSA 与 CRSA600 样品的磁化强度（M）随磁场（H）的测量结果见补充材料图 S1。
Electron-transparent (<200 nm thick) specimens for TEM investigations were prepared using focused ion beam (FIB) sputtering with Ga in a dual beam scanning electron microscope (FEI Helios 400). A protective C layer was deposited on the surface of the alloys, followed by a conventional lift-off preparation process. The specimens were transferred to Cu Omniprobe grids. Ga-ion-beam-induced surface damage was reduced by subsequently using low energy (<1 keV) Ar ion beam milling (Fischione 1040 Nanomill).
用于 TEM 研究的电子透明（厚度 <200 nm）样品，在双束扫描电镜（FEI Helios 400）中采用 Ga 聚焦离子束（FIB）溅射制备。先在合金表面沉积保护性 C 层，再进行常规的剥离（lift-off）制样。样品转移到 Cu Omniprobe 载网上。随后用低能（<1 keV）Ar 离子束减薄（Fischione 1040 Nanomill）以减轻 Ga 离子束引起的表面损伤。
Microstructural and chemical composition analyses were carried out using a probe-aberration-corrected scanning transmission electron microscope (FEI Titan 300-200) equipped with an in-column EDXS system (Super-X technology). High-angle annular dark-field (HAADF) scanning TEM (STEM) and EDXS spectrum images were recorded and processed using ThermoFisher Velox software. The inner semi-angle of the annular dark-field detector was 69.1 mrad for HAADF STEM imaging. Magnetic imaging of domain structures was carried out in magnetic-field-free conditions (Lorentz mode) by switching off the conventional objective lens in an image aberration-corrected TEM (FEI Titan 60-300). Images were recorded using a 4k direct electron counting detector (Gatan K2 IS) in counting mode. Off-axis electron holography was carried out using a single electron biprism, with a positive voltage of up to 120 V applied to the biprism wire, giving a 3.2 nm interference fringe spacing and ~40% fringe contrast. Holograms were analyzed using a standard fast Fourier transform algorithm in custom software written in the Semper image processing language.
组织与化学成分分析使用探针球差校正的扫描透射电镜（FEI Titan 300-200）进行，该机配备柱内 EDXS 系统（Super-X 技术）。高角环形暗场（HAADF）扫描 TEM（STEM）与 EDXS 谱图用 ThermoFisher Velox 软件采集与处理。HAADF STEM 成像时环形暗场探测器的内半角为 69.1 mrad。磁畴结构的磁成像在无磁场条件（洛伦兹模式）下进行——在像差校正 TEM（FEI Titan 60-300）中关闭常规物镜，用 4k 直接电子计数探测器（Gatan K2 IS）以计数模式采集图像。离轴电子全息采用单电子双棱镜，对双棱镜丝施加最高 120 V 的正电压，得到 3.2 nm 的干涉条纹间距和约 40% 的条纹衬度。全息图用以 Semper 图像处理语言编写的自定义软件、采用标准快速傅里叶变换算法分析。

3.1 CRSA 样品的组织3.1 Microstructure of the CRSA sample

Previous atom probe tomography and TEM studies of the CRSA sample revealed the presence of Al+Ni rich L12 (ordered FCC) precipitates of average size ~1.95 nm dispersed in an FCC matrix. Fig. 1(a) shows a composition-sensitive HAADF STEM image (Image intensity ~Z², where Z is atomic number) and a corresponding bright-field (BF) STEM image recorded from the same region. The HAADF STEM image confirms the presence of local fluctuations in chemical composition, while the BF STEM image, which is sensitive to diffraction conditions, reveals dense contrast variations that are attributed to the presence of the L12 precipitates. As this specimen was prepared for magnetic imaging with a large lateral size and a uniform thickness of ~100 nm, many particles contribute to the contrast. The fact that the precipitates are coherent with the matrix is evidenced by the absence of misfit dislocations in the high-resolution HAADF STEM image shown in Fig. 1(b).
此前对 CRSA 样品的原子探针层析和 TEM 研究表明，FCC 基体中弥散着平均尺寸约 1.95 nm、富 Al+Ni 的 L12（有序 FCC）析出相。图 1(a) 给出了对成分敏感的 HAADF STEM 像（像强度 ~Z²，Z 为原子序数）以及在同一区域记录的相应明场（BF）STEM 像。HAADF STEM 像证实存在化学成分的局部起伏，而对衍射条件敏感的 BF STEM 像则显示出密集的衬度变化，归因于 L12 析出相的存在。由于该样品为磁成像制备、横向尺寸大且厚度均匀（约 100 nm），大量颗粒都对衬度有贡献。图 1(b) 的高分辨 HAADF STEM 像中不存在错配位错，证明析出相与基体共格。

3.2 CRSA 样品的磁成像3.2 Magnetic imaging of the CRSA sample

The magnetic domain structure was first studied qualitatively using Fresnel imaging at a defocus of 1 mm in magnetic-field-free conditions. Magnetic domain walls are visible as black and white contrast lines (Fig. 1(c)). Splitting of vertical domain walls into pairs close to the specimen edge is associated with the formation of closure domains to reduce energetically-costly stray fields. As it is difficult to extract quantitative information from Fresnel defocus images, off-axis electron holography was used to further study the magnetic properties, recovering the phase shift φ of the electron wave induced by the projected in-plane component of the magnetic field. Fig. 1(d) shows a magnetic induction map of closure domains in the lower left corner of Fig. 1(c), confirming one 180° and two 90° magnetic domain walls. The width of the 180° domain wall was determined from the differential (∂φ/∂x) of the recorded phase shift using a fitting function y = y0 ± a·tanh((x − x0)/w). The domain wall width δw = πw is measured to be 171 ± 5 nm in the CRSA sample, larger than typical domain wall widths in Co (24 nm), Fe (64 nm) and Ni (82 nm). The electron beam direction is close to perpendicular to the field direction in the magnetic domains, providing a reliable way to determine the domain wall width in thin TEM specimens.
首先在无磁场条件下、以 1 mm 离焦量的菲涅耳（Fresnel）成像定性研究磁畴结构。磁畴壁表现为黑白衬度线（图 1(c)）。靠近样品边缘处竖直畴壁劈裂成对，与形成闭合畴以减少能量代价高的杂散场有关。由于难以从菲涅耳离焦像中提取定量信息，遂用离轴电子全息进一步研究磁性能，重建由磁场投影面内分量所诱导的电子波相移 φ。图 1(d) 给出图 1(c) 左下角闭合畴的磁感应图，证实存在一条 180° 畴壁和两条 90° 畴壁。180° 畴壁宽度由所记录相移的微分（∂φ/∂x）确定，采用拟合函数 y = y0 ± a·tanh((x − x0)/w)。CRSA 样品中畴壁宽度 δw = πw 测得为 171 ± 5 nm，大于 Co（24 nm）、Fe（64 nm）和 Ni（82 nm）的典型畴壁宽度。电子束方向接近垂直于磁畴中的磁场方向，为在薄 TEM 样品中确定畴壁宽度提供了可靠途径。

3.3 CRSA600 样品的组织与磁成像3.3 Microstructure & magnetic imaging of the CRSA600 sample

Heat treatment of the CRSA600 sample resulted in the formation of a striking nanorod structure comprising FCC+L12 and BCC+B2 regions, as reported previously. For magnetic imaging, a TEM specimen containing more than one grain was prepared so that magnetic domains oriented both parallel and perpendicular to the nanorods could be studied. Fig. 2(a) shows a low magnification BF TEM image. Region I contains multiple long nanorods of FCC(+B2)/L12 colonies in a BCC(+B2) matrix, whose width varies between 50 and 200 nm. Region II appears to contain equidimensional grains, which correspond to the nanorods viewed along their long axes. SAED patterns (Fig. 2(b, c)) show region I oriented along [110]FCC/[111]BCC and region II along [001]FCC/[001]BCC. The diffraction spots from the FCC and BCC phases overlap, indicating a close epitaxial relationship; a mistilt of ~3° was measured between the FCC and BCC columns. Red triangles mark weak superlattice reflections associated with L12 and B2 ordering.
如此前报道，CRSA600 样品的热处理形成了引人注目的纳米棒结构，包含 FCC+L12 与 BCC+B2 区域。为进行磁成像，制备了含多个晶粒的 TEM 样品，以便研究取向既平行又垂直于纳米棒的磁畴。图 2(a) 为低倍 BF TEM 像。区域 I 含有多根长纳米棒——BCC(+B2) 基体中的 FCC(+B2)/L12 集束，宽度在 50–200 nm 之间。区域 II 看似含等轴晶粒，实为沿长轴方向观察的纳米棒。SAED 花样（图 2(b、c)）显示区域 I 沿 [110]FCC/[111]BCC 取向、区域 II 沿 [001]FCC/[001]BCC 取向。FCC 与 BCC 相的衍射斑相互重叠，表明二者存在紧密的外延关系；测得 FCC 与 BCC 柱之间约 3° 的偏转（mistilt）。红色三角标记了与 L12、B2 有序相关的弱超晶格衍射斑。
Fig. 2(d, e) shows Fresnel defocus images of representative magnetic domain structures in the two regions in magnetic-field-free conditions. Strikingly, the magnetic domain walls in region I are regular and follow the straight heterophase interfaces closely, with fine black and white streaks visible inside the columns. In contrast, region II contains a highly dense random network of magnetic domain walls. Fig. S4 shows a large-field-of-view image of the projected in-plane magnetic induction in region I determined from a pair of Fresnel defocus images using the transport-of-intensity equation.
图 2(d、e) 给出无磁场条件下两区域中代表性磁畴结构的菲涅耳离焦像。引人注目的是，区域 I 中的磁畴壁规整，紧贴平直的异相界面走向，柱体内部可见细小的黑白条纹。相比之下，区域 II 含有极为密集的随机磁畴壁网络。图 S4 给出由一对菲涅耳离焦像经强度传输方程（TIE）确定的区域 I 投影面内磁感应的大视场图像。

3.4 磁畴与组织的关联3.4 Correlation between magnetic domains and microstructure

STEM EDXS spectrum imaging correlated the compositional variations of the FCC and BCC phases with the positions of magnetic domains in the CRSA600 sample. Fig. 3(a) shows a BF STEM image and corresponding Co, Fe, Ni and Al maps from region II, showing that the Co–Fe-rich BCC phase is the matrix, in which Ni–Al-rich FCC columns are distributed. The EDXS maps reveal compositional fluctuations in the Ni–Al-rich FCC columns and additional Ni enrichment parallel to the FCC/BCC interphase boundaries. Fig. 3(d) shows EDXS line scans across the interface; within the FCC column, an approximately 7-nm-wide Ni–Al-rich and Co–Fe-poor region runs parallel to the FCC/BCC interface. Based on these results and APT reconstructions (Fig. S2), the morphology of the FCC and BCC regions can be described as colonies of FCC/L12 nanorods embedded in a BCC/B2 matrix. Within both the FCC/L12 nanorods and the BCC/B2 matrix, there is a finer-scale decomposition into ordered and disordered lamellar regions. A high-resolution HAADF STEM image (Fig. 3(e)) shows a uniform lattice in both grains.
STEM EDXS 谱图成像将 FCC 与 BCC 相的成分变化与 CRSA600 样品中磁畴的位置关联起来。图 3(a) 给出区域 II 的 BF STEM 像及相应的 Co、Fe、Ni、Al 元素图，显示富 Co–Fe 的 BCC 相为基体，其中分布着富 Ni–Al 的 FCC 柱。EDXS 图揭示了富 Ni–Al 的 FCC 柱内的成分起伏，以及沿 FCC/BCC 相界平行方向的额外 Ni 富集。图 3(d) 给出跨界面的 EDXS 线扫描；在 FCC 柱内，一条约 7 nm 宽、富 Ni–Al 而贫 Co–Fe 的区域平行于 FCC/BCC 界面延伸。基于这些结果及 APT 重构（图 S2），FCC 与 BCC 区域的形貌可描述为嵌于 BCC/B2 基体中的 FCC/L12 纳米棒集束。在 FCC/L12 纳米棒和 BCC/B2 基体内部，还存在更细尺度的、分解为有序与无序片层区域的结构。高分辨 HAADF STEM 像（图 3(e)）显示两晶粒中晶格均匀。
The magnetic domains in the CRSA600 sample were further analyzed using off-axis electron holography. To separate the electrostatic and magnetic contributions to the phase shift, pairs of electron holograms were recorded before and after turning the specimen over, using a cartridge-based holder; half of the sum and half of the difference give the electrostatic and magnetic contributions, respectively. The downward-oriented magnetic domains in the FCC+L12 columns contain straight magnetic contour lines, with small variations that may be associated with ordered L12 regions. In contrast, well-defined regions with no magnetic field lines in the BCC+B2 columns indicate weakly magnetic or non-magnetic inclusions; EDXS confirms these correspond to Ni–Al-rich inclusions. The width of the domain walls pinned at grain boundaries was determined to be δw = 35 ± 3 nm (Fig. 4(c)), almost 5 times smaller than in the CRSA sample. The domain wall width scales as δw ~ √(A/Keff), where A is the exchange stiffness and Keff the effective magnetic anisotropy; however, the CRSA600 sample contains several phases with different magnetic properties, and the magnetic domain walls overlap with the Al–Ni-rich enrichment between the FCC and BCC columns.
进一步用离轴电子全息分析 CRSA600 样品中的磁畴。为分离相移中的静电与磁贡献，使用可翻转样品盒在翻转样品前后各记录一对电子全息图；二者之和的一半与之差的一半分别给出静电与磁贡献。FCC+L12 柱中向下取向的磁畴含有平直的磁等值线，其中的小幅变化可能与有序 L12 区域相关。相反，BCC+B2 柱中界限清晰、无磁力线的区域指示弱磁或非磁夹杂；EDXS 证实这些区域对应富 Ni–Al 夹杂。钉扎于晶界处的畴壁宽度测得为 δw = 35 ± 3 nm（图 4(c)），几乎比 CRSA 样品小 5 倍。畴壁宽度满足 δw ~ √(A/Keff)，其中 A 为交换刚度、Keff 为有效磁各向异性；但 CRSA600 样品含有多个磁性不同的相，且磁畴壁与 FCC、BCC 柱之间富 Al–Ni 的富集区相重叠。

4. 讨论4. Discussion

Magnetism in HEAs can be a complex phenomenon, especially in the presence of hierarchical multi-phase microstructures over multiple length scales, which in turn result from competing phase stabilities between solid solution and ordered phases. The possibility to combine transition metals with other elements and different processing parameters provides a wide range of opportunities to tailor the magnetic and functional properties of HEAs. In the present non-equimolar Al0.3CoFeNi CCA, solid-state decomposition induced by thermomechanical treatment results in a eutectoid-like microstructure of FCC+L12 nanorods in a BCC+B2 matrix. Thermomechanical treatment of the CRSA600 sample increases the coercivity by a factor of 64. High spatial resolution magnetic characterization reveals that the average magnetic domain size (Dc) is reduced by an order of magnitude in the CRSA600 sample, as a result of magnetic domain walls at heterophase boundaries separating the FCC and BCC phases. Furthermore, the domain wall width is reduced significantly from 171 ± 5 to 35 ± 3 nm, suggesting that homophase interfaces in the FCC alloy are ineffective at pinning domain walls, leading to low coercivity.
高熵合金中的磁性可能是一种复杂现象，尤其是在存在跨多个尺度的分级多相组织时，而这种组织又源自固溶体相与有序相之间相互竞争的相稳定性。将过渡金属与其他元素组合、并配以不同工艺参数，为调控高熵合金的磁性与功能性能提供了广阔空间。在本文非等摩尔的 Al0.3CoFeNi CCA 中，热机械处理诱导的固态分解形成了类共析组织——BCC+B2 基体中的 FCC+L12 纳米棒。CRSA600 样品的热机械处理使矫顽力增大 64 倍。高空间分辨磁表征揭示，由于磁畴壁位于分隔 FCC、BCC 相的异相界面处，CRSA600 样品的平均磁畴尺寸（Dc）减小了一个数量级。此外，畴壁宽度从 171 ± 5 nm 显著减小到 35 ± 3 nm，说明 FCC 合金中的同相界面对钉扎畴壁无效，导致低矫顽力。
In contrast, in the CRSA600 sample the presence of FCC and BCC phases with different compositions results in different local magnetic properties. In the FCC nanorods, the Ni–Al-rich ordered L12 phase displays straight magnetic field lines with only slight angular variations. In contrast, the Ni–Al-rich ordered B2 regions in the BCC phase strongly affect the magnetic induction, as the density of field lines is reduced at their locations. The saturation magnetization of the two samples is similar, with a slight increase in CRSA600 attributable to the BCC phase. The pinning of domain walls at heterophase FCC/BCC boundaries is influenced by crystallographic and compositional transitions across these boundaries, as well as by Ni and Al enrichment in the FCC phase along them, resulting in the relatively narrow domain wall width of 35 nm.
相比之下，在 CRSA600 样品中，成分不同的 FCC 与 BCC 相带来了不同的局部磁性能。在 FCC 纳米棒中，富 Ni–Al 的有序 L12 相表现出平直、仅有轻微角度变化的磁力线；而 BCC 相中富 Ni–Al 的有序 B2 区域则强烈影响磁感应——其所在位置磁力线密度降低。两样品的饱和磁化强度相近，CRSA600 略有升高，可归因于 BCC 相。异相 FCC/BCC 界面对畴壁的钉扎，受这些界面两侧的晶体学与成分过渡、以及沿界面 FCC 相中 Ni、Al 富集的影响，导致 CRSA600 样品相对较窄的 35 nm 畴壁宽度。
Our observations suggest that the magnetization reversal mechanism responsible for coercivity changes from nucleation-type in the CRSA sample to pinning-type in the CRSA600 sample. To validate this, in situ magnetization reversal experiments were carried out inside the TEM by exciting the objective lens to a pre-calibrated value and tilting the sample between −30° and +30° to apply an effective in-plane field. In the CRSA sample, domain walls nucleate at the specimen edge and move across the field of view without interruption, showing a lack of pinning. In the CRSA600 sample, even at the maximum in-plane field (~600 mT) a saturated state could not be reached; switching events occurred at discrete domain walls as the field increased from 0 to +81 and +133 mT, with the domain wall contrast reversing sign locally. Magnetization switching forms domain walls containing both black and white contrast—so-called Bloch caps in 180° domain walls. These experiments confirm that the reversal process changes from nucleation-type (CRSA) to pinning-type (CRSA600) as a result of phase decomposition after prolonged annealing at 600 °C for 50 h, producing a nanorod microstructure with heterophase FCC/BCC interfaces.
我们的观察表明，决定矫顽力的磁化反转机制从 CRSA 样品的形核型转变为 CRSA600 样品的钉扎型。为验证这一点，在 TEM 内进行了原位磁化反转实验——将物镜励磁到预校准值，并将样品在 −30° 到 +30° 之间倾转以施加有效面内磁场。在 CRSA 样品中，畴壁在样品边缘形核并不受阻地扫过视场，表明缺乏钉扎。在 CRSA600 样品中，即使在最大面内磁场（约 600 mT）下也无法达到饱和态；随磁场从 0 增大到 +81、+133 mT，在离散的畴壁处发生翻转事件，畴壁衬度局部反号。磁化翻转形成了同时含黑白衬度的畴壁——即 180° 畴壁中的所谓布洛赫帽（Bloch cap）。这些实验证实，经 600 °C 长时间退火 50 h 的相分解，形成了带异相 FCC/BCC 界面的纳米棒组织，使反转过程从（CRSA 的）形核型转变为（CRSA600 的）钉扎型。
The present study provides fundamental insight into the role of interphase interfaces on magnetization reversal through domain wall pinning. Heterophase interfaces between phases with different crystal structures (e.g., FCC/BCC) are effective at pinning magnetic domain walls and consequently increase coercivity, particularly when they occur at near-periodic intervals on deep-sub-μm length scales, as in the present nanorod microstructure. In contrast, homophase interfaces of order/disorder type (FCC+L12 or BCC+B2) do not significantly hinder domain wall motion. This insight can guide the design of both high entropy and conventional alloys with desired combinations of magnetic and mechanical properties: for high strength with low coercivity, introducing a large volume fraction of ordered precipitates within a parent solid solution is effective, especially if misfit strain is minimized; conversely, higher coercivity can be produced by a nanorod structure with heterophase boundaries, and uniaxial anisotropy can be tailored via strain- or magnetic-field-induced anisotropy during annealing—analogous to strategies used for Alnico-type and Sm–Co hard magnets.
本研究为界面（相间界面）通过畴壁钉扎对磁化反转的作用提供了基础性认识。晶体结构不同的相之间的异相界面（如 FCC/BCC）能有效钉扎磁畴壁，从而增大矫顽力，尤其是当它们在深亚微米尺度上近乎周期性出现时（如本文的纳米棒组织）。相反，有序/无序型的同相界面（FCC+L12 或 BCC+B2）不会显著阻碍畴壁运动。这一认识可指导设计具有所需磁-力学性能组合的高熵合金与传统合金：若要在低矫顽力下获得高强度，在母相固溶体中引入大体积分数的有序析出相是有效的，尤其是在错配应变最小化时；反之，带异相界面的纳米棒结构可产生更高矫顽力，而单轴各向异性可通过退火过程中应变或磁场诱导的各向异性来定制——这与 Alnico 型和 Sm–Co 硬磁体所用的策略类似。

5. 结论5. Conclusions

Our high spatial resolution investigation of the microstructural and magnetic behavior of an Al0.3CoFeNi complex concentrated alloy has revealed an intricate coupling between the presence of a hierarchical multi-length-scale multi-phase microstructure and the motion of magnetic domain walls. High-resolution in situ techniques show that the magnetization reversal process, which is responsible for coercivity, changes from a nucleation-type mechanism in the FCC+L12 microstructure of this alloy, with a domain wall width of 171 nm, to a pinning-type mechanism in the nanorod microstructure of the same alloy, with a domain wall width of 35 nm. Significantly, heterophase FCC/BCC nanoscale interfaces strongly pin magnetic domain walls, increasing coercivity when compared to homophase chemically ordered/disordered interfaces. These results provide a powerful guide for the rational design of microstructure to tune the magnetic and mechanical properties of both high entropy alloys and more conventional magnetic alloys.
我们对 Al0.3CoFeNi 复杂浓缩合金组织与磁行为的高空间分辨研究，揭示了分级、多尺度、多相组织的存在与磁畴壁运动之间的复杂耦合。高分辨原位技术表明，决定矫顽力的磁化反转过程，从该合金 FCC+L12 组织中的形核型机制（畴壁宽度 171 nm），转变为同一合金纳米棒组织中的钉扎型机制（畴壁宽度 35 nm）。尤为重要的是，异相 FCC/BCC 纳米尺度界面强烈钉扎磁畴壁，相较同相化学有序/无序界面显著增大矫顽力。这些结果为通过合理设计组织来调控高熵合金及更传统磁性合金的磁性与力学性能提供了有力指导。
