Nature Communications · 2024

两吉帕高强度、可塑性变形的软磁体Two-gigapascal-strong ductile soft magnets

Nat. Commun. (2024) 15:10119 · doi:10.1038/s41467-024-53793-2 · Liuliu Han, Nicolas J. Peter, Fernando Maccari, András Kovács, Jin Wang, Yixuan Zhang, Ruiwen Xie, Yuxiang Wu, Ruth Schwaiger, Hongbin Zhang, Zhiming Li, Oliver Gutfleisch, Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski, Dierk Raabe
本页为「摘要 + 正文（Results and discussion）」中文翻译；已省略 Methods（方法）、补充材料与参考文献。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Soft magnetic materials (SMMs) are indispensable for electromechanical energy conversion in high-efficiency applications, but they are exposed to increasing mechanical loading conditions in electric motors due to higher rotational speeds. Enhancing the yield strength of SMMs is essential to prevent the degradation in magnetic performance and failure from plastic deformation, yet most SMMs have yield strengths far below one gigapascal. Here, we present a multicomponent nanostructuring strategy that doubles the yield strength of SMMs while maintaining ductility. We introduce morphologically anisotropic nanoprecipitates through dislocation-driven precipitation induced by preceding deformation during heat treatment in an iron–nickel–cobalt–tantalum material. With all dimensions of the precipitates below the magnetic domain wall width, we achieve a high precipitate number density with a large specific surface area, small interprecipitate spacing, and high lattice mismatch, which impede dislocation glide and strengthen the material. Both the matrix and precipitates are ferromagnetic, yielding a high magnetic moment. This nanostructuring approach offers a pathway to two-gigapascal-strong ductile SMMs with moderately increased coercivity that can be tolerated in exchange for significantly improved mechanical performance for sustainable electrification.
软磁材料（SMM）对高效应用中的机电能量转换不可或缺，但随着转速提高，它们在电机中承受的机械载荷日益增大。提升软磁材料的屈服强度对于防止磁性能退化和塑性变形失效至关重要，然而大多数软磁材料的屈服强度远低于 1 GPa。本文提出一种多组元纳米结构化策略，在保持塑性的同时使软磁材料的屈服强度翻倍。我们在一种铁–镍–钴–钽材料中，利用预先变形引入的位错，在热处理时驱动位错主导的析出，形成"形貌各向异性"的纳米析出相。由于析出相的各个维度都小于磁畴壁宽度，我们获得了高的析出相数量密度，兼具大的比表面积、小的析出相间距和高的晶格错配，从而阻碍位错滑移、强化材料。基体和析出相均为铁磁性，带来高磁矩。这种纳米结构化方法为实现两吉帕强度、可塑性变形的软磁体提供了途径——其矫顽力有适度升高，但为换取显著提升的力学性能，这一代价在可持续电气化中是可以接受的。
Soft magnetic materials (SMMs) exhibit rapid magnetic flux variations in response to changes in external magnetic fields. Electrification using SMMs in energy conversion applications is the fastest and most sustainable approach for counteracting climate change and solving the global energy crisis. Minimization of coercivity is crucial for reducing magnetic hysteresis losses in the form of dissipative heat during high-frequency magnetization and demagnetization. At the same time, increasing demand is required for SMMs with high yield strength and good ductility that can withstand harsh and dynamic mechanical loads in electrical motors and high-speed flywheel energy storage systems. The yield strength is the stress at which irreversible plastic deformation starts; thus, loading stresses must remain below this value for safe and efficient operation, otherwise high-speed electrical machines and their rotating parts undergo irreversible dimensional changes. SMMs with good ductility are also needed for manufacturing flexibility and damage tolerance. If the yield strength is too low, a build-up of inelastic deformation carriers will impede the free motion of magnetic domain walls, deteriorating the soft magnetic properties. Realizing a high yield strength requires preventing dislocation nucleation and motion through microscopic backstresses exerted by atomic-scale lattice distortion and lattice defects. This effect, termed mechanical strengthening, results in a fundamental design challenge because the internal stress fields of lattice defects can pin magnetic domain wall motion, thereby destroying the soft magnetic properties. Conventional microstructural strengthening measures often result in embrittlement and undesired domain wall interactions; coercivity and hysteresis-related energy losses may increase, and soft magnetic performance is lost.
软磁材料（SMM）能随外磁场的变化产生快速的磁通变化。在能量转换应用中使用软磁材料进行电气化，是应对气候变化、解决全球能源危机最快、最可持续的途径。尽量降低矫顽力对减少高频磁化–退磁过程中以耗散热形式出现的磁滞损耗至关重要。与此同时，人们对兼具高屈服强度和良好塑性、能承受电机和高速飞轮储能系统中严酷动态机械载荷的软磁材料需求日益增长。屈服强度是不可逆塑性变形开始时的应力；因此，载荷应力必须低于该值才能安全高效运行，否则高速电机及其旋转部件会发生不可逆的尺寸变化。塑性良好的软磁材料还能提供加工灵活性和损伤容限。若屈服强度过低，非弹性变形载体的积累会阻碍磁畴壁的自由运动，恶化软磁性能。要实现高屈服强度，就需要通过原子尺度晶格畸变和晶格缺陷施加的微观背应力来阻止位错的形核与运动。这一效应称为力学强化，它带来一个根本性的设计难题：晶格缺陷的内应力场会钉扎磁畴壁运动，从而破坏软磁性能。传统的组织强化手段常导致脆化和不利的畴壁相互作用；矫顽力和磁滞相关的能量损耗可能增大，软磁性能随之丧失。
Attempts have been made to address this challenge by introducing and tuning various types, sizes, and coherency states of second-phase precipitates into conventional SMMs and multicomponent-based magnets. Coherent cuboidal precipitates of well-tuned size can result in low magnetic domain wall pinning. However, the increase in attainable strength has proven to be limited to values far below one gigapascal. This is because the strengthening effect of coherent precipitates by dislocation shearing increases with size but cannot exceed the Orowan stress, at which dislocations circumvent precipitates. In contrast, incoherent precipitates are nonshearable obstacles for dislocations. Yet their large interfacial energy usually leads to coarse micron-sized precipitates that can strongly pin the motion of magnetic domain walls, thus losing soft magnetic features.
人们曾尝试通过在传统软磁材料和多组元磁体中引入并调控不同类型、尺寸和共格状态的第二相析出相来应对这一挑战。尺寸调控得当的共格立方（cuboidal）析出相可实现较低的磁畴壁钉扎。然而，可达到的强度提升被证明仅限于远低于 1 GPa 的水平。这是因为共格析出相通过位错剪切的强化效应虽随尺寸增大而增强，但不能超过 Orowan 应力——达到该应力时位错会绕过析出相。相反，非共格析出相是位错无法剪切的障碍，但其较大的界面能通常导致粗大的微米级析出相，会强烈钉扎磁畴壁运动，从而丧失软磁特性。
We propose a strategy to resolve this dilemma through a nanostructuring approach involving morphologically anisotropic nanoprecipitates. The synergistic effects of the morphology, distribution, size, and composition of the precipitates are achieved by: (a) The anisotropic morphology increases the surface area-to-volume ratio and lattice mismatch, strengthening the material via mechanical interactions with dislocations. (b) A finer size distribution reduces the interprecipitate spacing, enhancing strength. (c) The micromagnetically relevant length scales of the precipitates are kept below the magnetic domain wall width, minimizing interference with domain wall motion and thus reducing magnetic losses. (d) High concentrations of ferromagnetic elements with the highest possible magnetization are used. (e) We address the design challenge of triggering coherent, ferromagnetic, and anisotropic nanoprecipitates under thermodynamically nonequilibrium conditions while suppressing capillary-driven coarsening and avoiding the transition to incoherency.
我们提出一种策略，借助引入"形貌各向异性"纳米析出相的纳米结构化方法来化解这一两难。析出相在形貌、分布、尺寸和成分上的协同效应通过以下途径实现：(a) 各向异性形貌增大表面积/体积比和晶格错配，从而通过与位错的力学相互作用强化材料；(b) 更细的尺寸分布减小析出相间距，提高强度；(c) 析出相在微磁学上相关的尺度始终小于磁畴壁宽度，最大限度减少对畴壁运动的干扰，从而降低磁损耗；(d) 采用高浓度、尽可能高磁化强度的铁磁性元素；(e) 我们解决了在热力学非平衡条件下触发共格、铁磁、各向异性纳米析出相，同时抑制毛细驱动粗化、避免向非共格转变这一设计挑战。
These considerations have led us to a class of non-equiatomic iron–nickel–cobalt–tantalum multicomponent alloys (MCAs). The first three elements form a ferromagnetic matrix providing high magnetization, while the fourth triggers ordered precipitates. To achieve morphologically anisotropic and coherent nanoprecipitates with a fine distribution, we introduce microbands via plastic deformation along {111} planes to control the dislocation density and precipitate morphology. The microbands contain a high line density of planar dislocations, promoting rapid nucleation. Short-term isothermal annealing (1073 K, 10 min) triggers precipitates whose longitudinal edges lie along the fcc slip planes—a behaviour resulting from dislocation-assisted nucleation that promotes coherent-interface precipitates in a thermodynamic nonequilibrium state, enabling rapid nucleation, high number density, and small interprecipitate spacing. A minor fraction of partially coherent precipitates with similar chemistry/morphology but different structure coexists, formed during the evolution toward a more stable state. Extended treatment (1073 K, 60–300 min) tends to form incoherent precipitates until equilibrium; incoherent phase boundaries and coarsening cause an undesired decrease in strength and increase in coercivity.
基于这些考量，我们确定了一类非等原子比的铁–镍–钴–钽多组元合金（MCA）。前三种元素构成提供高磁化强度的铁磁性基体，第四种元素则触发有序析出相。为获得形貌各向异性、共格且细弥散的纳米析出相，我们通过沿 {111} 面的塑性变形引入微带（microband），以控制位错密度和析出相形貌。微带含有高线密度的平面位错，促进快速形核。短时等温退火（1073 K，10 min）触发析出相，其纵向棱边沿 fcc 滑移面分布——这一行为源于位错辅助形核，能在热力学非平衡态下促进形成具有共格界面的析出相，实现快速形核、高数量密度和小析出相间距。少量成分/形貌相近但结构不同的部分共格析出相与之共存，它们在向更稳定态演化的过程中相继形成。延长处理（1073 K，60–300 min）则倾向于形成非共格析出相直至达到平衡；非共格相界和粗化会带来不利的强度下降和矫顽力上升。

组织分析Microstructural analysis

To enhance the mechanical interaction between precipitates and dislocations, we designed an Fe35Co30Ni30Ta5 (at.%) alloy system using Thermo-Calc with the High Entropy Alloy database. This system aims for a supersaturated solid solution single-phase structure by solution treatment (1473 K) and a second phase with a different crystal structure by annealing (773–1173 K), to obtain a strong ferromagnetic solid solution matrix and an intermetallic phase with different crystal symmetry or anisotropic interfacial energies that can assume an anisotropic morphology. The base material "B-MCA" with a single-phase fcc structure was made by conventional casting, hot rolling, and homogenization at 1473 K (confirmed by XRD). B-MCA was cold rolled for 60% thickness reduction to introduce a high line density (3.2 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²) of planar dislocations on {111}fcc planes as nucleation sites (Fig. 1a, recorded by ECC imaging). The resulting single-fcc alloy with elongated grains along the cold-rolling direction is termed D-MCA ("D" = deformed). No obvious elemental segregation was observed from the microscale to the near-atomic scale, indicating chemical homogeneity.
为增强析出相与位错之间的力学相互作用，我们用 Thermo-Calc 配合高熵合金数据库设计了 Fe35Co30Ni30Ta5（原子分数）合金体系。该体系旨在通过固溶处理（1473 K）获得过饱和固溶体单相结构，并通过退火（773–1173 K）获得晶体结构不同的第二相，从而得到强铁磁性固溶体基体和一种具有不同晶体对称性或各向异性界面能、可呈现各向异性形貌的金属间化合物相。基础材料"B-MCA"（单相 fcc 结构）通过常规铸造、热轧和 1473 K 均匀化制得（经 XRD 证实）。将 B-MCA 冷轧减薄 60%，在 {111}fcc 面上引入高线密度（3.2 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²）的平面位错作为形核位点（图 1a，用 ECC 成像记录）。所得沿冷轧方向具有拉长晶粒的单相 fcc 合金记为 D-MCA（"D"代表变形 deformed）。从微观尺度到近原子尺度均未观察到明显的元素偏析，表明其化学均匀。
Nanoprecipitation was initiated by annealing D-MCA at 1073 K for 10 min. The resulting alloy contains a high number density (~(2.32 ± 0.34) × 10²² m⁻³) and volume fraction (~38%) of morphologically anisotropic nanoprecipitates (53 nm long, 8 nm wide) and is termed A-MCA ("A" = anisotropic; Fig. 1b). This gives a small effective planar inter-precipitate spacing (26.1 ± 6.1 nm) and a large specific surface area (3.3 × 10⁸ m⁻¹). The precipitates are distributed homogeneously in the fcc matrix (average grain size 101 ± 49 μm) with an {001}<211> texture and deformation-induced twins; their longitudinal edges are parallel to <111>fcc. STEM-HAADF imaging shows some precipitates in hcp arrangements (partially coherent), while others are L12 (ordered fcc) fully coherent with the matrix. The L12 nanoprecipitates constitute the major fraction, since no hcp diffraction peaks/superlattice spots appeared in XRD and SAED. The anisotropic morphology is attributed to anisotropic interfacial energies—0.11 and 0.64 J·m⁻² for the (111) and (100) planes, respectively (DFT). APT (Fig. 1d, 7 at.% Ta interface threshold) gives matrix Fe39.2Co30.5Ni27.6Ta2.7 and precipitate Ni37.7Co31.1Ta20.2Fe11.0 (at.%); Fe is depleted and Ta partitions strongly into the precipitates.
通过将 D-MCA 在 1073 K 退火 10 min 引发纳米析出。所得合金含有高数量密度（约 (2.32 ± 0.34) × 10²² m⁻³）和高体积分数（约 38%）的形貌各向异性纳米析出相（长 53 nm、宽 8 nm），记为 A-MCA（"A"代表各向异性 anisotropic；图 1b）。由此得到小的有效面内析出相间距（26.1 ± 6.1 nm）和大的比表面积（3.3 × 10⁸ m⁻¹）。析出相均匀分布于 fcc 基体（平均晶粒尺寸 101 ± 49 μm）中，基体具有 {001}<211> 织构和变形诱导孪晶；析出相的纵向棱边平行于 <111>fcc。STEM-HAADF 成像显示部分析出相为 hcp 排列（部分共格），另一些为与基体完全共格的 L12（有序 fcc）。L12 纳米析出相占主要比例，因为 XRD 和 SAED 中未出现 hcp 衍射峰/超晶格斑。各向异性形貌归因于各向异性的界面能——据 DFT，(111) 面和 (100) 面的界面能分别为 0.11 和 0.64 J·m⁻²。APT（图 1d，以 7 at.% Ta 作为界面阈值）给出基体成分 Fe39.2Co30.5Ni27.6Ta2.7、析出相成分 Ni37.7Co31.1Ta20.2Fe11.0（原子分数）；Fe 贫化，Ta 强烈分配进入析出相。
An intermediate treatment (973 K, 10 min) produced "I-MCA", expected to share A-MCA's precipitation mechanism at a lower kinetic factor. Its dislocation arrangement resembles D-MCA (lower line density 2.8 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²). Correlative TEM-APT (Fig. 1f) identified two dislocation lines as Ta-enriched regions (7 at.% Ta iso-surface), confirming preferential Ta segregation to dislocations that creates highly distorted regions initiating dislocation-assisted preferred precipitation. Pronounced superlattice diffraction from the ordered fcc lattice confirms the phase transformation of I-MCA is fundamentally the same as A-MCA. Notably, recrystallization was not observed even at 1173 K (0.7 Tm) and 300 min, because static recrystallization is suppressed by (1) precipitates pinning grain boundaries and (2) the high stacking fault energy (73.9 mJ·m⁻²) enabling dislocation double cross slip and screw annihilation. Prolonging the dwell or raising the temperature promotes transformation to more stable incoherent hcp precipitates. To confirm the role of preceding planar dislocations, B-MCA subjected to the same 1073 K/10 min treatment gave "S-MCA" ("S" = spherical) with coherent spherical precipitates (~11.1 ± 4.8 nm, volume fraction ~44.3 ± 3.6%).
中间处理（973 K，10 min）得到"I-MCA"，预期在较低动力学因子下与 A-MCA 共享析出机制。其位错排布与 D-MCA 相似（线密度较低，2.8 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²）。相关性 TEM-APT（图 1f）将两条位错线识别为富 Ta 区域（7 at.% Ta 等浓度面），证实 Ta 优先偏聚于位错、形成高度畸变区并引发位错辅助的择优析出。来自有序 fcc 晶格的明显超晶格衍射证实 I-MCA 的相变与 A-MCA 本质相同。值得注意的是，即使在 1173 K（0.7 倍熔点）和 300 min 下也未观察到再结晶，因为静态再结晶被以下因素抑制：(1) 析出相钉扎晶界；(2) 高层错能（73.9 mJ·m⁻²）使位错发生双交滑移并湮灭螺型分量。延长保温或提高温度会促进向更稳定的非共格 hcp 析出相转变。为确认预置平面位错的作用，对 B-MCA 施加相同的 1073 K/10 min 处理得到"S-MCA"（"S"代表球形 spherical），其含有共格球形析出相（约 11.1 ± 4.8 nm，体积分数约 44.3 ± 3.6%）。

力学与磁性能Mechanical and magnetic properties

The introduction of morphologically anisotropic precipitates yields a two-gigapascal-strong ductile soft magnet. Figure 2a shows the tensile stress–strain curves of A-MCA: yield strength (σy) 1.93 ± 0.02 GPa, total elongation 12.6 ± 0.9%. This high yield strength keeps the material elastic even under severe stresses without generating dislocations, preventing irreversible shape changes and undesired dislocation–domain-wall interactions during service, while its good ductility allows manufacturing flexibility and damage tolerance. Compared with variants of the same chemistry—B-MCA (homogenized, no precipitates), D-MCA (cold-rolled, no precipitates), and S-MCA (spherical precipitates)—A-MCA shows a nearly fourfold (~385%) increase in yield strength over precipitate-free B-MCA. D-MCA shows no strain hardening (necks immediately after yield) because dislocation motion/multiplication is impeded by the dense dislocation network (~3.2 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²). S-MCA has a yield strength below one gigapascal. A-MCA maintains a high dislocation density (2.5 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²) plus a high number density (2.3 × 10²² m⁻³) of anisotropic nanoprecipitates, giving 1.9 GPa yield strength while remaining ductile—resolving a long-standing dilemma—with a multi-stage work-hardening capability.
引入形貌各向异性析出相得到了两吉帕强度、可塑性变形的软磁体。图 2a 给出 A-MCA 的拉伸应力–应变曲线：屈服强度（σy）1.93 ± 0.02 GPa，总延伸率 12.6 ± 0.9%。如此高的屈服强度使材料即使在严酷应力下也保持弹性、不产生位错，从而防止服役中不可逆的形状变化以及不利的位错–畴壁相互作用；而良好塑性则提供加工灵活性和损伤容限。与成分相同的各变体——B-MCA（均匀化、无析出相）、D-MCA（冷轧、无析出相）和 S-MCA（球形析出相）——相比，A-MCA 的屈服强度较无析出相的 B-MCA 提高近四倍（约 385%）。D-MCA 无应变硬化（屈服后立即颈缩），因为位错运动/增殖被密集的位错网络（约 3.2 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²）阻碍。S-MCA 的屈服强度低于 1 GPa。A-MCA 既保持高位错密度（2.5 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²），又具有高数量密度（2.3 × 10²² m⁻³）的各向异性纳米析出相，屈服强度达 1.9 GPa 且仍保持塑性——化解了长期存在的两难——并表现出多阶段加工硬化能力。
Figure 2b shows the magnetic performance. A-MCA exhibits typical soft ferromagnetic behaviour with a saturation magnetization (Ms) of 115.4 ± 0.2 Am²·kg⁻¹ and coercivity (Hc) of 0.36 ± 0.03 kA·m⁻¹. Compared with our previously designed strong ductile soft magnet Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7 (Ms 100.2 Am²·kg⁻¹), A-MCA shows a ~15% higher Ms. DFT (exact muffin-tin orbital method) gives a total magnetic moment of 1.50 μB/f.u. for Fe35Co30Ni30Ta5, ~15% higher than 1.32 μB/f.u. for Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7, consistent with experiment. Since the local moments of Fe, Co, Ni are only slightly higher, the overall increase is attributed mainly to the higher concentration of ferromagnetic elements (95 at.% in A-MCA vs 88 at.%). Importantly, A-MCA has the lowest Hc among the variants of identical chemistry, achieved by controlling the micromagnetically relevant length scales—coherency, type, density, and morphology of defects—to reduce pinning from lattice distortion and internal stress. Ashby maps (Fig. 2c, d) show A-MCA combines high σy with moderate Hc, outperforming conventional SMMs and established MCAs; its yield strength exceeds all other MCAs (Supplementary Fig. 13). Its electrical resistivity is higher than pure metals, Fe-Si (<6.5 wt.% Si) and Fe-Co, and comparable to Fe-Ni and established MCAs—favorable for reducing eddy currents in high-frequency applications.
图 2b 给出磁性能。A-MCA 表现出典型的软铁磁行为，饱和磁化强度（Ms）115.4 ± 0.2 Am²·kg⁻¹，矫顽力（Hc）0.36 ± 0.03 kA·m⁻¹。与我们此前设计的强韧软磁体 Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7（Ms 100.2 Am²·kg⁻¹）相比，A-MCA 的 Ms 高约 15%。DFT（精确 muffin-tin 轨道法）给出 Fe35Co30Ni30Ta5 的总磁矩为 1.50 μB/f.u.，比 Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7 的 1.32 μB/f.u. 高约 15%，与实验一致。由于 Fe、Co、Ni 的局部磁矩仅略高，磁化强度的整体提升主要归因于更高的铁磁性元素浓度（A-MCA 中为 95 at.%，对比 88 at.%）。重要的是，在成分相同的各变体中 A-MCA 矫顽力最低，这是通过控制微磁学相关尺度——缺陷的共格性、类型、密度和形貌——以减少晶格畸变和内应力钉扎而实现的。Ashby 图（图 2c、d）显示 A-MCA 兼具高 σy 与中等 Hc，优于传统软磁材料和已有 MCA；其屈服强度超过所有其他 MCA（补充图 13）。其电阻率高于纯金属、Fe-Si（<6.5 wt.% Si）和 Fe-Co，与 Fe-Ni 及已有 MCA 相当——有利于在高频应用中降低涡流。

强化与增韧机制Strengthening and toughening mechanisms

To understand the combination of high strength and good ductility, we studied the deformation substructure using EBSD, ECCI and TEM at different straining stages (Fig. 3). Pile-up of geometrically necessary dislocations at grain boundaries is reflected by the kernel average misorientation (KAM), a local measure of inelastic lattice curvature (Fig. 3a). Microbands aligned along {111}fcc show pronounced dynamic refinement (Fig. 3b), inducing high dislocation passing stresses and continuous strain hardening that prevents damage initiation. Two types of discontinuous, incoherent, Ta-enriched grain-boundary precipitates form (promoted by Ta's high diffusion coefficient, 4.7 × 10⁻¹⁸ m²·s⁻¹ vs ~10⁻¹⁸–10⁻¹⁹ for Ni, Fe, Co at 1073 K). Although strain localization near these precipitates causes weakness and detachment at a later stage (Fig. 3c), A-MCA shows typical ductile fracture because the incoherent precipitates' small volume fraction (0.3%) keeps their influence on damage initiation small.
为理解高强度与良好塑性的组合，我们用 EBSD、ECCI 和 TEM 研究了不同应变阶段的变形亚结构（图 3）。晶界处几何必需位错的塞积由核平均取向差（KAM）反映，KAM 是对非弹性晶格曲率的局部度量（图 3a）。沿 {111}fcc 排列的微带表现出明显的动态细化（图 3b），产生高的位错通过应力和持续的应变硬化，从而防止损伤萌生。形成了两类不连续、非共格、富 Ta 的晶界析出相（由 Ta 的高扩散系数促成——1073 K 时 Ta 为 4.7 × 10⁻¹⁸ m²·s⁻¹，而 Ni、Fe、Co 约为 10⁻¹⁸–10⁻¹⁹）。尽管这些析出相附近的应变局部化在后期造成弱化和脱粘（图 3c），但由于非共格析出相体积分数很小（0.3%），其对损伤萌生的影响很小，A-MCA 仍表现出典型的韧性断裂。
TEM near the fracture surface revealed multiple parallel mechanical twins (number density (4.2 ± 0.7) × 10¹² m⁻², average width 14.7 ± 4.7 nm). Atomic-resolution STEM (Fig. 3d) shows twinning in the fcc matrix and at the matrix–precipitate interface, with FFT consistent with a <110>twin//<011>fcc system. Twinning is initiated by the high tensile stress (~2.0 GPa) reaching the critical value (~1.5 GPa) for activating twinning—generally difficult in bulk fcc materials whose SFE (73.9 mJ·m⁻²) far exceeds the typical regime (<~50 mJ·m⁻²), especially under quasistatic loading. This is enabled by the high strength from interactions between anisotropic precipitates and the dense dislocation network, providing an additional plastic mechanism at later strain that further ductilizes and toughens the material. The high strength of A-MCA arises from: (1) anisotropic nanoprecipitates with small inter-precipitate spacing (26.1 nm), high volume fraction (38%), specific surface area (3.3 × 10⁸ m⁻¹) and lattice mismatch (1.3%)—their anisotropic morphology giving a larger specific surface area than spherical precipitates (1.9 × 10⁸ m⁻¹) at the same number density/volume fraction (cf. cuboidal-precipitate Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7 at 6.6 × 10⁷ m⁻¹); and (2) the high dislocation density (2.5 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²). Together these add ~1.2 GPa of strength.
断口附近的 TEM 揭示了多条平行的形变孪晶（数量密度 (4.2 ± 0.7) × 10¹² m⁻²，平均宽度 14.7 ± 4.7 nm）。原子分辨 STEM（图 3d）显示 fcc 基体内及基体–析出相界面处的孪生，FFT 与 <110>孪晶//<011>fcc 体系一致。孪生由约 2.0 GPa 的高拉应力达到激活孪生的临界值（约 1.5 GPa）而引发——这在层错能（73.9 mJ·m⁻²）远高于典型激活范围（< 约 50 mJ·m⁻²）的块体 fcc 材料中通常很难实现，尤其是在准静态加载下。之所以能实现，是因为各向异性析出相与密集位错网络的相互作用带来了高强度，使孪生应力得以达到，从而在后期应变提供了额外的塑性机制，进一步增塑增韧材料。A-MCA 的高强度来自：(1) 各向异性纳米析出相——小析出相间距（26.1 nm）、高体积分数（38%）、大比表面积（3.3 × 10⁸ m⁻¹）和晶格错配（1.3%）；在相同数量密度/体积分数下，其各向异性形貌带来的比表面积大于球形析出相（1.9 × 10⁸ m⁻¹）（对比立方析出相的 Fe32Co28Ni28Ta8Al7 为 6.6 × 10⁷ m⁻¹）；以及 (2) 高位错密度（2.5 × 10¹⁵ m⁻²）。二者共同贡献约 1.2 GPa 的强度增量。

磁性特征表征与模拟Magnetic feature characterization and simulation

We used correlative MOKE microscopy with EBSD (Fig. 4a, b) to characterize magnetic-domain/defect interactions. As the field varied from −80 to −40 kA·m⁻¹, magnetic domains nucleated preferentially at grain boundaries and deformation twins, which have higher elastic stress fields (~1°) than defect-free interiors (~0.2°). Coarse domains (25.3 ± 8.6 μm) grow unaffected in grain interiors, while significantly refined stripe-like domains (0.7 ± 0.1 μm) appear between deformation twins at 40 kA·m⁻¹. MFM (Fig. 4c) shows domain patterns near twin boundaries down to the nanoscale; AFM confirms the surface is smooth (<10 nm height difference), so the distinctive patterns arise mainly from cold-rolling-induced crystallographic misorientation. Fresnel imaging of a TEM lamella at remanence (Fig. 4d) shows domain walls that reform randomly after 1.5 T saturation, indicating an intrinsically soft matrix, with flux-closure 180° and 90° walls (Fig. 4e). The domain wall width (δw) is 105 ± 5 nm at a 180° wall (off-axis electron holography, Fig. 4f). Since conventional SMM δw ranges from 50 nm to a few μm, the precipitate dimensions (53 × 8 nm) are smaller than δw and do not act as strong pinning sites.
我们用相关性 MOKE 显微术配合 EBSD（图 4a、b）表征磁畴与缺陷的相互作用。当磁场从 −80 变到 −40 kA·m⁻¹ 时，磁畴优先在晶界和形变孪晶处形核——这些缺陷的弹性应力场（约 1°）高于无缺陷晶粒内部（约 0.2°）。粗大磁畴（25.3 ± 8.6 μm）在晶粒内部不受阻碍地长大，而在 40 kA·m⁻¹ 时形变孪晶之间出现显著细化的条带状磁畴（0.7 ± 0.1 μm）。MFM（图 4c）显示孪晶界附近直至纳米尺度的磁畴图案；AFM 证实表面平整（高差 <10 nm），因此这些独特图案主要源自冷轧诱导的晶体学取向差。剩磁态（图 4d）下 TEM 薄片的菲涅耳成像显示，畴壁在 1.5 T 饱和后随机重构，表明基体本征软磁，并呈现闭合磁通的 180° 和 90° 畴壁（图 4e）。180° 畴壁处的畴壁宽度（δw）为 105 ± 5 nm（离轴电子全息，图 4f）。由于传统软磁材料的 δw 在 50 nm 到数微米之间，析出相尺寸（53 × 8 nm）小于 δw，不会成为强钉扎位点。
Since lattice mismatch and interfacial elastic distortion can also locally pin domain walls, we ran 3D micromagnetic simulations combining APT/TEM microstructure with DFT magnetic parameters (Fig. 4f). This highlights the exchange interaction between the stronger ferromagnetic fcc matrix (Ms = 161.6 Am²·kg⁻¹) and weaker ferromagnetic precipitates (Ms = 38.7 Am²·kg⁻¹); the weak precipitate magnetism makes interphase-exchange pinning negligible, which is the determining effect providing high strength without sacrificing coercivity. Simulations show that introducing the weak ferromagnetic anisotropic precipitates decreases coercivity by 18% versus A-MCA, mainly due to the precipitates' decreased exchange stiffness, which lowers the external field needed for spin rotation. Thus size, morphology, structure and composition of the precipitates must be tuned carefully. Although the coercivity is slightly higher than conventional SMMs, the excellent mechanical properties make A-MCA suitable for severe-stress applications where robustness outweighs minimal coercivity—achieved by balancing the micromagnetically relevant length scales of the anisotropic precipitates (high strength) against releasing structural defects (lower coercivity).
由于晶格错配和界面弹性畸变也能局部钉扎畴壁，我们将 APT/TEM 组织数据与 DFT 磁参数结合，进行了三维微磁模拟（图 4f）。该模拟突出了较强铁磁性 fcc 基体（Ms = 161.6 Am²·kg⁻¹）与较弱铁磁性析出相（Ms = 38.7 Am²·kg⁻¹）之间的交换相互作用；析出相磁性较弱，使相间交换钉扎可忽略，这正是在不牺牲矫顽力的前提下提供高强度的决定性因素。模拟表明，引入弱铁磁性的各向异性析出相使矫顽力相比 A-MCA 降低 18%，主要归因于析出相交换刚度降低，从而降低了自旋转动所需的外场。因此，析出相的尺寸、形貌、结构和成分都必须精心调控。尽管矫顽力略高于传统软磁材料，但优异的力学性能使 A-MCA 适用于以稳健性优先于最小矫顽力的严酷应力场合——这通过在各向异性析出相的微磁学相关尺度（保证高强度）与释放结构缺陷（降低矫顽力）之间取得平衡来实现。

小结Summary

In summary, we designed a bulk multicomponent soft magnetic material with two-gigapascal strength, good ductility (12%), high saturation magnetization (115 Am²·kg⁻¹), relatively low coercivity (0.36 kA·m⁻¹) and high electrical resistivity (62.5 μΩ·cm). We realized this by engineering morphologically anisotropic, coherent nanoprecipitates with optimized spatial dimensions and patterns, chemical compositions, and coherency stresses. The magnetic properties and magnetization mechanism were revealed by correlative experimental and computational probes across a wide range of length scales. Multiple strengthening and toughening mechanisms are combined with soft magnetic features, realized so as not to interfere with domain wall motion, thus reconciling otherwise mutually exclusive design targets. The versatile, practically infinite compositional space of such MCAs and non-equilibrium nanostructuring concepts are critical for developing energy-efficient SMMs that endure severe mechanical loading without sacrificing low hysteresis loss. Although a high density and different types of lattice defects give a moderately higher coercivity, this concept enables highly efficient electro-mechanical energy conversion where mechanical robustness is prioritized—particularly for highly stressed magnetic parts under severe centrifugal loads in service or manufacturing, for which conventional SMMs are too brittle or too soft, such as electric-vehicle motors and high-speed flywheel energy storage.
综上所述，我们设计出一种块体多组元软磁材料，具有两吉帕强度、良好塑性（12%）、高饱和磁化强度（115 Am²·kg⁻¹）、相对较低的矫顽力（0.36 kA·m⁻¹）和高电阻率（62.5 μΩ·cm）。我们通过设计具有优化空间尺寸与排布、化学成分和共格应力的形貌各向异性、共格纳米析出相实现了这一概念。材料的磁性能及磁化机制通过跨越广泛尺度的相关性实验与计算探针得以揭示。多种强化和增韧机制与软磁特性相结合，并以不干扰畴壁运动的方式实现，从而调和了原本互相排斥的设计目标。此类 MCA 多变且几乎无限的成分空间，以及非平衡纳米结构化理念，对开发既能承受严酷机械载荷又不牺牲低磁滞损耗的高效软磁材料至关重要。尽管高密度、多种类的晶格缺陷带来了适度升高的矫顽力，但该理念为以机械稳健性优先的高效机电能量转换提供了途径——尤其适用于服役或制造中承受严酷离心载荷、高应力的磁性部件（传统软磁材料对此过脆或过软），如电动汽车电机和高速飞轮储能装置。

Nature Communications · 2025

利用热磁场对纳米片层磁体进行磁硬化与力学硬化Magnetic and mechanical hardening of nano-lamellar magnets using thermo-magnetic fields

Nat. Commun. (2025) 16:2423 · doi:10.1038/s41467-025-57571-6 · Liuliu Han, Jin Wang, Nicolas J. Peter, Fernando Maccari, András Kovács, Ruth Schwaiger, Oliver Gutfleisch, Dierk Raabe
本页为「摘要 + 正文（Results and discussion）」中文翻译；已省略 Methods（方法）、补充材料与参考文献。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
High-performance magnetic materials based on rare-earth intermetallic compounds are critical for energy conversion technologies. However, the high cost and supply risks of rare-earth elements necessitate the development of affordable alternatives. Another challenge lies in the inherent brittleness of current magnets, which limits their applications for high dynamic mechanical loading conditions during service and complex shape design during manufacturing towards high efficiency and sustainability. Here, we propose a strategy to simultaneously enhance the magnetic and mechanical performance of a rare-earth-free multicomponent magnet. We achieve this by introducing nano-lamellar structures with high shape anisotropy into a cobalt–iron–nickel–aluminum material system through eutectoid decomposition under externally applied thermo-magnetic fields. Compared to the conventional thermally activated processing, the thermo-magnetic field accelerates phase decomposition kinetics, producing finer lamellae spacings and smaller eutectoid colonies. The well-tailored size, density, interface, and chemistry of the nano-lamellae enhance their pinning effect against the motion of both magnetic domain walls and dislocations, resulting in concurrent gains in coercivity and mechanical strength. Our work demonstrates a rational pathway to designing multifunctional rare-earth-free magnets for energy conversion devices such as high-speed motors and generators operating under harsh service conditions.
基于稀土金属间化合物的高性能磁性材料对能量转换技术至关重要。然而，稀土元素成本高、供应有风险，亟需开发经济的替代方案。另一个挑战在于现有磁体的固有脆性，这限制了它们在服役中承受高动态机械载荷、以及在制造中实现复杂形状设计以追求高效与可持续方面的应用。本文提出一种同时提升无稀土多组元磁体磁性能与力学性能的策略。我们在钴–铁–镍–铝材料体系中，通过在外加热磁场下进行共析分解，引入高形状各向异性的纳米片层结构。与传统的热激活工艺相比，热磁场加速了相分解动力学，产生更细的片层间距和更小的共析团（colony）。经精心调控的纳米片层——其尺寸、密度、界面和化学成分——增强了对磁畴壁和位错运动的钉扎作用，从而同时提升了矫顽力和力学强度。我们的工作展示了一条设计多功能无稀土磁体的合理路径，可用于在严酷服役条件下运行的高速电机和发电机等能量转换装置。
Magnetic materials are key components for the green energy transition due to their role in sustainable wind energy conversion, electro-mobility, automation, and robotics, and provide stable high torque in motor applications. They can be classified based on their coercivity and energy products into soft, semihard, and hard magnetic materials. The critical rare earth (RE) elements are generally required in magnetically hard materials to provide strong spin-orbit coupling for achieving high magnetocrystalline anisotropy, which is the basis for large magnetic hysteresis. Alternative methods for enhancing anisotropy include introducing microstructural features with high shape anisotropy, stress anisotropy, chemical anisotropy, and other ways to enhance magnetocrystalline anisotropy. In addition, extensive efforts have been made to mitigate the ever-increasing requirement for applications under harsh environments, including high temperature, corrosion, hydrogen embrittlement, and mechanical loading conditions for sustainable electrification.
磁性材料是绿色能源转型的关键部件，因其在可持续风能转换、电动出行、自动化和机器人中的作用，并在电机应用中提供稳定的高扭矩。依据矫顽力和磁能积，磁性材料可分为软磁、半硬磁和硬磁材料。硬磁材料通常需要关键稀土（RE）元素来提供强自旋–轨道耦合，以获得高磁晶各向异性，而后者是大磁滞的基础。增强各向异性的替代方法包括引入具有高形状各向异性、应力各向异性、化学各向异性的组织特征，以及其他增强磁晶各向异性的途径。此外，人们还投入大量努力，以满足在高温、腐蚀、氢脆和机械载荷等严酷环境下、面向可持续电气化不断增长的应用要求。
For example, hard magnets with good mechanical properties are required in advanced flywheel energy storage systems that target an ultra-fast rotating speed of over 50,000 revolutions per minute for high-efficiency and sustainable electrification. This is because the rotation generates high dynamic mechanical and cyclic stress, and almost all conventional hard magnets fail at such speeds, thus limiting the efficiency of the whole system. However, most commercial hard magnetic materials are based on intermetallic compounds and are thus inherently brittle, rendering them incapable of bearing plastic deformation during processing and service. Therefore, the strongest hard magnets—e.g., Nd–Fe–B and Sm–Co alloys—are generally synthesized by powder metallurgy, which consists of a series of energy- and time-consuming and health-risky steps (metal powder production, mixing, compacting and sintering), as compared to conventional casting and forging. Although near-net-shape manufacturing can reduce machining and material loss, high-performance magnetic materials made by adjustable processing that can fill the cost and performance gap of current systems are needed for efficient and sustainable electrification. Current manufacturing methods still face challenges in minimizing material waste and ensuring product quality, because magnet properties are highly dependent on microstructure, which in turn depends on the manufacturing process. The inherent brittleness of intermetallic compounds is prone to forming microcracks during rapid cooling, increasing the risk of fracture during machining—for instance, Sm–Co-based alloys have a machining failure of ~20% because of their low fracture toughness (1.9–2.9 MPa·m^1/2). This design challenge is characterized by the need for a multifunctional profile: high remanence and coercivity; good mechanical performance (high hardness, yield strength, ductility, damage tolerance); and good oxidation/corrosion resistance under harsh chemo-mechanical operating conditions.
例如，先进飞轮储能系统为追求高效与可持续电气化，目标转速超过每分钟 50,000 转，这就需要力学性能良好的硬磁体。因为高速旋转产生高动态机械应力和循环应力，几乎所有传统硬磁体在此转速下都会失效，从而限制整个系统的效率。然而，大多数商用硬磁材料基于金属间化合物，因而固有脆性，无法在加工和服役中承受塑性变形。因此，最强的硬磁体——如 Nd–Fe–B 和 Sm–Co 合金——通常采用粉末冶金制备，相较传统铸造和锻造，它包含一系列耗能、耗时且有健康风险的工序（金属粉末制备、混料、压制和烧结）。尽管近净成形制造能减少机加工和材料损耗，但为实现高效、可持续电气化，仍需要通过可调工艺制造、能填补现有体系成本与性能差距的高性能磁性材料。当前制造方法在最小化材料浪费和保证产品质量方面仍面临挑战，因为磁体性能高度依赖组织，而组织又依赖制造工艺。金属间化合物的固有脆性在快速冷却时易形成微裂纹，增大后续机加工中的断裂风险——例如 Sm–Co 基合金由于断裂韧性低（1.9–2.9 MPa·m^1/2），机加工失效率约达 20%。这一设计挑战的特征是需要多功能组合：高剩磁与高矫顽力；良好的力学性能（高硬度、屈服强度、塑性、损伤容限）；以及在严酷化学–机械工况下良好的抗氧化/耐腐蚀性。
To overcome these challenges, multicomponent alloys (MCAs), which provide a wide range of chemical compositions and microstructures and thus a certain potential for realizing multifunctionality, have been proposed. MCAs can reconcile different magnetic and mechanical features in one material because continuous chemical adjustment allows tuning within a continuous phase space into regimes where magnetic properties (Curie temperature, coercivity, saturation magnetization) and mechanical properties (hardness, yield strength) can be simultaneously enhanced. In addition, the option to chemically decorate and functionalize microstructural defects at different length scales (dislocations, interfaces, precipitates) can be exploited to pin the movement of magnetic domain walls during magnetization and demagnetization, leading to a larger magnetic hysteresis integral. The enhanced internal stress level from introducing defects also increases mechanical strength by impeding dislocation movement. We translated these mutually conflicting properties into a design strategy by triggering ferromagnetic nano-lamellae with high shape anisotropy via thermo-magnetic annealing. We selected a Co28.6Ni28.8Fe30.6Al12.0 (at.%) MCA system: the ferromagnetic 3d elements (Co, Ni, Fe) blended near-equiatomically form a high-symmetry fcc solid-solution matrix (enabling ductile plasticity and high spontaneous magnetization), while Al (large atom size, negative mixing enthalpy with Co/Ni/Fe) triggers precipitates that enhance coercivity and mechanical strength, enabling hierarchical precipitation reactions coupled with tunable magnetic properties.
为克服这些挑战，人们提出了多组元合金（MCA）——它提供宽广的化学成分和组织范围，因而具备实现多功能性的一定潜力。MCA 能在同一材料中调和不同的磁性与力学特征，因为连续的成分调节可在连续相空间内调控到某些区间，使磁性能（居里温度、矫顽力、饱和磁化强度）和力学性能（硬度、屈服强度）同时得到提升。此外，还可利用在不同尺度上对组织缺陷（位错、界面、析出相）进行化学修饰与功能化的可能性，在磁化和退磁过程中钉扎磁畴壁的移动，从而获得更大的磁滞积分。引入缺陷带来的内应力提升，也通过阻碍位错运动提高力学强度。我们将这些互相冲突的性能转化为一种设计策略：通过热磁退火触发高形状各向异性的铁磁性纳米片层。我们选取 Co28.6Ni28.8Fe30.6Al12.0（原子分数）MCA 体系：铁磁性 3d 元素（Co、Ni、Fe）以近等原子比混合，形成高对称的 fcc 固溶体基体（可提供韧性塑性和高自发磁化），而 Al（原子尺寸大、与 Co/Ni/Fe 有负混合焓）触发析出相，增强矫顽力和力学强度，从而实现与可调磁性能相耦合的分级析出反应。

合金设计与组织分析Alloy design and microstructure analysis

First, we performed thermodynamic simulations to identify a phase with high magnetization and high anisotropy in the Co28.6Ni28.8Fe30.6Al12.0 (at.%) system. The results (Supplementary Fig. 1) show a two-phase fcc + bcc regime from 923 to 1623 K; the fcc phase further decomposes via a eutectoid transformation below 923 K, with the potential to form a lamellar structure with high aspect ratio and shape anisotropy. We synthesized the material by vacuum induction melting and conducted a two-stage isothermal heat treatment: homogenization at 1473 K for 2 h to obtain ~85 vol.% fcc, then thermal annealing (TA) at 873 K targeting a eutectoid lamellar microstructure for both mechanical and magnetic hardening (the lamellae and their high interfacial density enhance pinning of both domain walls and dislocations). Considering the external field's effect on decomposition kinetics, variants were processed by thermo-magnetic annealing (TMA) under a 9 T field at 873 K for 1–10 h (Fig. 1a). The external field aligns all magnetic moments in the ferromagnetic phases during annealing, enabling tailoring of crystallinity, texture, and chemistry toward high magnetization, high interface density, small lamellae spacing, and small colony size.
首先，我们进行热力学模拟，以在 Co28.6Ni28.8Fe30.6Al12.0（原子分数）体系中确定一种兼具高磁化强度和高各向异性的相。结果（补充图 1）显示，在 923 至 1623 K 存在 fcc + bcc 两相区；fcc 相在 923 K 以下通过共析转变进一步分解，有望形成高长径比、高形状各向异性的片层结构。我们采用真空感应熔炼制备材料，并进行两阶段等温热处理：先在 1473 K 均匀化 2 h 获得约 85 vol.% 的 fcc，再在 873 K 进行热退火（TA），目标是获得可同时实现力学与磁硬化的共析片层组织（片层及其高界面密度增强对畴壁和位错的钉扎）。考虑外加磁场对分解动力学的影响，各变体在 9 T 磁场、873 K 下进行热磁退火（TMA）1–10 h（图 1a）。退火时外加磁场使铁磁相中所有磁矩取向一致，从而可将结晶度、织构和成分调控到高磁化强度、高界面密度、小片层间距和小团尺寸的方向。
Ex-situ EBSD and ECCI analyses reveal eutectoid decomposition during TMA (Fig. 1b, c). The decomposition area gradually increased with TMA time until the remaining fcc phase was fully consumed after 5 h. The decomposition kinetics under TA are slower than under TMA (6 h to fully consume the fcc phase), also indicated by the slower migration of the decomposition interface. In-situ STEM at 873 K (Fig. 1d) with HAADF imaging revealed the formation and growth of nano-lamellae during TA. Figure 2a shows typical hysteresis loops for different treatments; the evolution of intrinsic coercivity (Hc) and saturation magnetization (Ms) with annealing time is in Fig. 2b. After 5 h of TMA (TMA5), Hc increased nearly 40-fold (~3930%) versus the homogenized H-MCA—from 0.7 to 27.5 kA·m⁻¹—which is twice the highest reported Hc (12.7 kA·m⁻¹) in the Co–Fe–Ni–Al system. Ms increased from 110.5 (H-MCA) to 123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹ (TMA5) then decreased to 118.4 (TMA10). TA-processed magnets follow similar trends but with lower peak Hc/Ms reached later (19.9 kA·m⁻¹ and 120.2 Am²·kg⁻¹ after 6 h).
离位 EBSD 和 ECCI 分析揭示了 TMA 过程中的共析分解（图 1b、c）。分解区域随 TMA 时间逐渐扩大，直至 5 h 后剩余 fcc 相被完全消耗。TA 下的分解动力学比 TMA 慢（需 6 h 才能完全消耗 fcc 相），分解界面迁移较慢也印证了这一点。873 K 下的原位 STEM（图 1d）配合 HAADF 成像，揭示了 TA 过程中纳米片层的形成与长大。图 2a 给出不同处理的典型磁滞回线；本征矫顽力（Hc）和饱和磁化强度（Ms）随退火时间的演化见图 2b。经 5 h TMA（TMA5）后，Hc 相比均匀化态 H-MCA 提高近 40 倍（约 3930%）——从 0.7 升至 27.5 kA·m⁻¹——是 Co–Fe–Ni–Al 体系已报道最高 Hc（12.7 kA·m⁻¹）的两倍。Ms 从 110.5（H-MCA）升至 123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹（TMA5），随后降至 118.4（TMA10）。TA 处理的磁体呈相似趋势，但峰值 Hc/Ms 较低且到达更晚（6 h 后为 19.9 kA·m⁻¹ 和 120.2 Am²·kg⁻¹）。
An Ashby-type plot (Fig. 2c) comparing Ms vs. Hc with commercial magnets shows that introducing and tailoring nano-lamellae by TMA tunes the Co–Fe–Ni–Al MCAs from a soft (H-MCA) to a semihard (TMA5) magnetic state while increasing Ms. The TMA5 magnet's high Hc outperforms all Fe–Co–Ni alloys studied thus far and is comparable to Fe–Co–V–Cr alloys, which have a lower fraction of the critical elements Co and Ni and thus lower cost—yet Fe–Co–V–Cr alloys generally contain 49–54 wt.% Co (Hc ~28 kA/m) whereas TMA5 contains only ~32 wt.% Co with comparable Hc, and TMA5's Ms (123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹) is much higher than that of Fe–Co–V–Cr alloys (<100 Am²·kg⁻¹). To understand the good combination of high Hc and Ms in TMA5, we studied its microstructure and chemistry from the microscale to the near-atomic scale (Fig. 3). High-resolution ECC imaging and transmission Kikuchi diffraction show the decomposition region consists of fine eutectoid colonies with an average size of 4.5 ± 3.4 μm. HAADF-STEM (Fig. 3a) shows alternating dark and bright lamellae with average spacings of 64.7 ± 22.9 nm and 182.2 ± 14.6 nm ("fine" and "coarse" layers); network structures with much finer widths (9.1 ± 2.3 nm) are distributed in the coarse layer.
将 Ms 与 Hc 同商用磁体对比的 Ashby 型图（图 2c）显示，通过 TMA 引入并调控纳米片层，可将 Co–Fe–Ni–Al MCA 从软磁态（H-MCA）调控到半硬磁态（TMA5），同时提升 Ms。TMA5 磁体的高 Hc 超过迄今研究的所有 Fe–Co–Ni 合金，并与 Fe–Co–V–Cr 合金相当——后者关键元素 Co、Ni 含量更低、成本更低；然而 Fe–Co–V–Cr 合金通常含 49–54 wt.% Co（Hc 约 28 kA/m），而 TMA5 仅含约 32 wt.% Co 却有相当的 Hc，且 TMA5 的 Ms（123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹）远高于 Fe–Co–V–Cr 合金（<100 Am²·kg⁻¹）。为理解 TMA5 中高 Hc 与高 Ms 的良好组合，我们从微观尺度到近原子尺度研究了其组织与化学（图 3）。高分辨 ECC 成像和透射菊池衍射显示，分解区由细小的共析团组成，平均尺寸 4.5 ± 3.4 μm。HAADF-STEM（图 3a）显示交替的暗、亮片层，平均间距分别为 64.7 ± 22.9 nm 和 182.2 ± 14.6 nm（即"细"层与"粗"层）；粗层中还分布着宽度更细（9.1 ± 2.3 nm）的网状结构。
Atomically resolved HAADF-STEM and FFT patterns identify both the network structure and the surrounding coarse layer as ordered B2 structures (Fig. 3b); the fine layer is a disordered fcc structure with a uniform distribution of coherently ordered L12 (Fig. 3c). The hierarchical structure from solid-state decomposition results from competition between severe lattice distortion and chemical ordering tendencies. APT (Fig. 3d–g) reveals the near-atomic chemistry: the network/coarse B2 layer is enriched in Ni and Al, while Fe and Co partition into the coarse layer (Co47.2Fe43.6Ni4.6Al4.5, at.%). The fcc and L12 phases were determined to be Ni33.0Co31.9Fe28.8Al6.3 and Ni65.7Al20.0Co7.3Fe7.0 (at.%), respectively.
原子分辨 HAADF-STEM 与 FFT 花样将网状结构及其周围的粗层都判定为有序 B2 结构（图 3b）；细层则为无序 fcc 结构，其中均匀分布着共格有序的 L12（图 3c）。这种由固态分解形成的分级结构，源自严重晶格畸变与化学有序化倾向之间的竞争。APT（图 3d–g）揭示了近原子尺度的化学：网状/粗层 B2 富集 Ni 和 Al，而 Fe、Co 分配进入粗层（Co47.2Fe43.6Ni4.6Al4.5，原子分数）。fcc 相与 L12 相的成分分别确定为 Ni33.0Co31.9Fe28.8Al6.3 和 Ni65.7Al20.0Co7.3Fe7.0（原子分数）。

磁结构表征Magnetic structure characterization

We used MOKE microscopy to characterize the interaction between magnetic domains and nano-lamellae during magnetization/demagnetization (Fig. 4a). Magnetizing from a demagnetized state to 300 mT and then to −200 mT revealed two domain sizes: coarse domains (average width 3.6 ± 1.5 μm) nucleate and grow inside the micron-sized bcc phase (formed during homogenization, ~15 vol.%), while fine domains, uniformly distributed in the eutectoid region, have widths below optical resolution. Domains grow unaffected in the bcc phase but are impeded at phase boundaries. Correlative MFM–AFM (Fig. 4b, c) shows a maze pattern in the bcc phase (anisotropy perpendicular to the surface) and strong magnetic contrast between fine (bright) and coarse (dark) layers; AFM confirms a smooth surface (max height difference 9 nm), so the distinctive domain patterns arise mainly from the difference in magnetization between alternating layers. Lorentz TEM at remanence (Fig. 4d, e) outlines domain walls; extrapolating defocus series gives an average domain wall width in the bcc phase of 43 ± 6 nm (typical for soft magnets), while the enlarged MFM image shows the alternating eutectoid layers act as pinning sites against domain wall motion.
我们用 MOKE 显微术表征磁化/退磁过程中磁畴与纳米片层的相互作用（图 4a）。从退磁态磁化到 300 mT 再到 −200 mT，观察到两种畴尺寸：粗畴（平均宽度 3.6 ± 1.5 μm）在微米级 bcc 相内部形核并长大（该相在均匀化时形成，约 15 vol.%），而在共析区均匀分布的细畴宽度低于光学分辨率。磁畴在 bcc 相内不受阻碍地长大，但在相界处受阻。相关性 MFM–AFM（图 4b、c）显示 bcc 相中呈迷宫状图案（各向异性垂直于样品表面），细层（亮）与粗层（暗）之间磁衬度强烈；AFM 证实表面平整（最大高差 9 nm），因此这些独特的畴图案主要源自交替层之间磁化强度的差异。剩磁态下的洛伦兹 TEM（图 4d、e）勾勒出畴壁；对离焦系列外推得到 bcc 相中平均畴壁宽度为 43 ± 6 nm（软磁材料的典型值），而放大的 MFM 图像显示交替的共析层充当了阻碍畴壁运动的钉扎位点。
Since coercivity is an extrinsic property largely determined by domain-wall/microstructure interactions, it strongly depends on the distribution of lattice defects and their pinning. The significant Hc increase of TMA5 during TMA is mainly attributed to strong pinning by the introduced defects—specifically eutectoid colonies with a small average size (4.5 ± 3.4 μm) and nano-lamellae with a fine spacing (123.5 ± 18.7 nm). The Hc increase until completion of eutectoid decomposition (5 h for TMA) correlates with the gradually increasing volume fraction of nano-lamellae and their interfacial density. Further annealing decreases coercivity due to coarsening of colony size and lamellae spacing (12.9 ± 10.1 μm and 192.6 ± 36.6 nm for TMA10), driven by a decrease in total interface energy. Future work could target variants with enhanced magnetic performance while preserving mechanical performance and lowering cost, by tailoring the chemistry, dimension, interlamellar spacing, and number density of the nano-lamellae—e.g., increasing pre-existing heterogeneous nucleation sites (grain boundaries, dislocations) via additive manufacturing to refine colony size and spacing, or promoting stronger grain texture and well-aligned lamellae via thermo-mechanical treatment (plastic forming, cold drawing, directional solidification) for enhanced remanence.
由于矫顽力是外禀性质，主要由畴壁与组织的相互作用决定，它强烈依赖于晶格缺陷的分布及其钉扎作用。TMA5 在 TMA 过程中 Hc 的显著增大，主要归因于所引入缺陷的强钉扎——具体为平均尺寸小的共析团（4.5 ± 3.4 μm）和片层间距细的纳米片层（123.5 ± 18.7 nm）。直到共析分解完成（TMA 为 5 h）之前 Hc 的增大，与纳米片层体积分数及其界面密度的逐渐增加相关。进一步退火则因团尺寸和片层间距的粗化（TMA10 为 12.9 ± 10.1 μm 和 192.6 ± 36.6 nm）而使矫顽力下降，其驱动力为总界面能的降低。未来工作可致力于在保持力学性能、降低成本的同时提升磁性能的变体——通过调控纳米片层的化学成分、尺寸、层间距和数量密度实现，例如借助增材制造增加预存的非均匀形核位点（晶界、位错）以细化团尺寸和间距，或通过热机械处理（塑性成形、冷拔、定向凝固）促进更强的晶粒织构和取向良好的片层以提升剩磁。
We studied the intrinsic magnetic properties by in-situ magnetization–temperature measurements. On heating from 300 to 1000 K at 20 K/min, the magnetization of H-MCA drops sharply to only 3.5% of its room-temperature value at 750 K, whereas TMA5 retains 37.5% at 1000 K. Slope analysis indicates H-MCA contains a ferromagnetic phase with Tc = 688 K, while TMA5 contains two ferromagnetic phases with higher Curie temperatures (Ni33.0Co31.9Fe28.8Al6.3, Tc ~ 740 K; Co47.2Fe43.6Ni4.6Al4.5, Tc > 1000 K). By casting these phases into individual ingots using APT-derived nominal compositions, we found that the enhanced Ms of TMA5 is mainly due to a ferromagnetic Co–Fe-enriched B2 phase with high magnetization (total magnetic moment 4.06 μB/f.u. by DFT), compared with the fcc phase in H-MCA before annealing (1.40 μB/f.u.).
我们通过原位磁化–温度测量研究本征磁性能。以 20 K/min 从 300 K 升温到 1000 K 时，H-MCA 的磁化强度在 750 K 急剧下降到仅为室温值的 3.5%，而 TMA5 在 1000 K 仍保留 37.5%。斜率分析表明，H-MCA 含有一个 Tc = 688 K 的铁磁相，而 TMA5 含有两个居里温度更高的铁磁相（Ni33.0Co31.9Fe28.8Al6.3，Tc 约 740 K；Co47.2Fe43.6Ni4.6Al4.5，Tc > 1000 K）。通过用 APT 得到的名义成分将这些相铸成单独的铸锭，我们发现 TMA5 增强的 Ms 主要源自一个高磁化的铁磁性、富 Co–Fe 的 B2 相（DFT 计算总磁矩 4.06 μB/f.u.），相比之下退火前 H-MCA 中的 fcc 相仅为 1.40 μB/f.u.。

力学性能Mechanical performance

We applied high-throughput nanoindentation to statistically analyze the hardness of the material after 3 h of TMA (TMA3), whose microstructures comprise micron-sized bcc, non-decomposed coarse-grained fcc, and eutectoid nano-lamellae. An array of 150 × 150 indents over 120 × 120 μm (100 nm depth, 800 nm spacing) was made at room temperature. An EBSD phase map (Fig. 5a) identified fcc and bcc; ECCI (Fig. 5b) contained 22,500 indents, each assigned to its microstructure (nano-lamellae treated as one phase; indents across microstructures termed "overlapping"). The reconstructed array (Fig. 5d) and hardness map (Fig. 5e) match the EBSD/ECCI results. Hardness–modulus analysis (Fig. 5f) revealed four clusters; the nano-lamellae and micron-sized bcc are much harder/stiffer than the undecomposed coarse-grain fcc. Median hardness values are 9.27 (nano-lamellae), 9.52 (micron bcc), and 6.06 GPa (coarse fcc); the overlapping region scatters from ~3 to 12 GPa. The high measured hardness reflects the indentation size effect (depth <100 nm); deeper indents (200 mN) still gave high hardness for the major phases, with dense <110>fcc slip lines forming in the coarse-grained fcc around the indents that stopped at fcc/bcc or fcc/nano-lamellae boundaries. Compressive tests show H-MCA and TA-5h can be plastically compressed up to 50%; the TA-5h alloy with eutectoid lamellae shows enhanced yield strength and strain hardening versus H-MCA, indicating that introducing nano-lamellae via eutectoid decomposition significantly hardens the magnetic material.
我们采用高通量纳米压痕，对经 3 h TMA（TMA3）后材料的硬度进行统计分析，其组织包含微米级 bcc、未分解的粗晶 fcc 以及共析纳米片层。在室温下于 120 × 120 μm 区域内做了 150 × 150 阵列压痕（深 100 nm、间距 800 nm）。EBSD 相图（图 5a）识别出 fcc 和 bcc；ECCI（图 5b）含 22,500 个压痕，每个压痕都归属到相应组织（纳米片层视为一相；跨组织的压痕称为"重叠"区）。重构阵列（图 5d）和硬度图（图 5e）与 EBSD/ECCI 结果吻合。硬度–模量分析（图 5f）揭示出四个聚类；纳米片层和微米级 bcc 远比未分解的粗晶 fcc 更硬、更刚。中位硬度分别为 9.27（纳米片层）、9.52（微米 bcc）和 6.06 GPa（粗晶 fcc）；重叠区在约 3 到 12 GPa 之间散布。所测得的高硬度反映了压痕尺寸效应（深度 <100 nm）；更深的压痕（200 mN）下主要相仍给出高硬度，压痕周围的粗晶 fcc 中形成了密集的 <110>fcc 滑移线，并终止于 fcc/bcc 或 fcc/纳米片层界面处。压缩试验表明 H-MCA 和 TA-5h 均可塑性压缩至 50%；含共析片层的 TA-5h 合金相比 H-MCA 表现出更高的屈服强度和应变硬化，说明通过共析分解引入纳米片层能显著硬化磁性材料。
These results show that introducing high-shape-anisotropy nano-lamellae via eutectoid decomposition also mechanically hardens the material. Nano-lamellae are unusual features of advanced magnets but provide an important additional benefit for mechanically loaded applications. Their size, density, interface, and chemistry—tailored by an external thermo-magnetic field—must be carefully controlled to increase structural defects to a level that enhances pinning of both magnetic domain walls and dislocations, while avoiding mechanical weakness and strain localization that would prevent the material from bearing plastic deformation during processing and use.
这些结果表明，通过共析分解引入高形状各向异性的纳米片层，同时也能对材料进行力学硬化。纳米片层是先进磁体中不常见的特征，但为承受机械载荷的应用提供了重要的额外益处。它们由外加热磁场调控的尺寸、密度、界面和化学成分必须精心控制，把结构缺陷增加到能增强对磁畴壁和位错双重钉扎的水平，同时避免造成力学薄弱和应变局部化，以免材料在加工和使用中无法承受塑性变形。

小结Summary

In summary, we developed a multicomponent magnet with magnetic properties (coercivity ~27.5 kA·m⁻¹, saturation magnetization ~123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹) matching those of commercial rare-earth-free hard magnets (e.g., Fe–Co–V–Cr alloys) and good mechanical performance in terms of high hardness. We realized this in a Co–Fe–Ni–Al system by introducing ferromagnetic nano-lamellae with high shape anisotropy via eutectoid decomposition under an external thermo-magnetic field. The additional field significantly accelerates decomposition kinetics and increases the effective pinning strength by reducing colony size and lamellae spacing. The nano-lamellae, with high average total magnetic moments, enhance saturation magnetization and act as barriers against the motion of both magnetic domain walls and dislocations, thus simultaneously enhancing coercivity and mechanical hardness. This strategy lays the groundwork for mechanically strong, magnetically hard multicomponent magnets for harsh service conditions, and is applicable to other magnetic families where external energy fields or pre-existing defects can modify microstructure for better multifunctionality. Future efforts could target higher coercivity and remanence while preserving mechanical properties and reducing dependence on critical elements for lower cost; secondary synthesis using scraps or waste with higher impurity content but lower cost could also serve as raw materials, with the impurity effects worth further investigation.
综上所述，我们研制出一种多组元磁体，其磁性能（矫顽力约 27.5 kA·m⁻¹，饱和磁化强度约 123.8 Am²·kg⁻¹）可与商用无稀土硬磁体（如 Fe–Co–V–Cr 合金）媲美，并在高硬度方面具有良好力学性能。我们在 Co–Fe–Ni–Al 体系中，通过外加热磁场下的共析分解引入高形状各向异性的铁磁性纳米片层实现了这一目标。附加磁场显著加速分解动力学，并通过减小团尺寸和片层间距提高有效钉扎强度。具有高平均总磁矩的纳米片层既提升饱和磁化强度，又充当阻碍磁畴壁和位错运动的障碍，从而同时增强矫顽力和力学硬度。该策略为开发面向严酷服役条件、力学强且磁性硬的多组元磁体奠定了基础，并可推广到其他磁体族——只要外加能量场或预存缺陷能修饰组织以获得更好的多功能性。未来工作可在保持力学性能、降低对关键元素依赖以降低成本的同时，追求更高的矫顽力和剩磁；采用杂质含量较高但成本更低的废料进行二次合成也可作为原料，其杂质影响值得进一步研究。

Acta Materialia · 2024

开发含共格体心立方纳米析出相的新型高温软磁 B2 基多主元合金Developing novel high-temperature soft-magnetic B2-based multi-principal-element alloys with coherent body-centered-cubic nanoprecipitates

Acta Materialia 266 (2024) 119686 · doi:10.1016/j.actamat.2024.119686 · Zhenhua Wang, Junhao Yuan, Qing Wang, Zhen Li, Xuyang Zhou, Junhua Luan, Jing Wang, Shijian Zheng, Zengbao Jiao, Chuang Dong, Peter K. Liaw
本页为「摘要 + 正文」中文翻译（含引言、实验、结果、讨论、结论各节，部分段落合并精炼但保留全部关键数据）；已省略参考文献与致谢。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Multi-principal-element alloys (MPEAs) have attracted considerable attention due to their enhanced possibilities of obtaining superior properties by tailoring chemical compositions in an enormous space. This work developed a series of novel soft-magnetic MPEAs via the cluster formula approach of Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14. Through deliberately manipulating their microstructures, ultrafine ferromagnetic body-centered-cubic (BCC) nanoparticles (3 ~ 8 nm in diameter) are coherently precipitated in a B2 matrix. These alloys exhibit a high saturation magnetization of 107.4 ~ 167.5 Am²/kg and a low coercivity of 143 ~ 303 A/m in the as-homogenized and aged states. Even after aging for 480 h at 873 ~ 1073 K, the prominent soft-magnetic properties can still be retained, which can be ascribed to the excellent stability of the coherent BCC/B2 microstructure. Importantly, these materials also show excellent soft-magnetic properties at high temperatures. The Al3Co7Fe7 alloy exhibits a saturation magnetization of 134.7 Am²/kg and a coercivity of 167.2 A/m at 973 K. Moreover, they have high Curie temperatures (1254 K for Al3Co7Fe7 and 1052 K for Al3Co6Fe6Cr2) and electrical resistivity (262 ~ 285 μΩ·cm). The outstanding high-temperature magnetic properties of the presently developed alloys is discussed in light of the microstructural stability and evolution with chemical composition and temperature, and the coercivity is found to be closely related to the particle size of BCC nanoprecipitates. With the advantages of the currently developed BCC/B2 MPEAs over conventional soft-magnetic alloys, the coherent precipitation approach opens a new way to design novel high-temperature soft-magnetic materials.
多主元合金（MPEA）因其可在巨大的成分空间中通过调控化学成分来获得优异性能，而受到广泛关注。本工作通过团簇式（cluster formula）方法 Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14 开发了一系列新型软磁多主元合金。通过有意调控其组织，在 B2 基体中共格析出了超细的铁磁性体心立方（BCC）纳米颗粒（直径 3~8 nm）。在均匀化态和时效态下，这些合金表现出高饱和磁化强度（107.4~167.5 Am²/kg）和低矫顽力（143~303 A/m）。即使在 873~1073 K 时效 480 h 后，仍能保持突出的软磁性能，这归因于共格 BCC/B2 组织的优异稳定性。重要的是，这些材料在高温下也表现出优异的软磁性能。Al3Co7Fe7 合金在 973 K 时饱和磁化强度为 134.7 Am²/kg、矫顽力为 167.2 A/m。此外，它们具有高居里温度（Al3Co7Fe7 为 1254 K，Al3Co6Fe6Cr2 为 1052 K）和高电阻率（262~285 μΩ·cm）。本文结合组织随化学成分和温度的稳定性与演化，讨论了所开发合金优异的高温磁性能，并发现矫顽力与 BCC 纳米析出相的颗粒尺寸密切相关。凭借所开发的 BCC/B2 多主元合金相对传统软磁合金的优势，这种共格析出方法为设计新型高温软磁材料开辟了新途径。

1. 引言1. Introduction

Soft-magnetic materials with high saturation magnetization (or saturation induction intension) and permeability as well as low coercivity have been widely applied in power generation, electronic, and aerospace fields. Commercial soft-magnetic alloys, such as Fe–(3~4.5)Si and Fe–(30~50)Co (wt.%) alloys, are prevalent among industries due to their high saturation magnetization (Bs = 1.9 ~ 2.4 T) and low coercivity (Hc < 150 A/m). Nevertheless, their low electrical resistivity (ρ < 50 μΩ·cm) leads to a high eddy current loss, limiting their applications in high-frequency fields. Fe- and Co-based amorphous/nanocrystalline alloys have high electrical resistivity of 100 ~ 150 μΩ·cm due to their random atomic packing in a long range. Meanwhile, this kind of alloys possess high Bs (1.2 ~ 2.0 T) and low Hc (< 10 A/m), which is ascribed mainly to the uniform distribution of ultrafine ferromagnetic body-centered-cubic (BCC) nanoprecipitates (less than 20 nm) in the amorphous matrix, as exemplified by the Fe-based Finemet Fe73.5Si13.5Nb3B9Cu1 alloy. However, the metastable nature of the amorphous matrix limits their high-temperature applications to below 573 K (Curie temperature Tc ~ 850 K). The partial substitution of Co for Fe can increase the Bs, Hc, and Tc, as evidenced by the Hitperm Fe44Co44Zr7B4Cu1 (Bs = 2.0 T, Hc = 160 A/m, Tc = 1253 K). Moreover, this series of alloys have the largest resistivity (ρ ~ 150 μΩ·cm) among all existing conventional soft-magnetic alloys.
具有高饱和磁化强度（或饱和磁感应强度）和高磁导率、以及低矫顽力的软磁材料，已广泛应用于发电、电子和航空航天领域。商用软磁合金，如 Fe–(3~4.5)Si 和 Fe–(30~50)Co（wt.%）合金，因其高饱和磁化强度（Bs = 1.9~2.4 T）和低矫顽力（Hc < 150 A/m）而在工业界广泛使用。然而，它们较低的电阻率（ρ < 50 μΩ·cm）导致高涡流损耗，限制了在高频领域的应用。Fe 基和 Co 基非晶/纳米晶合金因其长程随机原子堆积而具有 100~150 μΩ·cm 的高电阻率。同时，这类合金还具有高 Bs（1.2~2.0 T）和低 Hc（< 10 A/m），这主要归因于非晶基体中均匀分布的超细铁磁性体心立方（BCC）纳米析出相（小于 20 nm），以 Fe 基 Finemet 合金 Fe73.5Si13.5Nb3B9Cu1 为代表。然而，非晶基体的亚稳性质将其高温应用限制在 573 K 以下（居里温度 Tc ~ 850 K）。以 Co 部分替代 Fe 可提高 Bs、Hc 和 Tc，Hitperm 合金 Fe44Co44Zr7B4Cu1 即为例证（Bs = 2.0 T，Hc = 160 A/m，Tc = 1253 K）。此外，这一系列合金在所有现有传统软磁合金中电阻率最大（ρ ~ 150 μΩ·cm）。
Similarly, multi-principal-element alloys (MPEAs), also known as high- and medium-entropy alloys (H/MEAs), possess high electrical resistivity due to the long-range disordered occupations of atoms on lattice sites. The high-entropy alloying strategy has attracted much attention since it can endow MPEAs with both prominent mechanical and functional properties, including corrosion/oxidation resistance, irradiation resistance, magnetic properties, electromagnetic wave-absorption, and thermoelectric properties. For instance, the Co27.7Fe32.6Ni27.7Ta5.0Al7.0 (at.%) MPEA possesses fascinating mechanical and soft-magnetic properties (a tensile yield strength of 904 MPa, saturation magnetization of 100 Am²/kg, coercivity of 78 A/m, electronic resistivity of 103 μΩ·cm, and Curie temperature of 694 K), which were ascribed to the special microstructure composed of the ferromagnetic face-centered-cubic (FCC) matrix and paramagnetic coherent L12 nanoprecipitates. It is noted that any crystalline defects (grain boundaries, phase boundaries, dislocations, solute elements, etc.) to strengthen alloys would impede the movement of magnetic domains through pinning the domain walls, leading to an increase in coercivity. For instance, the coercivity of the Co27.7Fe32.6Ni27.7Ta5.0Al7.0 alloy is strongly dependent on the particle size of coherent precipitates. As the average particle size increases from 24 to 91 nm, the coercivity decreases from 763 to 78 A/m, because the coherent stress characterized by the specific surface area and lattice misfit between the matrix and nanoprecipitates with a small particle size is higher than that with a medium particle size. If the particle size of nanoprecipitates is comparable to or slightly larger than the magnetic domain width, the coercivity might increase drastically and the soft-magnetic feature would disappear.
类似地，多主元合金（MPEA，也称高熵与中熵合金 H/MEA）因原子在晶格位置上的长程无序占据而具有高电阻率。高熵合金化策略之所以备受关注，是因为它能赋予 MPEA 兼具突出的力学与功能性能，包括耐腐蚀/抗氧化、抗辐照、磁性、电磁波吸收和热电性能。例如，Co27.7Fe32.6Ni27.7Ta5.0Al7.0（原子分数）MPEA 兼具引人瞩目的力学与软磁性能（拉伸屈服强度 904 MPa、饱和磁化强度 100 Am²/kg、矫顽力 78 A/m、电阻率 103 μΩ·cm、居里温度 694 K），这归因于由铁磁性面心立方（FCC）基体和顺磁性共格 L12 纳米析出相组成的特殊组织。值得注意的是，任何用于强化合金的晶体缺陷（晶界、相界、位错、溶质元素等）都会通过钉扎畴壁而阻碍磁畴运动，导致矫顽力增大。例如，Co27.7Fe32.6Ni27.7Ta5.0Al7.0 合金的矫顽力强烈依赖于共格析出相的颗粒尺寸：随平均颗粒尺寸从 24 增大到 91 nm，矫顽力从 763 降至 78 A/m，这是因为小颗粒尺寸时由比表面积和基体/纳米析出相晶格错配表征的共格应力高于中等颗粒尺寸时的情形。若纳米析出相的颗粒尺寸与磁畴宽度相当或略大，矫顽力可能急剧增大，软磁特性随之消失。
Extensive reports have demonstrated that the saturation magnetization is dependent on the concentration of ferromagnetic elements (Fe, Co, and Ni), while the coercivity and electrical resistivity are primarily controlled by the microstructure, including the phase constitution and morphology. In particular, the coercivity, as the fundamental parameter for soft magnetism, is closely related to the size of ferromagnetic precipitates in nanocrystalline alloys or the grain size in conventional polycrystalline alloys. MPEAs open a new avenue to design novel soft-magnetic alloys. For instance, the FCC-FeCoNi alloy exhibits an extremely-high saturation magnetization of 155.7 Am²/kg and low coercivity of 189 A/m, but further alloying with Al and Mn would deteriorate the soft-magnetic properties, as evidenced by the BCC/B2 FeCoNiMnAl alloy (Ms = 132.2 Am²/kg and Hc = 266 A/m) and the dual-phase FeCoNiMn0.5Al0.5 alloy with an FCC + BCC/B2 structure (Ms = 51.9 Am²/kg and Hc = 730 A/m). Recently, we developed a soft-magnetic Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr alloy via the cluster-formula approach with the formula of Al2(Mn14) (M14 = Co8Fe4Cr2), which exhibits a saturation magnetization of 135.3 Am²/kg, coercivity of 244 A/m, Curie temperature of 1061 K, and electrical resistivity of 244 μΩ·cm. The outstanding soft-magnetic properties are ascribed to its unique coherent microstructure with ultrafine, spherical, and ferromagnetic BCC nanoprecipitates with a size of 3 ~ 7 nm uniformly distributed in a B2 matrix. However, when the BCC/B2 coherent microstructure was reversed, i.e., large-sized cuboidal B2 nanoparticles (~100 nm) in the BCC matrix in an Al0.7NiCoFeCr2 alloy designed with the cluster formula of Al2Ni14, the soft-magnetic properties deteriorate seriously (Ms = 28.9 Am²/kg and Hc = 947 A/m). When a weave-like BCC/B2 microstructure appears in an Al0.5NiCoFeCr (Al2M14) alloy, the soft-magnetic feature almost vanishes due to the extremely high coercivity (4035 A/m) and extremely-low saturation magnetization (11.7 Am²/kg). These results demonstrated that the magnetic properties of MPEAs are strongly dependent on the microstructure.
大量研究表明，饱和磁化强度取决于铁磁性元素（Fe、Co、Ni）的浓度，而矫顽力和电阻率主要由组织（包括相组成和形貌）控制。尤其是矫顽力，作为软磁性的基本参数，与纳米晶合金中铁磁性析出相的尺寸、或传统多晶合金中晶粒尺寸密切相关。MPEA 为设计新型软磁合金开辟了新途径。例如，FCC-FeCoNi 合金表现出极高的饱和磁化强度（155.7 Am²/kg）和低矫顽力（189 A/m），但进一步添加 Al 和 Mn 会恶化软磁性能，BCC/B2 的 FeCoNiMnAl 合金（Ms = 132.2 Am²/kg，Hc = 266 A/m）和 FCC + BCC/B2 双相的 FeCoNiMn0.5Al0.5 合金（Ms = 51.9 Am²/kg，Hc = 730 A/m）即为佐证。近期我们通过团簇式方法、以 Al2(M14)（M14 = Co8Fe4Cr2）设计开发了软磁 Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr 合金，其饱和磁化强度 135.3 Am²/kg、矫顽力 244 A/m、居里温度 1061 K、电阻率 244 μΩ·cm。其优异软磁性能归因于独特的共格组织——B2 基体中均匀分布着尺寸 3~7 nm 的超细、球形、铁磁性 BCC 纳米析出相。然而，当 BCC/B2 共格组织反转，即以团簇式 Al2Ni14 设计的 Al0.7NiCoFeCr2 合金中 BCC 基体内出现大尺寸立方 B2 纳米颗粒（约 100 nm）时，软磁性能严重恶化（Ms = 28.9 Am²/kg，Hc = 947 A/m）。而当 Al0.5NiCoFeCr（Al2M14）合金中出现编织状（weave-like）BCC/B2 组织时，由于矫顽力极高（4035 A/m）、饱和磁化强度极低（11.7 Am²/kg），软磁特性几乎消失。这些结果表明，MPEA 的磁性能强烈依赖于组织。
In the present work, we applied the cluster formula of Al3M14 to design a new series of MPEAs in the Al–Co–Cr–Fe system, where M can be tailored and represents different combinations of Co, Cr, and Fe. The cluster formula approach has been successfully applied to design alloy compositions in multi-component systems, in which the alloying elements could be rationally matched and thus the amount of each element could be optimized. More significantly, the BCC/B2 coherent microstructure with spherical or cuboidal precipitates could be well controlled via the cluster formula in Al-TMs (transition metals) alloys to achieve prominent mechanical and functional properties simultaneously. Ni is removed in order to form Fe/Co-rich BCC nanoparticles to maximize the saturation magnetization since the magnetic moment (μH,i) of Ni is smaller than that of Fe and Co (μH,Fe = 2.2 μB, μH,Co = 1.7 μB, and μH,Ni = 0.6 μB; μB is the Bohr magneton). Meanwhile, a proper addition of Al via the cluster formula can avoid the formation of weave-like BCC/B2 microstructure. Cr is introduced because it has the potential to tailor the lattice misfit between the BCC and B2 phases for the formation of ultrafine spherical nanoprecipitates. Under the above considerations, five MPEAs were designed, i.e., S1-Al3Co7Fe7, S2-Al3Co6Fe7Cr1, S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2, S4-Al3Co7Fe4Cr3, and S5-Al3Co4Fe7Cr3. The microstructures and magnetic properties of these alloys in different states were systematically characterized.
本工作应用团簇式 Al3M14 在 Al–Co–Cr–Fe 体系中设计了一系列新型 MPEA，其中 M 可调，代表 Co、Cr、Fe 的不同组合。团簇式方法已成功用于多组元体系的合金成分设计，能合理匹配合金元素、优化各元素含量。更重要的是，在 Al–过渡金属（TM）合金中，可通过团簇式很好地控制带球形或立方析出相的 BCC/B2 共格组织，从而同时获得突出的力学与功能性能。为最大化饱和磁化强度而去除 Ni，以形成富 Fe/Co 的 BCC 纳米颗粒，因为 Ni 的磁矩（μH,i）小于 Fe 和 Co（μH,Fe = 2.2 μB，μH,Co = 1.7 μB，μH,Ni = 0.6 μB；μB 为玻尔磁子）。同时，通过团簇式适量加入 Al 可避免形成编织状 BCC/B2 组织。引入 Cr 是因为它有望调控 BCC 与 B2 相之间的晶格错配，以形成超细球形纳米析出相。基于上述考量，设计了五种 MPEA，即 S1-Al3Co7Fe7、S2-Al3Co6Fe7Cr1、S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2、S4-Al3Co7Fe4Cr3 和 S5-Al3Co4Fe7Cr3，并系统表征了它们在不同状态下的组织与磁性能。

2. 实验方法2. Experimental

The Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14 MPEA samples were prepared by arc melting and suction-casting into a copper mold (2 mm × 9 mm × 60 mm) under an argon atmosphere; raw metals were better than 99.99 wt.%. Ingots (~10 g) were re-melted at least five times to ensure chemical homogeneity before suction-casting. Plates were homogenized at 1573 K for 2 h and aged for 24 h at 773, 873, 973, and 1073 K; some representative alloys were aged at 873 ~ 1073 K for 480 h to study thermal stability, with water quenching after all heat treatments. Crystal structures were examined by XRD (Cu-Kα, λ = 0.15406 nm) at 2°/min; microstructures by SEM, aberration-corrected TEM with EDS, and EBSD; and near-atomic-scale compositions by atom probe tomography (APT, CAMECA LEAP 5000 XR). Magnetization and coercivity at RT and high temperatures (873 and 973 K) were measured by VSM (max field 1200 kA/m); Curie temperatures were measured from 375 to 1123 K under 800 kA/m. Electrical resistivity at RT and high temperatures was measured with a variable-temperature-resistivity meter (TRT-1000) from RT to 1073 K (heating rate 10 K/min, every 50 K interval).
Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14 MPEA 样品通过电弧熔炼并在氩气氛下吸铸入铜模（2 mm × 9 mm × 60 mm）制备；原料金属纯度优于 99.99 wt.%。铸锭（约 10 g）在吸铸前至少重熔五次以确保化学均匀。板材在 1573 K 均匀化 2 h，并在 773、873、973、1073 K 时效 24 h；部分代表性合金在 873~1073 K 时效 480 h 以研究热稳定性，所有热处理后均水淬。晶体结构用 XRD（Cu-Kα，λ = 0.15406 nm，2°/min）测定；组织用 SEM、带 EDS 的球差校正 TEM 和 EBSD 表征；近原子尺度成分用原子探针层析（APT，CAMECA LEAP 5000 XR）分析。室温及高温（873、973 K）下的磁化强度与矫顽力用 VSM（最大磁场 1200 kA/m）测量；居里温度在 800 kA/m 下从 375 到 1123 K 测量。室温及高温电阻率用变温电阻仪（TRT-1000）从室温到 1073 K 测量（升温速率 10 K/min，每 50 K 一个间隔）。

3. 结果 —— 软磁性能与组织3. Results — soft-magnetic properties & microstructure

In the as-homogenized state, initial-permeability values μi range from 24.8 (S1-Al3Co7Fe7, lowest) to 36.5 (S5-Al3Fe4Co7Cr3). RT hysteresis loops give saturation magnetization of 107.4 ~ 167.5 Am²/kg and coercivity of 143 ~ 303 A/m; increasing Cr gradually reduces Ms from 167.5 to 107.4 Am²/kg (S1 has the largest Ms), while coercivity stays at a low level. Upon aging at 773–1073 K for 24 h, the soft-magnetic properties of S1-Al3Co7Fe7 are insensitive to aging temperature (Ms 160–165 Am²/kg, Hc 151–183 A/m, comparable to the homogenized state), whereas the coercivity of S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2 (Ms ~129 Am²/kg) increases slightly with aging temperature (150–183 A/m at 773–873 K; 270–374 A/m at 973–1073 K). For S5-Al3Co4Fe7Cr3, coercivity increases drastically after HT aging (811.9 A/m at 973 K, 1974 A/m at 1073 K), implying a remarkable microstructural change. The saturation magnetization is not affected by aging temperature, confirming its close relation to ferromagnetic-element content; the low Ms (103–111 Am²/kg) of S5 is ascribed to its higher non-ferromagnetic Cr content (17.65 at.%). After long-term aging at 973–1073 K for 480 h, S1 still shows excellent soft-magnetic properties (Ms 157.9–165.4 Am²/kg, Hc 135.3–183.1 A/m), while long-term aging at 973 K deteriorates them (Hc up to 1950.2 A/m); S3 still exhibits prominent soft-magnetic properties (Ms 132.0 Am²/kg, Hc 159.2 A/m) after 480 h at 873 K.
在均匀化态，初始磁导率 μi 从 24.8（S1-Al3Co7Fe7，最低）到 36.5（S5-Al3Fe4Co7Cr3）。室温磁滞回线给出饱和磁化强度 107.4~167.5 Am²/kg、矫顽力 143~303 A/m；随 Cr 增加，Ms 从 167.5 逐渐降至 107.4 Am²/kg（S1 的 Ms 最大），而矫顽力保持在较低水平。在 773–1073 K 时效 24 h 后，S1-Al3Co7Fe7 的软磁性能对时效温度不敏感（Ms 160–165 Am²/kg，Hc 151–183 A/m，与均匀化态相当），而 S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2（Ms 约 129 Am²/kg）的矫顽力随时效温度略有增大（773–873 K 时为 150–183 A/m；973–1073 K 时为 270–374 A/m）。对于 S5-Al3Co4Fe7Cr3，高温时效后矫顽力急剧增大（973 K 时 811.9 A/m，1073 K 时 1974 A/m），意味着组织发生了显著变化。饱和磁化强度不受时效温度影响，证实其与铁磁性元素含量密切相关；S5 较低的 Ms（103–111 Am²/kg）归因于其较高的非铁磁性 Cr 含量（17.65 at.%）。在 973–1073 K 长时时效 480 h 后，S1 仍表现出优异的软磁性能（Ms 157.9–165.4 Am²/kg，Hc 135.3–183.1 A/m），而 973 K 长时时效则使其恶化（Hc 高达 1950.2 A/m）；S3 在 873 K 时效 480 h 后仍表现出突出的软磁性能（Ms 132.0 Am²/kg，Hc 159.2 A/m）。
XRD of the as-homogenized alloys shows they consist of disordered BCC solid solution and ordered B2 phases (100 superlattice peak). The lattice misfit ε between BCC and B2 phases increases with Cr content, in the range 0.07 ~ 0.24 %; S1-Al3Co7Fe7 has the lowest (ε ~ 0.07 %). EBSD, SEM and TEM show coarse equiaxed grains (200–400 μm) containing micro-scale cells (120–400 nm); STEM-EDS suggests the cells arise from Co and Fe segregation at cell boundaries. Spheroidal BCC nanoparticles of 3 ~ 5 nm are coherently precipitated in the ordered B2 matrix (DF image and SAED, HRTEM/FFT confirm perfect coherency with ε < 0.2 %). Thus these MPEAs have a hierarchical microstructure of macro-scale equiaxed grains, micro-scale cells, and nano-scale precipitates; the small lattice misfit favors spherical (rather than plate/needle-like) nanoprecipitates. After aging, macro-grains and micro-cells do not change significantly. For S1 aged at 773 K/24 h, ultrafine BCC nanoparticles (3–8 nm) precipitate in an L21 matrix; at 973/1073 K for 480 h they remain coherent at 3–8 nm with a volume fraction f ~ 10 %, showing that the BCC nanoprecipitates resist both aging temperature and time. APT gives BCC nanoprecipitates Al13.1Co45.7Fe41.2 and B2 matrix Al15.7Co43.0Fe41.3 (at.%). For S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2, BCC nanoprecipitates (~90 nm at 973 K/24 h) coarsen markedly and merge into coarse ellipsoidal particles (volume fraction ~28 %, ~150 nm long, ~190 nm wide after 480 h); APT shows the BCC particles are enriched in Cr and Fe (core Cr, shell Fe) while Al and Co partition to the B2 matrix, with a core-shell structure.
均匀化态合金的 XRD 显示其由无序 BCC 固溶体和有序 B2 相组成（100 超晶格峰）。BCC 与 B2 相之间的晶格错配 ε 随 Cr 含量增加，范围为 0.07~0.24%；S1-Al3Co7Fe7 最低（ε ~ 0.07%）。EBSD、SEM 和 TEM 显示粗大等轴晶（200–400 μm），晶内含微米级胞（120–400 nm）；STEM-EDS 表明胞源自 Co、Fe 在胞界的偏析。球状 BCC 纳米颗粒（3~5 nm）在有序 B2 基体中共格析出（DF 像与 SAED、HRTEM/FFT 证实完美共格，ε < 0.2%）。因此这些 MPEA 具有分级组织：宏观等轴晶、微米级胞、纳米级析出相；小的晶格错配有利于形成球形（而非片状/针状）纳米析出相。时效后，宏观晶粒和微米胞变化不大。对 773 K/24 h 时效的 S1，超细 BCC 纳米颗粒（3–8 nm）在 L21 基体中析出；973/1073 K 时效 480 h 后仍共格保持 3–8 nm、体积分数 f ~ 10%，表明 BCC 纳米析出相对时效温度和时间都很稳定。APT 给出 BCC 纳米析出相成分 Al13.1Co45.7Fe41.2、B2 基体 Al15.7Co43.0Fe41.3（原子分数）。对 S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2，BCC 纳米析出相（973 K/24 h 时约 90 nm）明显粗化并合并为粗大椭球颗粒（480 h 后体积分数约 28%、长约 150 nm、宽约 190 nm）；APT 显示 BCC 颗粒富集 Cr 和 Fe（核为 Cr、壳为 Fe），而 Al、Co 分配到 B2 基体，呈核-壳结构。
Curie temperatures are 1254 K (S1-Al3Co7Fe7) and 1052 K (S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2); non-ferromagnetic Cr decreases Tc. The soft-magnetic properties of S1 are insensitive to testing temperature, still showing high saturation magnetization (134–151 Am²/kg) and low coercivity (167–191 A/m) up to 973 K (slightly below the RT Ms of 162.8 Am²/kg). By contrast, the Ms of S3 decreases to 104–115 Am²/kg at 773–873 K and 58.0 Am²/kg at 973 K, while Hc increases sharply to 151 A/m at 773–873 K and 517.4 A/m at 973 K. Therefore S1 retains excellent soft-magnetic properties up to 973 K and S3 up to 873 K. The electrical resistivity of the homogenized MPEAs is 262 ~ 285 μΩ·cm (rising with non-ferromagnetic Cr); for S3 it rises slowly from RT to 300 μΩ·cm at 873 K, then dramatically up to 328 μΩ·cm at 1078 K—surpassing most HEAs (100 ~ 300 μΩ·cm). This high resistivity (about twice that of amorphous/nanocrystalline alloys and five times that of Fe-Si steels), combined with high Tc (>1000 K for S1, S3 and Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr), makes these alloys attractive as affordable high-temperature soft magnets whose HT soft-magnetic properties far exceed those of amorphous and nanocrystalline alloys.
居里温度分别为 1254 K（S1-Al3Co7Fe7）和 1052 K（S3-Al3Co6Fe6Cr2）；非铁磁性 Cr 降低 Tc。S1 的软磁性能对测试温度不敏感，直到 973 K 仍表现出高饱和磁化强度（134–151 Am²/kg）和低矫顽力（167–191 A/m）（略低于室温 Ms 162.8 Am²/kg）。相比之下，S3 的 Ms 在 773–873 K 降至 104–115 Am²/kg、973 K 降至 58.0 Am²/kg，而 Hc 在 773–873 K 急升至 151 A/m、973 K 升至 517.4 A/m。因此 S1 在 973 K 以下、S3 在 873 K 以下均保持优异软磁性能。均匀化态 MPEA 的电阻率为 262~285 μΩ·cm（随非铁磁性 Cr 升高）；S3 从室温缓慢上升到 873 K 的 300 μΩ·cm，随后急剧上升到 1078 K 的 328 μΩ·cm——超过大多数 HEA（100~300 μΩ·cm）。这种高电阻率（约为非晶/纳米晶合金的两倍、Fe-Si 钢的五倍），加之高 Tc（S1、S3 和 Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr 均 >1000 K），使这些合金成为有吸引力的经济型高温软磁体，其高温软磁性能远超非晶和纳米晶合金。

4. 讨论4. Discussion

(4.1 Thermal stability of BCC/B2.) The as-homogenized alloys consist of disordered BCC solid solution and ordered B2, and the low lattice misfit (0.07 ~ 0.24 %) between them produces the coherent microstructure with ultrafine ferromagnetic BCC nanoparticles (3 ~ 8 nm) in the B2 matrix. This renders prominent, incomparable soft-magnetic properties (Ms 107.4 ~ 167.5 Am²/kg, Hc 143 ~ 303 A/m). S1-Al3Co7Fe7 has the highest thermal stability: even after 1073 K/480 h its BCC nanoprecipitates do not coarsen significantly (3 ~ 8 nm), so its soft-magnetic properties remain comparable to the as-homogenized state. In S3 the BCC nanoprecipitates show negligible coarsening at 873 K but coarsen remarkably at 973 K (~90 nm at 24 h to ~150 nm at 480 h), raising coercivity to 1950 A/m. The lattice misfit between coherent phases induces elastic strain energy—the driving force for coarsening—and is closely related to composition difference; adding Cr enlarges the composition/lattice misfit (Cr strongly segregates to BCC), reducing thermal stability. In addition, Cr increases the BCC volume fraction (~28 % in S3 vs ~10 % in S1) and, above 973 K, Cr's fast diffusion (9.26 × 10⁻¹⁸ m²/s at 973 K, 1.55 × 10⁻¹⁶ at 1073 K—two to four times that of Al, Fe, Co) accelerates coarsening. Cr's strong segregation forms the core-shell composite nanoprecipitates (Cr core, Fe shell).
（4.1 BCC/B2 的热稳定性。）均匀化态合金由无序 BCC 固溶体和有序 B2 组成，二者之间低的晶格错配（0.07~0.24%）形成了 B2 基体中超细铁磁性 BCC 纳米颗粒（3~8 nm）的共格组织。这带来了突出且无可比拟的软磁性能（Ms 107.4~167.5 Am²/kg，Hc 143~303 A/m）。S1-Al3Co7Fe7 热稳定性最高：即使 1073 K/480 h 后其 BCC 纳米析出相也不明显粗化（3~8 nm），软磁性能仍与均匀化态相当。在 S3 中，BCC 纳米析出相在 873 K 几乎不粗化，但在 973 K 显著粗化（24 h 约 90 nm 到 480 h 约 150 nm），使矫顽力升至 1950 A/m。共格相之间的晶格错配诱发弹性应变能——这是粗化的驱动力——并与成分差异密切相关；加 Cr 会增大成分/晶格错配（Cr 强烈偏析到 BCC），降低热稳定性。此外，Cr 增大 BCC 体积分数（S3 约 28%，对比 S1 约 10%），且在 973 K 以上 Cr 的快速扩散（973 K 时 9.26 × 10⁻¹⁸ m²/s，1073 K 时 1.55 × 10⁻¹⁶——为 Al、Fe、Co 的两到四倍）加速粗化。Cr 的强偏析形成了核-壳复合纳米析出相（Cr 核、Fe 壳）。
(4.2 Coercivity vs. microstructure.) Coercivity depends on magnetic domains, whose motion is impeded by internal stress from crystalline defects. It is expressed as Hc = (λs·Δσ)/(2μ0·Ms·cosθ) · [3δ/l]/[1+3(δ/l)²] (Eq. 1), where λs is magnetostriction, Δσ and l the amplitude and characteristic wavelength of the internal stress field, and δ the domain wall width, estimated by δ = π(Aex/K1)^(1/2) (Eq. 2). For S1 across the as-homogenized and aged states, the hierarchical microstructure (macro grains, micro cells, nano particles) is very stable, with sizes 292 μm, 354 nm and 4 nm respectively. Coercivity varies with size as Hc ∝ 1/Dp^6 in the nanocrystalline regime (particle size ≪ domain wall width, anisotropy averaged over several structural units) and as Hc ∝ 1/Dp for large-scale variations (D > 100 μm); only when the size is comparable to the domain wall width does Hc rise to a maximum (>3000 A/m, i.e. semi-hard). The domain wall width was estimated at 211 nm (Eq. 2, a0 = 0.2870 nm, Tc = 1254 K, K1 = 6.8 kJ/m³). With interparticle distance λp = √(2/3)·Dp·(√(π/4f) − 1) (Eq. 3, f ~ 10 %), the coercivity contributions of macro grains, micro cells, and nano particles are proportional to their surface areas (Hc = A1·Hc-macro + A2·Hc-micro + A3·Hc-nano, with A1 = 0.0002, A2 = 0.1008, A3 = 0.89). The estimated total coercivity 133.5 A/m agrees with the measured 135 ~ 191 A/m, showing that the nano-scale particles dominate coercivity and that S1's low coercivity is primarily due to the uniform distribution of ultrafine ferromagnetic BCC nanoprecipitates. For S3, coarsening of BCC nanoprecipitates (4 → 90 → 350 nm) increases coercivity; when the particle size becomes comparable to or slightly larger than the domain wall width, coercivity increases drastically.
（4.2 矫顽力与组织。）矫顽力取决于磁畴，而磁畴运动受晶体缺陷内应力阻碍。其表达式为 Hc = (λs·Δσ)/(2μ0·Ms·cosθ) · [3δ/l]/[1+3(δ/l)²]（式 1），其中 λs 为磁致伸缩，Δσ 与 l 分别为内应力场的幅值和特征波长，δ 为畴壁宽度，由 δ = π(Aex/K1)^(1/2)（式 2）估算。对于跨越均匀化态与时效态的 S1，其分级组织（宏观晶粒、微米胞、纳米颗粒）非常稳定，尺寸分别为 292 μm、354 nm 和 4 nm。矫顽力随尺寸变化：在纳米晶区间（颗粒尺寸 ≪ 畴壁宽度、各向异性在若干结构单元上被平均）为 Hc ∝ 1/Dp⁶，在大尺度变化（D > 100 μm）下为 Hc ∝ 1/Dp；只有当尺寸与畴壁宽度相当时，Hc 才升至最大值（>3000 A/m，即半硬磁）。畴壁宽度估算为 211 nm（式 2，a0 = 0.2870 nm，Tc = 1254 K，K1 = 6.8 kJ/m³）。以颗粒间距 λp = √(2/3)·Dp·(√(π/4f) − 1)（式 3，f ~ 10%），宏观晶粒、微米胞和纳米颗粒对矫顽力的贡献与其表面积成正比（Hc = A1·Hc-macro + A2·Hc-micro + A3·Hc-nano，其中 A1 = 0.0002，A2 = 0.1008，A3 = 0.89）。估算总矫顽力 133.5 A/m 与实测 135~191 A/m 吻合，表明纳米级颗粒主导矫顽力，S1 的低矫顽力主要源自超细铁磁性 BCC 纳米析出相的均匀分布。对于 S3，BCC 纳米析出相的粗化（4 → 90 → 350 nm）使矫顽力增大；当颗粒尺寸与畴壁宽度相当或略大时，矫顽力急剧增大。
(4.3 Saturation magnetization vs. composition.) The saturation magnetization Ms (or saturation induction Bs) depends on the amount of ferromagnetic elements (Fe, Co, Ni), whose magnetic moments are 2.2, 1.7, and 0.6 μB, respectively. The mean magnetic moment per ferromagnetic atom is μ̄H = ΣμH,i·xi, where xi is the amount of element i. Bs shows a linear upward trend with the increased mean magnetic moment per ferromagnetic atom. The Bs values of some alloys (e.g., Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr, Hitperm Fe44Co44Zr7B4Cu1, and S1-Al3Co7Fe7) are slightly higher than a linear relationship would predict, probably due to strong short-range interactions among ferromagnetic atomic pairs, which contribute to the magnetic moment per atom effectively.
（4.3 饱和磁化强度与成分。）饱和磁化强度 Ms（或饱和磁感应强度 Bs）取决于铁磁性元素（Fe、Co、Ni）的含量，其磁矩分别为 2.2、1.7、0.6 μB。每个铁磁性原子的平均磁矩为 μ̄H = ΣμH,i·xi，其中 xi 为元素 i 的含量。Bs 随每个铁磁性原子平均磁矩的增大呈线性上升趋势。某些合金（如 Al1.5Co4Fe2Cr、Hitperm Fe44Co44Zr7B4Cu1 和 S1-Al3Co7Fe7）的 Bs 略高于线性关系的预测值，这可能源自铁磁性原子对之间强烈的短程相互作用，从而有效贡献了每原子磁矩。

5. 结论5. Conclusions

The present work developed a series of novel soft-magnetic MPEAs with a coherent microstructure consisting of ultrafine BCC ferromagnetic nanoprecipitates in the B2 matrix, by deliberately tailoring the chemical compositions via Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14 (five alloys: Al3Co7Fe7, Al3Co6Fe7Cr1, Al3Co6Fe6Cr2, Al3Co7Fe4Cr3, Al3Co4Fe7Cr3). The main conclusions are: (1) The MPEAs exhibit excellent soft-magnetic properties (Ms 107.4 ~ 167.5 Am²/kg, Hc 143 ~ 303 A/m) as-homogenized; increasing Cr reduces Ms. The soft-magnetic properties of Al3Co7Fe7 are insensitive to aging temperature (high Ms 157.9 Am²/kg, low Hc 135.3 A/m even after 480 h at 773 ~ 873 K), whereas the coercivity of Al3Co6Fe6Cr2 increases with aging temperature (~150 A/m at 773–873 K to 270 ~ 374 A/m at 973 ~ 1073 K), and Al3Co4Fe7Cr3 increases drastically to 1974 A/m after 1073 K aging, ascribed to microstructural change. (2) The as-homogenized MPEAs contain ultrafine spherical BCC ferromagnetic nanoparticles (3 ~ 8 nm) in the B2 matrix, related to the small lattice misfit (ε < 0.21 %). Al3Co7Fe7 has the highest thermal stability (BCC nanoparticles do not coarsen after 480 h at 1073 K; slightly enriched in Co and Fe vs the Al-rich B2 matrix), giving Ms 134.7 Am²/kg and Hc 167.2 A/m at 973 K. (3) The coherent microstructure in Al3Co4Fe7Cr3 is also stable after 480 h at 873 K, giving comparable soft-magnetic properties (Ms ~130 Am²/kg, Hc ~150 A/m); these ultrafine nanoprecipitates have a core-shell structure (Cr core, Fe shell), and it shows Ms 104.4 Am²/kg, Hc 151.2 A/m at 873 K. Further increasing aging temperature/time coarsens BCC particles (~90 nm at 973 K/24 h, ~500 nm long × ~190 nm wide at 480 h), sharply raising coercivity to ~1950 A/m. (4) The coercivity is primarily dependent on the size of BCC nanoprecipitates; when the particle size is comparable to the domain wall width (~200 nm), coercivity increases drastically, degrading soft magnetism. (5) The BCC/B2 microstructure has high Curie temperature (1254 K for Al3Co7Fe7, 1052 K for Al3Co6Fe6Cr2) and electrical resistivity (262 ~ 285 μΩ·cm), showing great potential for HT soft-magnetic applications.
本工作通过团簇式 Al3(Co,Fe,Cr)14 有意调控化学成分，开发了一系列具有共格组织（B2 基体中含超细 BCC 铁磁性纳米析出相）的新型软磁 MPEA（五种合金：Al3Co7Fe7、Al3Co6Fe7Cr1、Al3Co6Fe6Cr2、Al3Co7Fe4Cr3、Al3Co4Fe7Cr3）。主要结论如下：(1) 均匀化态 MPEA 表现出优异软磁性能（Ms 107.4~167.5 Am²/kg，Hc 143~303 A/m）；增加 Cr 会降低 Ms。Al3Co7Fe7 的软磁性能对时效温度不敏感（即使 773~873 K 时效 480 h 后仍有高 Ms 157.9 Am²/kg、低 Hc 135.3 A/m），而 Al3Co6Fe6Cr2 的矫顽力随时效温度升高（773–873 K 约 150 A/m 到 973~1073 K 的 270~374 A/m），Al3Co4Fe7Cr3 在 1073 K 时效后急剧增至 1974 A/m，归因于组织变化。(2) 均匀化态 MPEA 在 B2 基体中含超细球形 BCC 铁磁性纳米颗粒（3~8 nm），与小晶格错配（ε < 0.21%）相关。Al3Co7Fe7 热稳定性最高（1073 K 时效 480 h 后 BCC 纳米颗粒不粗化；相对富 Al 的 B2 基体略富 Co、Fe），在 973 K 下 Ms 134.7 Am²/kg、Hc 167.2 A/m。(3) Al3Co4Fe7Cr3 的共格组织在 873 K 时效 480 h 后也很稳定，给出可比的软磁性能（Ms 约 130 Am²/kg，Hc 约 150 A/m）；这些超细纳米析出相为核-壳结构（Cr 核、Fe 壳），在 873 K 下 Ms 104.4 Am²/kg、Hc 151.2 A/m。进一步提高时效温度/时间会使 BCC 颗粒粗化（973 K/24 h 约 90 nm，480 h 长约 500 nm × 宽约 190 nm），使矫顽力急升至约 1950 A/m。(4) 矫顽力主要取决于 BCC 纳米析出相的尺寸；当颗粒尺寸与畴壁宽度（约 200 nm）相当时，矫顽力急剧增大，软磁性能退化。(5) BCC/B2 组织具有高居里温度（Al3Co7Fe7 为 1254 K，Al3Co6Fe6Cr2 为 1052 K）和高电阻率（262~285 μΩ·cm），在高温软磁应用中展现出巨大潜力。
