Acta Materialia · 2026

纳米片层异质结构主导 Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 合金从超软到硬态的磁性可调性Nano-lamellar heterostructures dictate magnetic tunability from ultra-soft to hard states in Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 alloy

Acta Materialia 315 (2026) 122428 · doi:10.1016/j.actamat.2026.122428 · Shaowen Yan, Weixin Lou, Hongbo Xie, Zengbao Jiao, Yiping Lu
本页为「摘要 + 正文」中文翻译（含引言、实验、结果与讨论、结论各节，部分段落合并精炼但保留全部关键数据）；已省略参考文献与致谢。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Achieving broad magnetic tunability within a single alloy, without compromising saturation magnetization (Ms) and mechanical performance, remains a significant challenge. Inherent trade-offs between magnetic and mechanical properties have severely constrained the design of such materials. Here, we achieve synergistic optimization of coercivity, saturation magnetization, and yield strength by introducing a hierarchical heterostructure strategy in Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 multi-principal element alloy. By systematically varying the aging time, we modulate the evolution of alternating BCC and L12 nano-lamellar structures whose evolution fundamentally governs the magnetic response. These structures induce pronounced nano-lamellar boundary strengthening while, paradoxically, reducing the coercivity by an order of magnitude to an ultra-soft magnetic state (~0.233 kA m⁻¹), thereby overturning the long-standing paradigm where strengthening phases typically increase coercivity. Extended aging further drives the structural evolution into a highly anisotropic nano-lamellar architecture, enabling a deterministic switch to a robust hard magnetic state (~21.452 kA m⁻¹) and spanning two orders of magnitude in coercivity. Remarkably, this unprecedented tunability is accompanied by a synergistic enhancement of Ms to 158 Am² kg⁻¹, effectively mitigating the conventional magnetic dilution effect. The underlying mechanism is attributed to distinct roles of the nano-lamellar BCC FeCo-rich phase, which exhibits an ultra-soft magnetic Ms (252 Am² kg⁻¹) and transitions from acting as an exchange-spring softening agent to serving as a potent domain wall pinning network with strong shape anisotropy. This work establishes a "composition-invariant, structure-tailorable" paradigm for multifunctional magnetic materials, offering direct implications for next-generation electromagnetic technologies.
在不牺牲饱和磁化强度（Ms）和力学性能的前提下，于单一合金中实现宽广的磁性可调性，仍是一项重大挑战。磁性与力学性能之间固有的权衡严重制约了此类材料的设计。本文在 Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 多主元合金中引入分级异质结构策略，实现了矫顽力、饱和磁化强度和屈服强度的协同优化。通过系统改变时效时间，我们调控交替 BCC 与 L12 纳米片层结构的演化，而这种演化从根本上支配磁响应。这些结构一方面带来显著的纳米片层界面强化，另一方面却又反常地将矫顽力降低一个数量级，达到超软磁态（约 0.233 kA·m⁻¹），从而颠覆了"强化相通常增大矫顽力"的长期范式。进一步延长时效驱动结构演化为高度各向异性的纳米片层构型，实现向稳健硬磁态（约 21.452 kA·m⁻¹）的确定性切换，矫顽力跨越两个数量级。值得注意的是，这种前所未有的可调性还伴随 Ms 协同提升至 158 Am²·kg⁻¹，有效缓解了传统的磁稀释效应。其机理归因于纳米片层富 FeCo 的 BCC 相的不同角色——该相具有超软磁的 Ms（252 Am²·kg⁻¹），并从充当交换弹簧的软化剂，转变为具有强形状各向异性的强效畴壁钉扎网络。本工作确立了多功能磁性材料的"成分不变、结构可裁"范式，对下一代电磁技术具有直接意义。

1. 引言1. Introduction

Magnetic materials are indispensable to modern technologies, driving advancements in power conversion, transportation electrification, and information processing. They are generally categorized as soft, semi-hard, or hard magnets based on their coercivity and energy products. Soft magnets, characterized by their low coercivity, high permeability, and high saturation magnetization, are crucial for the efficient operation of transformers, inductors, and recording devices. In contrast, hard magnets exhibit high coercivity, large remanence, and high energy products, making them essential for applications such as motors, generators, and storage media. The ongoing demand for greater efficiency and durability continues to drive the development of next-generation magnetic materials. Particularly, emerging dynamic applications, such as high-speed traction motors, impose stringent mechanical constraints, thereby exposing the extreme brittleness and limited machinability of conventional commercial magnets.
磁性材料对现代技术不可或缺，推动着功率转换、交通电气化和信息处理的进步。依据矫顽力和磁能积，它们通常分为软磁、半硬磁和硬磁体。软磁体以低矫顽力、高磁导率和高饱和磁化强度为特征，对变压器、电感器和记录器件的高效运行至关重要。相比之下，硬磁体具有高矫顽力、大剩磁和高磁能积，对电机、发电机和存储介质等应用不可或缺。对更高效率和耐久性的持续需求不断推动新一代磁性材料的开发。尤其是高速牵引电机等新兴动态应用施加了严苛的机械约束，从而暴露出传统商用磁体的极端脆性和有限的可加工性。
However, a fundamental challenge persists: the intrinsic trade-off between key properties. For soft magnetic materials, increasing mechanical strength often results in a decrease in magnetic softness—in metallic systems, lattice defects hinder dislocation motion and thereby enhance mechanical strength, but simultaneously act as domain wall pinning sites, resulting in increased coercivity and diminished magnetic softness. Therefore, soft magnetic design generally aims to minimize defects, whereas greater mechanical strength necessitates their deliberate introduction. Similarly, in permanent magnets, the simultaneous improvement of coercivity and saturation magnetization has been a longstanding challenge arising from the inherent inverse relationship between magnetization and the magnetocrystalline anisotropy field. Multi-principal element alloys (MPEAs) challenge traditional alloy design paradigms by offering unprecedented opportunities to tailor functional properties through their broad compositional space and unique core effects, providing a promising avenue for overcoming the traditional performance trade-off inherent in magnetic materials. Recent advances show that the incorporation of coherent, ordered nanoprecipitates within a ferromagnetic matrix can significantly enhance mechanical strength without compromising magnetic softness. For example, Han et al. developed non-equiatomic Fe-Co-Ni-Ta-Al alloys with paramagnetic coherent nanoparticles embedded in a ferromagnetic matrix, exhibiting ultralow coercivity (78 A m⁻¹), high tensile strength (~1.3 GPa) with 54% tensile ductility, moderate Ms (100 Am²/kg), and high resistivity (103 μΩ·cm).
然而，一个根本挑战始终存在：关键性能之间的固有权衡。对软磁材料而言，提高力学强度往往导致磁软性下降——在金属体系中，晶格缺陷阻碍位错运动从而增强力学强度，但同时又充当畴壁钉扎位点，导致矫顽力增大、磁软性减弱。因此，软磁设计通常力求最小化缺陷，而更高的力学强度又必然要求刻意引入缺陷。类似地，在永磁体中，同时提高矫顽力和饱和磁化强度长期以来都是难题，源自磁化强度与磁晶各向异性场之间固有的反比关系。多主元合金（MPEA）以其宽广的成分空间和独特的核心效应，提供了前所未有的调控功能性能的机会，从而挑战了传统合金设计范式，为克服磁性材料固有的性能权衡提供了有希望的途径。最新进展表明，在铁磁性基体中引入共格有序纳米析出相，能在不损害磁软性的前提下显著提升力学强度。例如，Han 等开发的非等原子比 Fe-Co-Ni-Ta-Al 合金，在铁磁性基体中嵌入顺磁性共格纳米颗粒，表现出超低矫顽力（78 A·m⁻¹）、高抗拉强度（约 1.3 GPa）与 54% 拉伸塑性、中等 Ms（100 Am²/kg）和高电阻率（103 μΩ·cm）。
The design concept based on MPEAs has led to the development of excellent new soft magnetic materials with exceptional properties, including gigapascal-class mechanical strength, even up to 2 GPa, along with excellent ductility, thermal stability, and ultra-high electrical resistivity. This strategy typically involves the incorporation of non-magnetic or weakly magnetic strengthening elements (such as Al, Ta, and Nb) that exhibit large negative mixing enthalpies (ΔHmix) with the primary ferromagnetic elements (Fe, Co, and Ni). This thermodynamic driving force facilitates the formation of coherent, ordered intermetallic nanoprecipitates. While this approach successfully combines ultra-low coercivity with outstanding mechanical properties, it introduces a fundamental limitation: the addition of these non-magnetic elements inevitably dilutes the net magnetic moment, thereby reducing the saturation magnetization (Ms). A low saturation magnetization directly constrains motor torque and power density, hindering advancements toward lightweight and miniaturized designs. Conversely, significant progress has also been made in engineering microstructures for magnetic hardening in rare-earth-free MPEAs. For instance, thermomagnetic processing can produce fine nano-lamellar structures that serve as strong pinning sites for both magnetic domain walls and dislocations, thereby simultaneously enhancing coercivity and mechanical strength in the Co-Fe-Ni-Al system. Recent studies further demonstrate that nanoscale chemical shape anisotropy (CSA) serves as an effective mechanism for achieving hard magnetic behavior. Collectively, these findings underscore the critical importance of precise microstructural control in tailoring magnetic properties.
基于 MPEA 的设计理念已催生出性能卓越的新型软磁材料，包括吉帕级、甚至高达 2 GPa 的力学强度，兼具优异塑性、热稳定性和超高电阻率。这一策略通常引入与主要铁磁性元素（Fe、Co、Ni）具有较大负混合焓（ΔHmix）的非磁性或弱磁性强化元素（如 Al、Ta、Nb）。这种热力学驱动力促进共格有序金属间化合物纳米析出相的形成。虽然该方法成功地将超低矫顽力与出色的力学性能结合起来，却带来了一个根本局限：加入这些非磁性元素不可避免地稀释净磁矩，从而降低饱和磁化强度（Ms）。低饱和磁化强度直接制约电机扭矩和功率密度，妨碍向轻量化、小型化设计的推进。反过来，在无稀土 MPEA 的磁硬化组织工程方面也取得了重要进展。例如，热磁处理可产生细小的纳米片层结构，充当磁畴壁和位错的强钉扎位点，从而在 Co-Fe-Ni-Al 体系中同时增强矫顽力和力学强度。最新研究进一步表明，纳米尺度化学形状各向异性（CSA）是实现硬磁行为的有效机制。总的来说，这些发现凸显了精确组织控制在调控磁性能中的关键作用。
Despite notable progress, significant challenges persist in the development of magnetic MPEAs. First, most reported MPEAs are specifically designed for either soft-magnetic or hard-magnetic applications, and the ability to selectively program distinct magnetic states from a single precursor through conventional thermal processing remains exceptionally rare. Second, although the role of coherent nanoprecipitates in achieving a synergy between mechanical strength and soft magnetic properties is well established, conventional paradigms in magnetic alloy design imply a rigid coupling between microstructure and coercivity; achieving an ultra-soft magnetic state and high saturation magnetization in a precipitation-strengthened alloy remains a major challenge. Third, direct, in-situ evidence linking complex multiphase structures to the underlying domain wall dynamics across the soft-to-hard transition is still limited. Here, we achieve synergistic optimization of coercivity, saturation magnetization, and yield strength by introducing a "composition-invariant, structure-tailorable" design strategy based on nano-lamellar heterostructures. Specifically, the nano-lamellar structure is engineered to transition from acting as an exchange-coupling channel (facilitating magnetic ultra-softness) to functioning as a potent pinning center (inducing magnetic hardness), while simultaneously serving as an effective barrier to dislocation motion to ensure high yield strength. Crucially, the high saturation magnetization is intrinsically derived from the ultra-high magnetic moment of the FeCo-rich lamellae. We utilize a Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 (at.%) MPEA as a model system.
尽管进展显著，磁性 MPEA 的开发仍存在重大挑战。其一，已报道的多数 MPEA 都是专门为软磁或硬磁应用设计的，而通过常规热处理从单一前驱体有选择地"编程"出不同磁态的能力极为罕见。其二，尽管共格纳米析出相在力学强度与软磁性能协同中的作用已被充分确立，传统磁性合金设计范式却暗示组织与矫顽力之间存在刚性耦合；在析出强化合金中同时实现超软磁态和高饱和磁化强度仍是重大挑战。其三，将复杂多相结构与软-硬转变过程中畴壁动力学相联系的直接原位证据仍然有限。本文通过引入基于纳米片层异质结构的"成分不变、结构可裁"设计策略，实现了矫顽力、饱和磁化强度和屈服强度的协同优化。具体而言，纳米片层结构被设计成从充当交换耦合通道（促成磁超软性）转变为充当强效钉扎中心（诱发磁硬性），同时充当阻碍位错运动的有效障碍以确保高屈服强度。关键在于，高饱和磁化强度本征地来自富 FeCo 片层的超高磁矩。我们以 Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5（原子分数）MPEA 作为模型体系。

2. 实验方法2. Experimental methods

The bulk alloy ingot (nominal Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5, at.%) was prepared by arc-melting high-purity raw metals (>99.9 wt.%) under high-purity argon (a Ti getter was pre-melted to remove residual oxygen and nitrogen); ingots were remelted at least five times for chemical homogeneity, then cut into 7-mm cubes. Phase diagrams were generated using Pandat with the PanHEA2023 database. Plates were solution-treated at 1323 K for 8 h and water-quenched (SS-MPEA); heterogeneous precipitation was driven by aging at 763 K for 4 h, 24 h, and 64 h (A4-MPEA, A24-MPEA, A64-MPEA). Bulk ingots corresponding to the FCC matrix, the nano-lamellar L12 phase, and the nano-lamellar BCC phase were individually arc-melted for reference. Characterization used XRD (Cu-Kα), EBSD, TEM/HAADF-STEM (aberration-corrected, geometric phase analysis GPA), and APT (LEAP 6000 XR). Magnetic response was measured by MPMS with VSM (field along the out-of-plane direction); Ms was obtained by fitting the high-field data to the law of approach to saturation. Electrical resistivity was measured by a four-probe Seebeck instrument (ZEM-3). Compression and tensile tests (Instron) and nanoindentation (Agilent G200) were performed at room temperature.
块体合金锭（名义成分 Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5，原子分数）在高纯氩气下由高纯原料金属（>99.9 wt.%）电弧熔炼制备（预熔 Ti 吸气剂以去除残余氧、氮）；铸锭重熔至少五次以保证化学均匀，再切成 7 mm 立方块。相图用 Pandat 配合 PanHEA2023 数据库生成。板材在 1323 K 固溶处理 8 h 后水淬（SS-MPEA）；通过在 763 K 时效 4 h、24 h、64 h 驱动异质析出（分别为 A4-MPEA、A24-MPEA、A64-MPEA）。另外单独电弧熔炼了对应 FCC 基体、纳米片层 L12 相和纳米片层 BCC 相的块体锭以作参照。表征采用 XRD（Cu-Kα）、EBSD、TEM/HAADF-STEM（球差校正，几何相分析 GPA）和 APT（LEAP 6000 XR）。磁响应用带 VSM 的 MPMS 测量（磁场沿面外方向）；Ms 通过将高场数据拟合到趋近饱和定律获得。电阻率用四探针塞贝克仪（ZEM-3）测量。压缩、拉伸试验（Instron）和纳米压痕（Agilent G200）在室温进行。

3.1 组织表征3.1 Microstructural characterization

The Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 alloy was designed by exploiting differential chemical affinities to tailor solid-state phase separation. Guided by thermodynamic calculations, the alloy was solution-treated at 1323 K and quenched to obtain a supersaturated solid solution; aging at 763 K within the L12 + BCC (B2) dual-phase region drives phase decomposition. This rationale is founded on the distinct hierarchy in binding affinities for Al and Ti: their mixing enthalpies with Ni and Al (−35 and −22 kJ/mol) are significantly more negative than those of Co and Fe. Owing to this, Ni preferentially associates with Al and Ti, promoting the ordered L12 phase; the remaining Fe and Co (near-zero mutual mixing enthalpy) partition into adjacent regions, stabilizing the BCC phase. The ideal configurational entropy is ~1.36 R, which is thermodynamically important for counterbalancing the strongly negative mixing enthalpies and suppressing premature intermetallic compound formation. XRD shows SS-MPEA and A4-MPEA are single-phase FCC, whereas A24-MPEA and A64-MPEA develop BCC peaks; BCC precipitation shows a pronounced kinetic delay (higher nucleation barrier). Quantitatively, ~20% BCC forms in A24-MPEA, increasing to ~67% in A64-MPEA.
Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 合金的设计利用了元素间不同的化学亲和力来裁剪固态相分离。在热力学计算指导下，合金在 1323 K 固溶并淬火以获得过饱和固溶体；在 763 K、L12 + BCC（B2）两相区内时效则驱动相分解。其依据在于 Al、Ti 结合亲和力的明显层级：Ni 与 Al、Ti 的混合焓（−35 和 −22 kJ/mol）比 Co、Fe 的负得多。因此，Ni 优先与 Al、Ti 结合，促进有序 L12 相；剩余的 Fe、Co（彼此混合焓近零）分配到邻近区域，稳定 BCC 相。理想构型熵约为 1.36 R，这对抵消强烈的负混合焓、抑制金属间化合物的过早形成在热力学上很重要。XRD 显示 SS-MPEA 和 A4-MPEA 为单相 FCC，而 A24-MPEA 和 A64-MPEA 出现 BCC 峰；BCC 析出表现出明显的动力学滞后（形核势垒较高）。定量上，A24-MPEA 中约形成 20% BCC，到 A64-MPEA 增至约 67%。
SS-MPEA is a disordered single-phase FCC. After short-term aging (A4-MPEA), coherent L12 nanoprecipitates (~2 nm, near-spherical, uniformly distributed) form in the FCC matrix and effectively redistribute the severe internal stress of SS-MPEA. In A24-MPEA, BF-STEM reveals two structural features: L12 nanoprecipitates (2–6 nm, spherical) within equiaxed FCC grains, and nano-lamellar regions where BCC and L12 lamellae alternate. The BCC nano-lamellae are enriched in Fe and Co (near 1:1), whereas the L12 lamellae are enriched in Ni, Al, and Ti; most lamellar BCC is shorter than 120 nm (average length ~47 nm) and <35 nm wide (average ~15 nm), following a Kurdjumov–Sachs (K–S) orientation relationship with the L12 lamellae. In A64-MPEA, a dual-phase BCC + L12 lamellar microstructure with high aspect ratio (~35, length ~485 nm, width ~14 nm) dominates, following the Nishiyama–Wassermann (N–W) relationship ({111}//{011}, ⟨110⟩//⟨100⟩); {111} twins form within the L12 lamellae to accommodate residual strain, and the BCC/L12 lattice mismatch is ~23–24% (semi-coherent). The lamellar morphology is dictated by a solid-state eutectoid reaction whose configuration is governed by the strong anisotropy of interfacial and elastic strain energies. APT gives the A24 BCC and L12 lamellae as Co48.1Fe47.6Ni3.3Al0.8Ti0.2 and Ni66.2Ti13Al10.2Co6.9Fe3.7 (at.%), and the A64 phases as Co47.3Fe47Ni4.2Al1Ti0.5 and Ni66.4Ti11.2Al10.1Co7.4Fe4.9 (at.%).
SS-MPEA 为无序单相 FCC。短时时效后（A4-MPEA），FCC 基体中形成共格 L12 纳米析出相（约 2 nm，近球形，均匀分布），有效重新分配了 SS-MPEA 的严重内应力。在 A24-MPEA 中，BF-STEM 揭示两种结构特征：等轴 FCC 晶粒内的 L12 纳米析出相（2–6 nm，球形），以及 BCC 与 L12 片层交替的纳米片层区域。BCC 纳米片层富集 Fe、Co（接近 1:1），而 L12 片层富集 Ni、Al、Ti；多数片层 BCC 短于 120 nm（平均长约 47 nm）、宽 <35 nm（平均约 15 nm），与 L12 片层遵循 Kurdjumov–Sachs（K–S）取向关系。在 A64-MPEA 中，以高长径比（约 35，长约 485 nm、宽约 14 nm）的 BCC + L12 双相片层组织为主，遵循 Nishiyama–Wassermann（N–W）关系（{111}//{011}，⟨110⟩//⟨100⟩）；L12 片层内形成 {111} 孪晶以协调残余应变，BCC/L12 晶格错配约 23–24%（半共格）。片层形貌由固态共析反应所决定，其构型受界面能与弹性应变能强各向异性的支配。APT 给出 A24 的 BCC 与 L12 片层成分分别为 Co48.1Fe47.6Ni3.3Al0.8Ti0.2 和 Ni66.2Ti13Al10.2Co6.9Fe3.7（原子分数），A64 的两相为 Co47.3Fe47Ni4.2Al1Ti0.5 和 Ni66.4Ti11.2Al10.1Co7.4Fe4.9（原子分数）。

3.2 磁性能与力学性能3.2 Magnetic and mechanical properties

After 24 h of aging (A24-MPEA), the coercivity Hc decreases by nearly an order of magnitude compared to the solution-treated condition, dropping from 2.390 ± 0.025 to 0.233 ± 0.094 kA m⁻¹. With aging increasing to 64 h (A64-MPEA), Hc increases dramatically to 21.452 ± 0.057 kA m⁻¹, representing a ~92-fold rise relative to A24. Meanwhile, Ms increases monotonically: 134 ± 1 (SS-MPEA), 142 ± 1 (A24-MPEA), and 158 ± 1 Am² kg⁻¹ (A64-MPEA). This trend reflects changes in intrinsic magnetic behavior: A24-MPEA shows two ferromagnetic phases (Curie temperatures Tc1 = 723 K and Tc2 = 973 K, corresponding to the FCC and high-moment BCC phases), whereas SS and A4 show a single ferromagnetic phase; in A64-MPEA an abnormal increase in magnetization occurs above 723 K, consistent with a "two-phase ferromagnet" mechanism (above Tc1 the FCC becomes paramagnetic and the magnetic response is dominated by the high-Tc, high-moment BCC phase). The yield strength increases continuously with aging: 370 ± 20 (SS), 455 ± 4 (A4), 840 ± 7 (A24), and 2360 ± 27 MPa (A64), with nanoindentation confirming that the BCC + L12 lamellae essentially drive the strengthening. Thus the soft-magnetic A24 shows a significant increase in both Ms and yield strength accompanied by a drastic reduction in coercivity, while the hard-magnetic A64 exhibits simultaneous enhancements in Ms, coercivity, and strength.
时效 24 h 后（A24-MPEA），矫顽力 Hc 相比固溶态下降近一个数量级，从 2.390 ± 0.025 降至 0.233 ± 0.094 kA·m⁻¹。当时效延长到 64 h（A64-MPEA），Hc 急剧增至 21.452 ± 0.057 kA·m⁻¹，相对 A24 提高约 92 倍。与此同时，Ms 单调升高：134 ± 1（SS-MPEA）、142 ± 1（A24-MPEA）、158 ± 1 Am²·kg⁻¹（A64-MPEA）。这一趋势反映了本征磁行为的变化：A24-MPEA 出现两个铁磁相（居里温度 Tc1 = 723 K、Tc2 = 973 K，分别对应 FCC 和高磁矩 BCC 相），而 SS 和 A4 为单一铁磁相；A64-MPEA 在 723 K 以上磁化强度反常升高，符合"两相铁磁体"机制（Tc1 以上 FCC 变为顺磁，磁响应由高 Tc、高磁矩的 BCC 相主导）。屈服强度随时效持续升高：370 ± 20（SS）、455 ± 4（A4）、840 ± 7（A24）、2360 ± 27 MPa（A64），纳米压痕证实 BCC + L12 片层实质上主导了这种强化。因此，软磁的 A24 在 Ms 和屈服强度显著提高的同时矫顽力急剧下降，而硬磁的 A64 则在 Ms、矫顽力和强度上同步增强。
The tunable magnetic properties are highlighted by comparison with representative materials: A24-MPEA achieves relatively high Ms and low Hc, with an Ms superior to amorphous and nanocrystalline alloys (though still below Fe-Co and Fe-Si), and a relatively high electrical resistivity ρ of 86 μΩ·cm (exceeding conventional Fe-Co and Fe-Si alloys, attributed to synergistic electron scattering from multiple crystal defects and the high density of phase boundaries in the nano-lamellar heterostructure that reduces the electron mean free path—advantageous for minimizing eddy-current losses). The A64-MPEA exhibits an excellent combination of extremely high Ms (158 ± 1 Am² kg⁻¹) and moderate coercivity (21.452 ± 0.057 kA m⁻¹), classifying it as a hard magnetic material (Hc ≥ 10 kA m⁻¹); its Ms exceeds existing commercial permanent magnets and most reported MPEAs. However, the extensive precipitation of the BCC phase inevitably leads to compressive brittleness, so the broad trade-off between magnetic and mechanical performance persists. Notably, this single-precursor paradigm offers distinct manufacturing advantages: by decoupling shaping from hardening, complex components can be machined in the relatively ductile precursor and then given a final aging treatment to impart hard-magnetic properties and high strength—circumventing the machinability constraints of brittle magnets such as sintered NdFeB.
通过与代表性材料对比可凸显这种可调磁性：A24-MPEA 兼具较高 Ms 和低 Hc，其 Ms 优于非晶和纳米晶合金（虽仍低于 Fe-Co 和 Fe-Si），并具有较高的电阻率 ρ（86 μΩ·cm，超过传统 Fe-Co 和 Fe-Si 合金）——这归因于多种晶体缺陷带来的协同电子散射，以及纳米片层异质结构中高密度相界减小电子平均自由程（有利于降低涡流损耗）。A64-MPEA 兼具极高 Ms（158 ± 1 Am²·kg⁻¹）和中等矫顽力（21.452 ± 0.057 kA·m⁻¹）的优异组合，可归类为硬磁材料（Hc ≥ 10 kA·m⁻¹）；其 Ms 超过现有商用永磁体和多数已报道的 MPEA。然而，BCC 相的大量析出不可避免地带来压缩脆性，因此磁性与力学性能之间的总体权衡依然存在。值得注意的是，这种单前驱体范式带来独特的制造优势：通过将成形与硬化解耦，复杂部件可在相对韧性的前驱体上加工，再经最终时效处理赋予硬磁性能和高强度——从而规避了烧结 NdFeB 等脆性磁体的可加工性限制。

3.3 磁态可调的机理3.3 Mechanisms for tunable magnetic states

To elucidate the mechanisms, the magnetocrystalline anisotropy constant K1 was calculated via the law of approach to saturation, M = Ms(1 − a/H − b/H² − …) + χp·H with b = (8/105)(K1²/μ0²Ms²). The K1 values rise monotonically: 5.2, 6.8, 9.9, and 18.3 kJ m⁻³ for SS, A4, A24, and A64. The significant enhancement in effective anisotropy in A64 is primarily attributed to the microstructural evolution—a dense, percolating network of high-aspect-ratio BCC nano-lamellae introducing strong shape anisotropy alongside the intrinsic magnetocrystalline anisotropy. In-situ Lorentz TEM tracks domain evolution. The broad tunability is attributed to the evolving role of the high-moment, Fe-Co-rich BCC phase, which transitions from a soft magnetic nucleation agent into a hard magnetic pinning network. Upon short-term aging (A4), ~2 nm coherent L12 precipitates partially alleviate internal stress; chemical ordering enhances magnetocrystalline anisotropy, slightly increasing coercivity to 2.414 kA m⁻¹ (competition between stress-relaxation softening and order-induced hardening).
为阐明机理，通过趋近饱和定律计算磁晶各向异性常数 K1：M = Ms(1 − a/H − b/H² − …) + χp·H，其中 b = (8/105)(K1²/μ0²Ms²)。K1 值单调上升：SS、A4、A24、A64 分别为 5.2、6.8、9.9、18.3 kJ·m⁻³。A64 中有效各向异性的显著增强，主要归因于组织演化——高长径比 BCC 纳米片层形成致密贯通的网络，在本征磁晶各向异性之外引入了强形状各向异性。原位洛伦兹 TEM 跟踪了磁畴演化。这种宽广的可调性归因于高磁矩、富 Fe-Co 的 BCC 相角色的演变——它从软磁形核剂转变为硬磁钉扎网络。短时时效后（A4），约 2 nm 的共格 L12 析出相部分缓解内应力；化学有序化增强磁晶各向异性，使矫顽力略升至 2.414 kA·m⁻¹（应力松弛软化与有序诱导硬化之间的竞争）。
Prolonged aging for 24 h (A24) induces profound magnetic softening, with coercivity dropping to an ultra-low 0.233 kA m⁻¹ via two synergistic mechanisms. First, effective averaging of local magnetocrystalline anisotropy, dictated by the fine scale of the alternating BCC and L12 nano-lamellae: the domain-wall widths δw = π(Aex/K1)^(1/2) are 111 nm (BCC) and 163 nm (FCC), much larger than the physical dimensions of the BCC nano-lamellae (average width ~15 nm, length ~47 nm), so the domain walls span multiple lamellae and effectively average out local anisotropy fluctuations, greatly reducing pinning. Second, and more dominant, exchange-coupled softening: bulk-sample measurements show the nano-lamellar BCC phase has a higher Ms (252 Am² kg⁻¹) and lower Hc, while the L12 lamellae are paramagnetic. The BCC lamellae act as "soft channels" that drag cooperative magnetization reversal of the FCC matrix (an exchange-spring mechanism), serving as effective nucleation sites; in-situ Lorentz TEM confirms that domain walls associated with the BCC nano-lamellae exhibit exceptionally high mobility. A24-MPEA shows minimal hysteresis at low fields but significantly larger hysteresis at high fields—a hallmark of exchange-coupled systems.
延长时效至 24 h（A24）引发深度磁软化，矫顽力通过两种协同机制降至超低的 0.233 kA·m⁻¹。其一，由交替 BCC 与 L12 纳米片层的细小尺度所决定的局部磁晶各向异性有效平均：畴壁宽度 δw = π(Aex/K1)^(1/2) 分别为 111 nm（BCC）和 163 nm（FCC），远大于 BCC 纳米片层的物理尺寸（平均宽约 15 nm、长约 47 nm），因此畴壁横跨多个片层，有效地平均掉局部各向异性起伏，大幅减少钉扎。其二（也是更主导的）为交换耦合软化：块体样品测量显示纳米片层 BCC 相具有更高的 Ms（252 Am²·kg⁻¹）和更低的 Hc，而 L12 片层为顺磁性。BCC 片层充当"软通道"，拖动 FCC 基体的协同磁化反转（交换弹簧机制），充当有效形核位点；原位洛伦兹 TEM 证实与 BCC 纳米片层相关的畴壁具有异常高的可动性。A24-MPEA 在低场下磁滞极小，但在高场区磁滞明显增大——这是交换耦合体系的标志性特征。
In contrast, prolonged aging to 64 h (A64) results in dramatic magnetic hardening, with coercivity increasing to ~21.452 kA m⁻¹. This signals a fundamental shift in the role of the BCC phase, which transitions from a softening to a potent hardening agent. This hardening is governed by a dual mechanism. The primary mechanism is strong domain wall pinning: as the BCC fraction increases to ~67%, it forms a dense, percolating nano-lamellar network creating a high density of interfaces between the high-moment BCC and low-moment L12; within this network, the paramagnetic L12 lamellae, functioning as non-magnetic planar defects, create local energy wells that strongly impede domain-wall motion. Concurrently, the continuous FeCo-rich BCC lamellae physically isolate the phase and decouple the direct exchange interactions between adjacent ferromagnetic regions—this disruption of exchange softening yields a reversal process dominated by pinning (confirmed by in-situ Lorentz TEM, where domain walls are firmly immobilized by the dense network). The second mechanism is shape anisotropy: the BCC nano-lamellae have a high aspect ratio (~35), and magnetostatic energy is minimized when magnetization aligns along their longest axis, compelling the magnetization within the high-Ms BCC to preferentially align along the length. This preferred orientation introduces a strong uniaxial anisotropy—an energy barrier to rotation that directly increases coercivity (analogous to classic AlNiCo magnets). K1 rises monotonically to 18.3 kJ m⁻³ in A64.
相反，延长时效至 64 h（A64）导致显著的磁硬化，矫顽力增至约 21.452 kA·m⁻¹。这标志着 BCC 相角色的根本转变——从软化剂变为强效硬化剂。这种硬化受双重机制支配。主要机制为强畴壁钉扎：随 BCC 分数增至约 67%，形成致密贯通的纳米片层网络，在高磁矩 BCC 与低磁矩 L12 之间创造出高密度界面；在该网络内，顺磁性 L12 片层作为非磁性平面缺陷，形成强烈阻碍畴壁运动的局部能量陷阱。与此同时，连续的富 FeCo 的 BCC 片层将该相物理隔离，并解耦相邻铁磁区之间的直接交换相互作用——这种对交换软化的破坏使反转过程由钉扎主导（原位洛伦兹 TEM 证实畴壁被致密网络牢固固定）。第二种机制是形状各向异性：BCC 纳米片层长径比高（约 35），当磁化沿其最长轴取向时静磁能最小，从而迫使高 Ms 的 BCC 内磁化优先沿长度方向排列。这种择优取向引入强单轴各向异性——即阻碍转动的能垒，直接增大矫顽力（与经典 AlNiCo 磁体类似）。A64 中 K1 单调升至 18.3 kJ·m⁻³。
It is worth noting that, although A64-MPEA achieves a robust hard magnetic state, conventional spinodal alloys with lower compositional complexity (e.g., FeCrCo-based systems, and laser powder bed fusion with tailored spinodal decomposition) also exhibit remarkable hard-magnetic and broad magnetic tunability. However, this single MPEA precursor undergoes a distinctly non-monotonic magnetic transition: first reaching an ultra-soft state (~0.233 kA m⁻¹) at the intermediate aging stage, then transforming to a hard state (~21.452 kA m⁻¹) after prolonged aging. Crucially, this magnetic evolution is accompanied by a simultaneous increase in both saturation magnetization and yield strength. A common limitation of precipitation-strengthened soft-magnetic MPEAs is the reduction in Ms due to dilution from non-magnetic elements; here, chemical partitioning results in Fe-Co-rich BCC and Ni/Al/Ti-rich L12, and since the BCC phase has a higher inherent magnetic moment (bulk BCC Ms = 252 Am² kg⁻¹), increasing the BCC fraction enhances rather than reduces Ms. This multi-phase synergy fundamentally differs from extrinsic composite strategies: the bulk alloy achieves broad magnetic tunability and high yield strength entirely through intrinsic phase decomposition, without extrinsic magnetic fillers or the associated property compromises.
值得注意的是，尽管 A64-MPEA 实现了稳健的硬磁态，成分复杂度较低的传统调幅分解合金（如 FeCrCo 基体系，以及带有定制调幅分解的激光粉末床熔融）同样表现出出色的硬磁性和宽广的磁性可调性。然而，这一单一 MPEA 前驱体经历了明显的非单调磁转变：在中间时效阶段先达到超软态（约 0.233 kA·m⁻¹），延长时效后再转变为硬态（约 21.452 kA·m⁻¹）。关键在于，这种磁演化伴随着饱和磁化强度和屈服强度的同步提高。析出强化软磁 MPEA 的常见局限是非磁性元素稀释导致 Ms 下降；而这里，化学分配形成富 Fe-Co 的 BCC 和富 Ni/Al/Ti 的 L12，由于 BCC 相具有更高的本征磁矩（块体 BCC 的 Ms = 252 Am²·kg⁻¹），增大 BCC 分数反而提升而非降低 Ms。这种多相协同与外源复合策略有本质区别：块体合金完全通过本征相分解实现宽广的磁性可调性和高屈服强度，无需外源磁性填料，也没有随之而来的性能折衷。

4. 结论4. Conclusions

In summary, we have achieved a synergistic optimization among coercivity, saturation magnetization, and yield strength by establishing a "composition-invariant, structure-tailorable" paradigm in the Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 MPEA. Through systematically varying the aging time to regulate the structural evolution of the nano-lamellar heterostructures, we achieved deterministic switching between distinct magnetic states from a single precursor. The alloy exhibits a unique non-monotonic magnetic transition: it evolves from an initial semi-hard magnetic state (−2.39 kA m⁻¹), first into an ultra-soft magnetic state (−0.233 kA m⁻¹), and subsequently into a robust hard magnetic state (−21.452 kA m⁻¹), spanning two orders of magnitude in coercivity. Crucially, this broad tunability is accompanied by a synergistic enhancement in both yield strength and saturation magnetization (reaching 158 Am² kg⁻¹), effectively mitigating the canonical magnetic dilution problem inherent in precipitation-strengthened alloys. We have elucidated that this unprecedented performance stems from the distinct functional evolution of the high-moment FeCo-rich nano-lamellae, which transition from acting as exchange-coupling channels that facilitate magnetization reversal (softening mechanism) to serving as a potent domain-wall pinning network enriched with strong shape anisotropy (hardening mechanism). This work provides direct, in-situ evidence of how complex multiphase architectures govern domain wall dynamics, offering a comprehensive understanding of the structure–property relationship, and provides a viable pathway for designing high-performance magnetic alloys for next-generation electromagnetic applications, ranging from load-bearing high-speed motors to information storage systems.
综上所述，我们通过在 Fe30Co30Ni30Al5Ti5 MPEA 中确立"成分不变、结构可裁"范式，实现了矫顽力、饱和磁化强度和屈服强度之间的协同优化。通过系统改变时效时间来调控纳米片层异质结构的结构演化，我们从单一前驱体实现了不同磁态之间的确定性切换。该合金呈现独特的非单调磁转变：从初始的半硬磁态（−2.39 kA·m⁻¹），先进入超软磁态（−0.233 kA·m⁻¹），随后进入稳健的硬磁态（−21.452 kA·m⁻¹），矫顽力跨越两个数量级。关键在于，这种宽广的可调性伴随着屈服强度和饱和磁化强度的协同提升（Ms 达 158 Am²·kg⁻¹），有效缓解了析出强化合金固有的典型磁稀释问题。我们阐明，这一前所未有的性能源自高磁矩富 FeCo 纳米片层不同的功能演变——它从充当促进磁化反转的交换耦合通道（软化机制），转变为富含强形状各向异性的强效畴壁钉扎网络（硬化机制）。本工作提供了复杂多相构型如何支配畴壁动力学的直接原位证据，给出对结构–性能关系的全面理解，并为设计面向下一代电磁应用（从承载型高速电机到信息存储系统）的高性能磁性合金提供了可行路径。

Advanced Materials · 2024

强而韧的高熵合金中的大非磁滞体积磁致伸缩Large Nonhysteretic Volume Magnetostriction in a Strong and Ductile High-Entropy Alloy

Adv. Mater. 2024, 36, 2404192 · doi:10.1002/adma.202404192 · Junming Gou, Yun Pan, Tianzi Yang, Yao Liu, Guoxin Liu, Ying Chen, Changsheng Zhang, Hao Li, Bojiang Lv, Chang Liu, Weixing Xia, Tianyu Ma
本页为「摘要 + 正文」中文翻译（含引言、结果与讨论 2.1–2.5、结论）；已省略 Experimental Section（方法）、支持信息与参考文献。默认显示中文，可用右上角按钮切换「对照 / EN」。
摘要 / Abstract
Rapid development of smart technologies poses a big challenge for magnetostrictive materials, which should not only permit isotropic and hysteresis-free actuation (i.e., nonhysteretic volume change) in magnetic fields, but also have high strength and high ductility. Unfortunately, the magnetostriction from self-assembly of ferromagnetic domains is volume-conserving; the volume magnetostriction from field-induced first-order phase transition has large intrinsic hysteresis; and most prototype magnetostrictive materials are intrinsically brittle. Here, a magnetic high-entropy alloy (HEA) Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ is reported that can rectify these challenges, exhibiting an unprecedented combination of large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction, high tensile strength and large elongation strain, over a wide working temperature range from room temperature down to 100 K. Its exceptional properties stem from a dual-phase microstructure, where the face-centered cubic (FCC) matrix phase with nanoscale compositional and structural fluctuations can enable a magnetic-field-induced transition from low-spin small-volume state to high-spin large-volume state, and the ordered body-centered cubic (BCC) B2 phase contributes to mechanical strengthening. The present findings may provide insights into designing unconventional and technologically important magnetostrictive materials.
智能技术的快速发展给磁致伸缩材料带来巨大挑战：这类材料不仅要能在磁场中实现各向同性、无磁滞的驱动（即无磁滞的体积变化），还要兼具高强度和高塑性。遗憾的是，来自铁磁畴自组织的磁致伸缩是体积守恒的；来自磁场诱导一级相变的体积磁致伸缩具有很大的本征磁滞；而大多数典型磁致伸缩材料又本征脆性。本文报道了一种磁性高熵合金（HEA）Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀，它能够破解上述难题——在从室温直到 100 K 的宽温度区间内，同时呈现大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩、高抗拉强度和大延伸应变，这一组合前所未有。其卓越性能源于一种双相组织：具有纳米尺度成分与结构涨落的面心立方（FCC）基体相，能够实现磁场诱导的从低自旋小体积态到高自旋大体积态的转变；而有序体心立方（BCC）的 B2 相则贡献力学强化。本研究可为设计非常规且具有重要技术价值的磁致伸缩材料提供启示。

引言1. Introduction

Magnetostrictive materials that can promptly change their shape under external magnetic fields have a variety of technological applications, from deep-sea exploration to smart robotics and communication satellites. High-performance engineering applications, such as high-precision servo valves and magnetic seals, require that the magnetostrictive components not only produce large and nonhysteresis actuations along longitudinal and transverse directions over a wide temperature range, but also have strong tolerance to mechanical loadings. The magnetostriction from re-orientation of ferromagnetic and/or ferroelastic domains is volume-conserving (e.g., Fe–Ga alloys), i.e., the magnets elongate along longitudinal direction and contract along transverse direction simultaneously in a magnetic field, known as the Joule magnetostriction effect (hereafter called Joulian ferromagnets). Besides, most remarkable magnetostrictive materials of this type, e.g., REFe₂-type (RE = rare earth) intermetallic compounds, are intrinsically brittle. In contrast, volume magnetostriction has been achieved in alloys with magnetic-field-induced first-order phase transition between parent and product phases with large differences in magnetization and unit cell volume, such as Fe–Rh and Ni–Co–Mn–Ti alloys. Though some alloys (e.g., Fe–Rh) are relatively ductile, the nature of first-order phase transition indicates a pronounced hysteresis and limited operating temperature range of the magnetostriction. The hysteresis is harmful for the recoverability of stored energy and precise control. Nevertheless, these wisdoms preclude achieving large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction, and novel solutions to rectify the big challenge need to be exploited.
能在外磁场下迅速改变形状的磁致伸缩材料具有广泛的技术应用，从深海勘探到智能机器人和通信卫星。高性能工程应用（如高精度伺服阀和磁密封件）要求磁致伸缩元件不仅要在宽温区内沿纵向和横向产生大而无磁滞的驱动，还要对机械载荷有很强的耐受性。来自铁磁畴和/或铁弹畴重取向的磁致伸缩是体积守恒的（例如 Fe–Ga 合金），即磁体在磁场中沿纵向伸长、同时沿横向收缩，这被称为焦耳磁致伸缩效应（下文称焦耳型铁磁体）。此外，这类材料中最出色的磁致伸缩材料——例如 REFe₂ 型（RE = 稀土）金属间化合物——本征脆性。相比之下，体积磁致伸缩已在具有磁场诱导一级相变的合金中实现，其母相与产物相之间在磁化强度和晶胞体积上差异很大，例如 Fe–Rh 和 Ni–Co–Mn–Ti 合金。尽管有些合金（如 Fe–Rh）相对较韧，但一级相变的本质决定了磁致伸缩存在显著磁滞和有限的工作温度范围。磁滞不利于储能的可回复性和精确控制。因此，这些传统思路都无法实现大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩，亟需开发破解这一重大难题的新方案。
In the last decade, design of high-entropy alloys (HEAs), a new class of materials, has been one of the most appealing strategies for achieving fruitful macroscopic properties, and the most intriguing concept in material science and metallurgical engineering. Numerous studies have shown that the HEAs are promising candidates for structural applications due to their high strength and ductility, excellent resistance to high-temperature softening and low-temperature brittleness, and strong fatigue and creep resistance. Besides its success in structural materials, the high-configurational-entropy strategy has been recently utilized to exploit novel functional properties. For instance, the remarkable Fe–Mn–Al–Ni-based high-entropy shape memory alloys (HESMAs) possess much wider temperature range of superelasticity than the conventional Ti–Ni alloys and extremely weak temperature dependence of superelastic stress, and the Ti–Zr–Hf–Fe–Ni HEA exhibits wide temperature range Elinvar effect and large elastic strain limit that are difficult to be achieved in conventional metals and alloys. Meanwhile, advanced magnetic HEAs, such as strong and ductile soft magnets, hard magnets, and magnetocaloric materials, have also been exploited. However, up to now, it is still rare to utilize the potentially unique spin–lattice coupling in magnetic HEAs for achieving novel magnetostriction properties. Previous investigations have shown that HEAs intrinsically possess nanoscale chemical fluctuation and severe local lattice distortion (LLD), which could result in localized spin fluctuations, i.e., the magnitude and orientation of magnetic moments are nonuniform. A magnetic HEA, simultaneously possessing these heterogeneous blocks and strong spin–lattice coupling effect, has the opportunity to exhibit the desired nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction, where the spin and lattice are simultaneously varied by external magnetic fields.
过去十年，高熵合金（HEA）这一新型材料的设计已成为获得丰富宏观性能最具吸引力的策略之一，也是材料科学与冶金工程中最引人入胜的概念。大量研究表明，高熵合金凭借其高强度和高塑性、优异的抗高温软化与抗低温脆化能力，以及强的抗疲劳与抗蠕变性能，是结构应用的有力候选。除在结构材料上的成功外，高构型熵策略近来也被用于开发新颖的功能特性。例如，出色的 Fe–Mn–Al–Ni 基高熵形状记忆合金（HESMA）相比传统 Ti–Ni 合金具有宽得多的超弹性温度范围，且超弹性应力的温度依赖性极弱；Ti–Zr–Hf–Fe–Ni 高熵合金则表现出宽温区的 Elinvar 效应和大的弹性应变极限，这些在传统金属与合金中都难以实现。与此同时，先进磁性高熵合金——如强而韧的软磁体、硬磁体和磁热材料——也已被开发。然而迄今为止，利用磁性高熵合金中潜在独特的自旋–晶格耦合来实现新颖磁致伸缩性能的研究仍很少。已有研究表明，高熵合金本征地具有纳米尺度化学涨落和严重的局部晶格畸变（LLD），这会导致局域自旋涨落，即磁矩的大小和取向不均匀。一种同时具备这些异质结构单元和强自旋–晶格耦合效应的磁性高熵合金，就有机会呈现所需的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩——其自旋与晶格能被外磁场同时改变。
Herein, we surprisingly found that a high-entropy alloy Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ (named as “Ni10-HEA”) can exhibit large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction over a wide temperature range (298 K down to 100 K), which is not allowed either in conventional Joulian ferromagnets or in materials with a magnetic-field-induced first-order phase transition. This alloy also has high tensile strength and good tensile ductility over this temperature range, superior to the conventional magnetostrictive materials. Such an exceptional combination makes it a promising candidate for high-performance magnetostrictive components in applications with wide temperature span.
本文中，我们惊奇地发现高熵合金 Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀（命名为“Ni10-HEA”）能在宽温度区间（298 K 直到 100 K）内呈现大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩——这在传统焦耳型铁磁体或具有磁场诱导一级相变的材料中都是不被允许的。该合金在此温区还具有高抗拉强度和良好的拉伸塑性，优于传统磁致伸缩材料。如此卓越的性能组合，使其成为宽温跨度应用中高性能磁致伸缩元件的有力候选材料。

结果与讨论2. Results and Discussion

微观组织表征2.1. Microstructural Characterization

The XRD pattern of the present high-entropy alloy Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ (Figure 1a) reveals a dual-phase microstructure, containing the dominant FCC phase and a very small amount of B2 (ordered BCC) phase. The Rietveld refinement results show that the lattice parameter is a = 3.59474(3) Å for the FCC phase and a = 2.86614(3) Å for the B2 phase, and the mass fraction is 98.2% for the FCC matrix and 1.8% for the B2 phase, respectively. Figure 1b is the EBSD inverse pole figure (IPF) of Ni10-HEA, where micron-sized FCC grains are randomly oriented without obvious texture. As shown in Figure 1c, the B2 phase is mainly distributed along FCC grain boundaries (GBs), which can be seen more clearly in the inset close-up view. In Figure 1d, a typical TEM bright-field image displays the dominant FCC phase and the intergranular B2 phase, which are identified by the corresponding diffraction patterns with [110]FCC and [100]B2 zone axes, respectively. Figure 1e shows the low-magnification STEM-EDS mappings, where the FCC phase is enriched with Fe and Cr, and the B2 phase is enriched with Al and Ni. A similar phenomenon of element co-aggregation has previously been reported in AlCoCrFeNi₂.₁ HEA with FCC + B2 dual-phase structure. Density variations can be seen for all the constituting elements (Fe, Co, Cr, Al, and Ni) in the FCC phase, indicating the existence of local compositional fluctuations. High-resolution TEM observation (Figure 1f) shows that the FCC phase possesses local lattice distortion (i.e., atoms deviated from their ideal positions), which is presumably resulted from the composition-induced atomic size mismatch and/or interatomic charge transition. The inset close-up view shows that the atoms within the FCC lattice are not strictly aligned along the [1̅10] direction. The corresponding geometric phase analysis (GPA, Figure 1g) reconstructs the local strain distribution, which exhibits intense strain fluctuations at nanoscale. Figure 1h shows the high-magnification STEM-EDS mappings of the FCC matrix, which display strong compositional fluctuations at nanoscale. This line-scanning element analysis (Figure 1i) further reveals that Fe and Co have opposite fluctuation tendencies, whereas Al, Ni and Cr fluctuate randomly with a similar degree of heterogeneity. In high-entropy alloys, strong nanoscale strain and compositional fluctuations have usually been observed, e.g., in the FCC-structured CrMnFeCoNi and CrFeCoNiPd HEAs.
本高熵合金 Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ 的 XRD 图谱（图 1a）显示出一种双相组织，包含占主导的 FCC 相和极少量的 B2（有序 BCC）相。Rietveld 精修结果表明，FCC 相的晶格常数 a = 3.59474(3) Å，B2 相的晶格常数 a = 2.86614(3) Å；FCC 基体的质量分数为 98.2%，B2 相为 1.8%。图 1b 是 Ni10-HEA 的 EBSD 反极图（IPF），其中微米级 FCC 晶粒随机取向，无明显织构。如图 1c 所示，B2 相主要沿 FCC 晶界（GB）分布，在插图放大视图中可更清晰地看到。图 1d 中，典型的 TEM 明场像显示了占主导的 FCC 相和晶界处的 B2 相，二者分别由 [110]FCC 和 [100]B2 晶带轴的相应衍射花样加以确认。图 1e 展示了低倍 STEM-EDS 元素分布图，其中 FCC 相富集 Fe 和 Cr，B2 相富集 Al 和 Ni。这种元素共聚集的类似现象，此前曾在具有 FCC + B2 双相结构的 AlCoCrFeNi₂.₁ 高熵合金中被报道。在 FCC 相中，所有组成元素（Fe、Co、Cr、Al、Ni）都可见密度变化，表明存在局部成分涨落。高分辨 TEM 观察（图 1f）显示 FCC 相存在局部晶格畸变（即原子偏离其理想位置），这大概源于成分导致的原子尺寸失配和/或原子间电荷转移。插图放大视图显示，FCC 晶格内的原子并未严格沿 [1̅10] 方向排列。相应的几何相位分析（GPA，图 1g）重构了局部应变分布，呈现出纳米尺度的强烈应变涨落。图 1h 给出了 FCC 基体的高倍 STEM-EDS 元素分布图，显示纳米尺度的强成分涨落。该线扫描元素分析（图 1i）进一步揭示，Fe 与 Co 具有相反的涨落趋势，而 Al、Ni、Cr 则以相近的不均匀程度随机涨落。在高熵合金中，通常都能观察到纳米尺度的强应变和成分涨落，例如在 FCC 结构的 CrMnFeCoNi 和 CrFeCoNiPd 高熵合金中。

特殊磁转变中的反常现象2.2. Anomalies in the Specific Magnetic Transition

Shown in Figure 2a are the magnetization–temperature (M–T) curves of Ni10-HEA, for which the Curie temperature TC is determined by the conventional “two-tangent” method. Interestingly, with the increase of hydrostatic pressure, the ferromagnetic ordering of this alloy becomes much more difficult. Its TC decreases from ≈330 K under 0 MPa, to ≈298 K under 396 MPa, and then to ≈275 K under 950 MPa, and its average sensitivity coefficient of TC (ΔTC/ΔP) is about −57.9 K GPa⁻¹. Such a phenomenon is not seen in conventional Joulian ferromagnets with nearly pressure-independent TC; for instance, the sensitivity coefficient of TC is nearly zero in pure iron and 3.6 K GPa⁻¹ in pure nickel. Hence, it indicates that the present Ni10-HEA possesses a special magnetic transition. Associated with it, this alloy also exhibits abnormal thermal expansion behavior. As shown in Figure 2b, its linear thermal expansion behaves normally above TC, exhibiting positive thermal expansion (PTE). However, below TC, its coefficient of thermal expansion (CTE) decreases apparently, and the cooling and heating thermal expansion curves exhibit slight mismatching. Such a phenomenon suggests that the specific magnetic transition in Ni10-HEA is accompanied by a transition from a high-temperature large-volume state to a low-temperature small-volume state, which partially compensates the volume contraction induced by ever-present atomic vibration. Figure 2c shows the temperature-dependent internal friction (tan δ) curves of Ni10-HEA, where tan δ starts to increase below the magnetic transition near TC, and internal friction peaks have been detected. The inset temperature-dependent storage modulus exhibits the Elinvar-like effect in the temperature range of 225–240 K. This phenomenon cannot be explained by the stress-induced motion of non-180° domain walls (DWs) in Joulian ferromagnets. Anomalies similar to the above measurements have been frequently observed in intermetallic compounds with first-order magneto-structural phase transition. However, in the present Ni10-HEA, neither the DSC curve (upper-left inset of Figure 2b) nor the resistivity–temperature (R–T) curve (bottom-right inset of Figure 2b) can detect the trace for a typical first-order phase transition. In situ XRD cooling patterns (Figure 2d) reveal that its average structure keeps unchanged upon cooling. Figure 2e shows the in situ TEM observations of the dominant FCC phase, where extra spots (i.e., satellite or incommensurate spots) are absent in the diffraction patterns with [110]FCC zone axes, and no newly formed phase can be observed in the TEM bright-field images. Thus, the anomalies during the magnetic transition in Ni10-HEA cannot be attributed to a typical first-order phase transition.
图 2a 给出了 Ni10-HEA 的磁化强度–温度（M–T）曲线，其居里温度 TC 由传统的“双切线”方法确定。有趣的是，随着静水压力增大，该合金的铁磁有序变得困难得多。其 TC 从 0 MPa 下的约 330 K，降至 396 MPa 下的约 298 K，再降至 950 MPa 下的约 275 K，TC 的平均敏感系数（ΔTC/ΔP）约为 −57.9 K GPa⁻¹。这种现象在 TC 几乎与压力无关的传统焦耳型铁磁体中并不出现；例如，纯铁的 TC 敏感系数近乎为零，纯镍为 3.6 K GPa⁻¹。因此，这表明本 Ni10-HEA 具有一种特殊的磁转变。与之相关，该合金还表现出反常的热膨胀行为。如图 2b 所示，其线膨胀在 TC 以上表现正常，呈现正热膨胀（PTE）；但在 TC 以下，其热膨胀系数（CTE）明显减小，且降温与升温的热膨胀曲线出现轻微不吻合。这种现象表明，Ni10-HEA 中的特殊磁转变伴随着从高温大体积态到低温小体积态的转变，从而部分补偿了始终存在的原子振动所引起的体积收缩。图 2c 给出了 Ni10-HEA 随温度变化的内耗（tan δ）曲线，其中 tan δ 在 TC 附近的磁转变以下开始增大，并检测到内耗峰。插图中随温度变化的储能模量在 225–240 K 温区呈现 Elinvar 样效应。这一现象无法用焦耳型铁磁体中非 180° 畴壁（DW）的应力诱导运动来解释。与上述测量类似的反常现象，常见于具有一级磁–结构相变的金属间化合物中。然而在本 Ni10-HEA 中，无论是 DSC 曲线（图 2b 左上插图）还是电阻率–温度（R–T）曲线（图 2b 右下插图），都检测不到典型一级相变的痕迹。原位 XRD 降温图谱（图 2d）表明其平均结构在降温过程中保持不变。图 2e 给出了占主导的 FCC 相的原位 TEM 观察：在 [110]FCC 晶带轴的衍射花样中没有额外斑点（即卫星斑或非公度斑），TEM 明场像中也观察不到新生成相。因此，Ni10-HEA 磁转变过程中的反常现象不能归因于典型的一级相变。

特殊磁态的研究2.3. Investigation of the Specific Magnetic State

It is noted that the Fe–Ni Invar alloy also exhibits strongly pressure-dependent TC (−35 K GPa⁻¹), abnormal thermal expansion behavior, and modulus softening effect, like the present Ni10-HEA. Previous ab initio calculations have suggested that the Fe–Ni Invar alloy situates in a noncollinear magnetic configuration, which has the following features: i) magnetic moments at individual sites in the crystal are not uniformly aligned along the direction of global magnetization; ii) magnitudes of the magnetic moments are varied; iii) at ground state, the noncollinear state possesses lower average magnetization and smaller lattice volume than the ideal linear ferromagnetic state, but is more energetically favorable. Hence, its Invar effect (i.e., nearly zero thermal expansion) can be attributed to the thermally induced transition from a high-spin large-volume (HS) state to a low-spin small-volume (LS) state in the noncollinear configuration, and this transition is significantly restrained by applying high hydrostatic pressure due to the spin–lattice coupling effect. The differences between noncollinear and linear ferromagnetic states are schematically illustrated in Figure S1, Supporting Information. The present Ni10-HEA may have a tendency toward the similar noncollinear magnetic state, because it possesses nanoscale lattice distortion and compositional fluctuations of solute atoms with distinct magnetism (e.g., nonmagnetic Al and antiferromagnetic Cr, as revealed in Figure 1), which helps to strengthen the disorder degree of spin.
值得注意的是，Fe–Ni 因瓦（Invar）合金也表现出强压力依赖的 TC（−35 K GPa⁻¹）、反常热膨胀行为和模量软化效应，与本 Ni10-HEA 相似。此前的第一性原理计算表明，Fe–Ni 因瓦合金处于一种非共线磁构型，具有以下特征：(i) 晶体中各个格点上的磁矩并非沿整体磁化方向均匀排列；(ii) 磁矩的大小各不相同；(iii) 在基态，非共线态相比理想线性铁磁态具有更低的平均磁化强度和更小的晶格体积，但在能量上更有利。因此，其因瓦效应（即近乎零热膨胀）可归因于非共线构型中由热诱导的从高自旋大体积（HS）态到低自旋小体积（LS）态的转变，而由于自旋–晶格耦合效应，施加高静水压力会显著抑制这一转变。非共线态与线性铁磁态之间的差异示意见支持信息图 S1。本 Ni10-HEA 可能具有趋向类似非共线磁态的倾向，因为它具有纳米尺度的晶格畸变，以及磁性各异的溶质原子（例如非磁性的 Al 和反铁磁性的 Cr，如图 1 所揭示）的成分涨落，这有助于增强自旋的无序程度。
Figure 3a shows the in situ neutron diffraction (ND) patterns of a Ni10-HEA cylinder sample. Notably, the diffraction intensities I of different FCC planes are varied with temperature. As shown in the close-up views, the ratio of I200K to I298K is 1.138, 0.834, 1.235, and 1.095 for the (111)FCC, (200)FCC, (220)FCC, and (311)FCC planes, respectively. According to the in situ XRD results (Figure 2d), the thermally induced structural transition is absent in the measured temperature range. Thus, the temperature-dependent ND diffraction intensities are attributed to the magnetic contribution. In the linear ferromagnetic state, only the intensity of the special plane (e.g., the {001} plane for a cubic ferromagnet with <001> easy axis) increases, which signifies that the magnitude of magnetic moments increases along the direction perpendicular to this plane (i.e., along the direction of spontaneous magnetization). However, the present Ni10-HEA exhibits unusual behaviors, for which the intensities of the (111)FCC, (220)FCC, and (311)FCC planes strengthen upon cooling. It indicates that the magnitude of magnetic moments (or the amplitude of spins) increases along the <111>FCC, <110>FCC, and <311>FCC directions simultaneously, which is strictly forbidden in the linear ferromagnetic state. Such a phenomenon reveals that the Ni10-HEA situates in the noncollinear state with varied spin orientations.
图 3a 给出了 Ni10-HEA 圆柱样品的原位中子衍射（ND）图谱。值得注意的是，不同 FCC 晶面的衍射强度 I 随温度变化。如放大视图所示，(111)FCC、(200)FCC、(220)FCC、(311)FCC 晶面的 I200K 与 I298K 之比分别为 1.138、0.834、1.235 和 1.095。根据原位 XRD 结果（图 2d），在测量温区内不存在热诱导的结构转变。因此，随温度变化的 ND 衍射强度归因于磁性贡献。在线性铁磁态中，只有特殊晶面（例如具有 <001> 易磁化轴的立方铁磁体的 {001} 面）的强度增大，这意味着磁矩沿垂直于该面的方向（即自发磁化方向）增大。然而本 Ni10-HEA 表现出反常行为：降温时 (111)FCC、(220)FCC、(311)FCC 晶面的强度都增强。这表明磁矩的大小（或自旋的幅度）沿 <111>FCC、<110>FCC、<311>FCC 方向同时增大，而这在线性铁磁态中是严格禁止的。此现象揭示 Ni10-HEA 处于自旋取向各异的非共线态。
We have also performed the in situ magnetizing Lorentz–TEM electron holograms on Ni10-HEA (Figure 3b), which can enable the visualization of its nanoscale magnetic configuration. Dark-contrast magnetic heterogeneities have been observed, and they evolve down to cryogenic temperature (Figure S2, Supporting Information). Interestingly, applying an external magnetic field enables the evolution of these magnetic heterogeneities. A designated spacing between two dark-contrast regions varies from 385 nm at 0 Oe to 431 nm at 100 Oe and then to 635 nm at 248 Oe. In the corresponding reconstructed phase images (Figure 3c), the white- and black-contrast contour lines align along the directions of local magnetization (as indicated by the green arrows), and the spacing of these contour lines provides information regarding the in-plane magnetic flux density. Interestingly, the local magnetization direction and magnetic flux density in the magnetic heterogeneities (as indicated by red-dashed rectangles) are varied, i.e., the contour lines are not arranged along a specific direction with fixed spacing, unlike those observed in the linear ferromagnets. Applying a small magnetic field leads to the movement of these magnetic heterogeneities, but the varied local magnetization within them cannot be rectified to a linear arrangement. Such a phenomenon suggests that the magnetic heterogeneities are not trapped in fixed lattice positions, and their movement under external field is presumably the result of minimizing the local strain energy and magnetostatic energy. The above in situ characterizations suggest that Ni10-HEA is not an ideal linear ferromagnet, but a noncollinear magnetic state.
我们还对 Ni10-HEA 进行了原位磁化的洛伦兹–TEM 电子全息（图 3b），可将其纳米尺度磁构型可视化。观察到了暗衬度的磁异质区，且它们一直演化到低温（支持信息图 S2）。有趣的是，施加外磁场会促使这些磁异质区演化。两个暗衬度区之间的指定间距从 0 Oe 时的 385 nm，变为 100 Oe 时的 431 nm，再到 248 Oe 时的 635 nm。在相应的重构相位像（图 3c）中，白、黑衬度的等值线沿局部磁化方向排列（如绿色箭头所示），这些等值线的间距提供了面内磁通密度的信息。有趣的是，磁异质区内（红色虚线矩形所示）的局部磁化方向和磁通密度各不相同，即等值线并未沿某一特定方向以固定间距排列，这与线性铁磁体中所观察到的不同。施加小磁场会导致这些磁异质区移动，但其内部各异的局部磁化却无法被整流为线性排列。此现象表明，磁异质区并未被束缚在固定的晶格位置，它们在外场下的移动大概是使局部应变能和静磁能最小化的结果。上述原位表征表明，Ni10-HEA 并非理想的线性铁磁体，而是一种非共线磁态。

磁致伸缩与力学性能2.4. Magnetostrictive and Mechanical Properties

The intriguing microstructure and special magnetic transition enable the present Ni10-HEA a combination of novel magnetostrictive performance and excellent mechanical properties. Figure 4a shows the parallel and perpendicular magnetostriction curves of Ni10-HEA and Fe–Ga alloys. Unlike the Fe–Ga alloy which exhibits typical volume-conserving Joule magnetostriction (i.e., λ∥ has opposite sign to λ⊥), both λ∥ and λ⊥ of Ni10-HEA gradually increase with external magnetic field, and are not saturated until 70 kOe. It reveals that this alloy exhibits a magnetic-field-induced continuous lattice expansion, i.e., volume magnetostriction. Notably, the magnetostriction curves during magnetizing and demagnetizing are nearly overlapped (the inset of Figure 4a), which indicates that its volume magnetostriction is recoverable and nonhysteretic. As shown in Figure 4b, this alloy exhibits large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction over a wide temperature range from room temperature (298 K) down to 100 K. Its volume magnetostriction constant ω under 70 kOe (i.e., λ∥ + 2λ⊥) reaches a maximum at 280 K (the temperature where dM/dH reaches a minimum), and the corresponding temperature-dependent parallel and perpendicular magnetostriction curves are shown in Figure S3, Supporting Information. In comparison with previously reported volume magnetostrictive materials, the present Ni10-HEA possesses the largest peak value of volume magnetostriction coefficient ā (Δω/ΔH) of 234.3 × 10⁻⁴ ppm/Oe. Moreover, this alloy has a large and stable ā at cryogenic temperatures, which highlights its potential applicability for severe environments.
引人入胜的微观组织和特殊磁转变，使本 Ni10-HEA 同时具备新颖的磁致伸缩性能和优异的力学性能。图 4a 给出了 Ni10-HEA 和 Fe–Ga 合金的平行与垂直磁致伸缩曲线。与呈现典型体积守恒焦耳磁致伸缩的 Fe–Ga 合金不同（其 λ∥ 与 λ⊥ 符号相反），Ni10-HEA 的 λ∥ 和 λ⊥ 都随外磁场逐渐增大，直到 70 kOe 仍未饱和。这表明该合金呈现磁场诱导的连续晶格膨胀，即体积磁致伸缩。值得注意的是，磁化与退磁过程中的磁致伸缩曲线几乎重合（图 4a 插图），表明其体积磁致伸缩是可回复且无磁滞的。如图 4b 所示，该合金在从室温（298 K）直到 100 K 的宽温区内呈现大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩。其在 70 kOe 下的体积磁致伸缩常数 ω（即 λ∥ + 2λ⊥）在 280 K（dM/dH 达到最小值的温度）达到最大值，相应随温度变化的平行与垂直磁致伸缩曲线见支持信息图 S3。与此前报道的体积磁致伸缩材料相比，本 Ni10-HEA 具有最大的体积磁致伸缩系数峰值 ā（Δω/ΔH），达 234.3 × 10⁻⁴ ppm/Oe。此外，该合金在低温下具有大而稳定的 ā，凸显了其在严酷环境中的潜在适用性。
The Ni10-HEA also possesses excellent tensile properties over the wide temperature range from 298 K to 100 K, as shown in Figure 4c. At 298 K, this alloy possesses a yield strength σ0.2 of 436 MPa, ultimate tensile strength σUTS of 638 MPa, and tensile elongation δ of 10.8%. With the decrease of temperature, its strength and ductility are improved simultaneously, which suggests that the ductile-to-brittle transition is absent in this alloy. At 100 K, its σ0.2, σUTS, and δ reach 697 MPa, 939 MPa, and 21.5%, respectively, thus having promising cryogenic applications. Most importantly, the tensile properties of Ni10-HEA are much superior to conventional magnetostrictive materials, as shown in Figure 4d. Consequently, the unusual property profile of large and nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction, high strength, good ductility and wide working temperature range may make the present Ni10-HEA appealing for advanced industrial applications.
如图 4c 所示，Ni10-HEA 在从 298 K 到 100 K 的宽温区内还具有优异的拉伸性能。在 298 K，该合金的屈服强度 σ0.2 为 436 MPa，抗拉强度 σUTS 为 638 MPa，拉伸延伸率 δ 为 10.8%。随着温度降低，其强度和塑性同时提升，表明该合金不存在韧脆转变。在 100 K，其 σ0.2、σUTS、δ 分别达到 697 MPa、939 MPa 和 21.5%，因而在低温应用中颇具前景。最重要的是，如图 4d 所示，Ni10-HEA 的拉伸性能远优于传统磁致伸缩材料。因此，大而无磁滞的体积磁致伸缩、高强度、良好塑性和宽工作温区这一非常规的性能组合，可使本 Ni10-HEA 在先进工业应用中颇具吸引力。

宏观性能的机理2.5. On the Mechanisms of Macroscopic Performances

We have noted that the nanoscale lattice distortion and compositional fluctuations within the FCC phase (Figure 1) have been reported in FCC-structured HEAs, which can lead to local stress concentration and thus resistance to dislocation glide. In the “B2 + FCC” dual-phase AlCoCrFeNi₂.₁ HEA, the B2 phase plays a mechanical strengthening role, because it can block the glide of dislocations generated in the mechanically softer FCC phase. Our TEM characterizations of a fractured Ni10-HEA (Figure S4a, Supporting Information) reveal that the deformed FCC phase contains high-density structural defects, e.g., stacking faults (SFs), while the mechanically harder B2 phase has no feature of severe plastic deformation. It means that the glide of dislocations within the FCC phase is interrupted by the FCC/B2 phase boundary (PB). The cooling-enhanced ductility was also observed in FCC-structured HEAs (e.g., CrMnFeCoNi, known as the Cantor alloy), which can be attributed to the dominant deformation mechanism changing from dislocation glide at room temperature to deformation twinning at cryogenic temperature. We also observed the deformation twin in Ni10-HEA after tensile fracture at cryogenic temperature (Figure S5, Supporting Information). It also excludes the plasticity resulting from SIMT (i.e., stress-induced martensitic transformation) even at cryogenic temperature (Figure S5, Supporting Information). Without SIMT that accounts for the superelasticity, the present Ni10-HEA exhibits an elastic strain limit less than 0.6% (Figure S6, Supporting Information), unlike the remarkable Fe–Mn–Al–Ni-based HESMAs.
我们注意到，FCC 相内的纳米尺度晶格畸变和成分涨落（图 1）在 FCC 结构高熵合金中已有报道，它们会导致局部应力集中，从而阻碍位错滑移。在“B2 + FCC”双相 AlCoCrFeNi₂.₁ 高熵合金中，B2 相起力学强化作用，因为它能阻挡在力学上较软的 FCC 相中产生的位错滑移。我们对断裂后的 Ni10-HEA 进行的 TEM 表征（支持信息图 S4a）表明，变形的 FCC 相含有高密度结构缺陷，如层错（SF），而力学上更硬的 B2 相则没有严重塑性变形的特征。这说明 FCC 相内的位错滑移被 FCC/B2 相界（PB）所阻断。降温增强塑性的现象也曾在 FCC 结构高熵合金（如 CrMnFeCoNi，即 Cantor 合金）中被观察到，可归因于主导变形机制从室温下的位错滑移转变为低温下的形变孪生。我们也在 Ni10-HEA 低温拉伸断裂后观察到形变孪晶（支持信息图 S5）。这也排除了即便在低温下由 SIMT（即应力诱导马氏体相变）引起塑性的可能（支持信息图 S5）。由于没有导致超弹性的 SIMT，本 Ni10-HEA 的弹性应变极限小于 0.6%（支持信息图 S6），这与出色的 Fe–Mn–Al–Ni 基 HESMA 不同。
Next, we shall discuss the mechanism for the large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction in the present Ni10-HEA. Firstly, we have prepared a Ni-free Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀ medium-entropy alloy (named as “Ni0-MEA”), which bears an average B2 structure (Figure S7, Supporting Information). At room temperature, Ni0-MEA exhibits typical Joule magnetostriction effect and strong ferromagnetism (Figure S8, Supporting Information). Then, the large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction in Ni10-HEA should stem from the dominant FCC phase, rather than the B2 phase with very low volume fraction. As shown in Figure 5a, the magnetization loops of Ni10-HEA reveal the following features: i) the magnetization and demagnetization curves are nearly overlapped, which are recoverable and nonhysteretic; ii) the magnetization increases gradually with external magnetic field, having no abrupt change. These features exclude the typical magnetic-field-induced first-order phase transition in Ni10-HEA. According to the previous theoretical study, the volume magnetostriction of a given magnet can be attributed to the intrinsic coupling between crystal lattice and external magnetic field, and its ω can be expressed as: ω(M, T) = (κC/V)[M² − M₀²(T)] (1)
接下来我们讨论本 Ni10-HEA 中大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩的机理。首先，我们制备了一种无镍的 Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀ 中熵合金（命名为“Ni0-MEA”），它具有平均 B2 结构（支持信息图 S7）。在室温下，Ni0-MEA 表现出典型的焦耳磁致伸缩效应和强铁磁性（支持信息图 S8）。由此可见，Ni10-HEA 中大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩应源于占主导的 FCC 相，而非体积分数极低的 B2 相。如图 5a 所示，Ni10-HEA 的磁化回线揭示以下特征：(i) 磁化与退磁曲线几乎重合，可回复且无磁滞；(ii) 磁化强度随外磁场逐渐增大，没有突变。这些特征排除了 Ni10-HEA 中典型的磁场诱导一级相变。根据此前的理论研究，给定磁体的体积磁致伸缩可归因于晶格与外磁场之间的本征耦合，其 ω 可表示为：ω(M, T) = (κC/V)[M² − M₀²(T)]（式 1）
where κ is the compressibility of crystals, C is the magneto-volume coupling constant, V represents the volume, M₀(T) is the temperature-dependent spontaneous magnetization, and M is the magnetization under external magnetic field H. And a linear relation between ω and M⁴ can be established at the temperature where M² is directly proportional to H/M. Figure 5b shows the Arrott plots (i.e., M² vs H/M) of Ni10-HEA, and a linear fitting is applicable at 280 K. At this temperature, a linear relation between ω and M⁴ can be established, as shown in the inset of Figure 5b. Thus, the large volume magnetostriction of Ni10-HEA can be qualitatively ascribed to the large κ and C of the FCC phase.
式中 κ 为晶体的压缩率，C 为磁–体积耦合常数，V 为体积，M₀(T) 为随温度变化的自发磁化强度，M 为外磁场 H 下的磁化强度。在 M² 与 H/M 成正比的温度下，可建立 ω 与 M⁴ 之间的线性关系。图 5b 给出了 Ni10-HEA 的 Arrott 图（即 M² 对 H/M），在 280 K 时线性拟合适用。在该温度下，可建立 ω 与 M⁴ 之间的线性关系，如图 5b 插图所示。因此，Ni10-HEA 大的体积磁致伸缩可定性地归因于 FCC 相较大的 κ 和 C。
Secondly, as revealed by the in situ investigations above, the FCC matrix of Ni10-HEA bears a noncollinear magnetic state. The noncollinear magnetic state is schematically shown in the left channel of Figure 5c, derived from the magnetic spin configuration of the Fe–Ni Invar alloy. In such a configuration, the volume magnetostriction effect can be understood as follows. Figure 5d shows the schematic illustration of the energy landscapes for the paramagnetic, ferromagnetic and noncollinear configurations at the ground state, using the average lattice volume as the order parameter. And the larger the lattice volume is, the higher the average magnetization is. At the noncollinear configuration, the increased external field impels the magnitude and direction of the varied magnetic moments towards the ferromagnetic state (the right channel of Figure 5c). It leads to a transition of the energy valley from the low-spin small-volume (LS) state to the high-spin large-volume (HS) state, thus giving rise to a large volume expansion, as illustrated in Figure 5e. When the external field is removed, the forced magnetic moments will rapidly revert to their initial states, resulting in the nonhysteretic change.
其次，如上文原位研究所揭示，Ni10-HEA 的 FCC 基体处于非共线磁态。该非共线磁态示意于图 5c 左侧，源自 Fe–Ni 因瓦合金的磁自旋构型。在这样的构型中，体积磁致伸缩效应可理解如下。图 5d 以平均晶格体积为序参量，示意了基态下顺磁、铁磁和非共线构型的能量景观。晶格体积越大，平均磁化强度越高。在非共线构型中，增大的外场促使各异磁矩的大小和方向趋向铁磁态（图 5c 右侧）。这导致能谷从低自旋小体积（LS）态转变到高自旋大体积（HS）态，从而产生大的体积膨胀，如图 5e 所示。当外场撤去后，被迫取向的磁矩会迅速回复到初始状态，从而产生无磁滞的变化。
Thirdly, we also note that in many defect-bearing or compositionally fluctuated ferroelastic systems, nanoscale displacive phase transitions can take place, which result in the formation of strain nanodomains, such as the R-like nanodomains in Ti–Ni alloy and the α″ nanodomains in Ti-2448 alloy. The nanoscale strain fluctuations observed in the FCC matrix of Ni10-HEA (Figure 1g) are like the strain nanodomains in these systems. In addition, the nanoscale displacive phase transition will lead to the frequency dispersion of dynamic properties (e.g., internal friction peaks), like the observation in Figure 2c. It signifies that the distorted nanoregions with various sizes and inhomogeneous strain have different relaxation times and responses to the stress field of different frequency. Such a nanoscale first-order transition may also produce a weak and broad hump in the temperature-dependent specific heat curve, Cp(T). In the present Ni10-HEA, its Cp(T) also shows a weak and broad hump (Figure S9, Supporting Information), unlike those observed in the typical first-order martensitic transition and in the second-order paramagnetic–ferromagnetic transition. Supposing that the nanoscale first-order transition could undergo in the present Ni10-HEA, its transition kinetics should be severely limited, because the surrounding regions with distinct chemical compositions can provide strong resistance to the long-range strain ordering. The cyclic tensile stress–strain curves indicate a much smaller elastic strain limit in Ni10-HEA (Figure S6, Supporting Information) than that in the defect-carrying Ti–Ni alloys. And the deformation FCC twins at 123 K (much lower than the magnetic ordering temperature, Figure S4b, Supporting Information) also support the difficulties of long-range strain ordering. The distorted nanoregions may create local magnetic heterogeneities when their spin and lattice are strongly coupled with each other, thus helping the system collapse into the noncollinear magnetic state. With the increase of external magnetic field, the enhanced magnetic ordering of these nanoregions may lead to local lattice expansion due to the strong spin–lattice coupling, that is, a field-induced nanoscale first-order transition from nanoheterogeneities with low spin and small volume to those with high spin and large volume. Such a transition presumably makes a contribution to the large volume magnetostriction in the present Ni10-HEA.
第三，我们还注意到，在许多含缺陷或成分涨落的铁弹体系中，可发生纳米尺度的位移型相变，从而形成应变纳米畴，例如 Ti–Ni 合金中的类 R 纳米畴和 Ti-2448 合金中的 α″ 纳米畴。Ni10-HEA 的 FCC 基体中观察到的纳米尺度应变涨落（图 1g）与这些体系中的应变纳米畴相似。此外，纳米尺度的位移型相变会导致动态性能（如内耗峰）的频率弥散，正如图 2c 中的观察。这表明具有不同尺寸和不均匀应变的畸变纳米区，对不同频率的应力场具有不同的弛豫时间和响应。这种纳米尺度的一级转变还可能在随温度变化的比热曲线 Cp(T) 中产生一个微弱而宽化的鼓包。在本 Ni10-HEA 中，其 Cp(T) 也显示出一个微弱而宽化的鼓包（支持信息图 S9），这与典型一级马氏体相变以及二级顺磁–铁磁转变中所观察到的不同。假设本 Ni10-HEA 中能够发生纳米尺度一级转变，其转变动力学应受到严重限制，因为周围具有不同化学成分的区域会对长程应变有序提供强阻力。循环拉伸应力–应变曲线表明，Ni10-HEA 的弹性应变极限（支持信息图 S6）远小于含缺陷的 Ti–Ni 合金。而在 123 K（远低于磁有序温度，支持信息图 S4b）的形变 FCC 孪晶也佐证了长程应变有序的困难。当畸变纳米区的自旋与晶格强烈耦合时，它们可能产生局部磁异质区，从而帮助体系坍缩为非共线磁态。随着外磁场增大，由于强自旋–晶格耦合，这些纳米区增强的磁有序可能导致局部晶格膨胀，即一种磁场诱导的纳米尺度一级转变——从低自旋小体积的纳米异质区转变为高自旋大体积的纳米异质区。这种转变大概对本 Ni10-HEA 大的体积磁致伸缩有所贡献。
The present work may have the following implications. First, the novel Ni10-HEA exhibits a combination of large nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction and excellent mechanical properties, which can be utilized as a multi-component for magneto-electric composite films that require multi-axial stress, a detector for magnetic field strength as a substitute for the Hall probe, and a nonconducting volume-changed valve driven by the magnetic field. In addition, it exhibits large internal friction over a wide temperature range and an Elinvar-like effect (Figure 2c), which can be utilized as a high damping material and a precision instrument. Second, the development of magnetostrictive materials has been stagnant in recent years. Our work indicates that the design strategy of HEAs can open a new window for exploiting novel magnetostrictive materials. In fact, the nonhysteretic volume magnetostriction effect can be found in a wide compositional space in the present HEA system, for example, in another FCC-structured Fe₃₅Ni₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Co₁₀ HEA (Ni35-HEA) (Figure S10, Supporting Information). The volume magnetostriction of Ni35-HEA is smaller than that of Ni10-HEA, indicating a strong composition dependence. An in-depth understanding of the correlation between composition and magnetostriction performance in the present high-entropy system awaits further study in the future. Finally, previous experimental and simulation studies have usually considered the magnetic HEAs as an ideal linear ferromagnetic state. Our results suggest that HEAs could fall into a disordered noncollinear magnetic state, which may facilitate more and more fruitful investigations on magnetic HEAs.
本工作可能具有以下启示。第一，新颖的 Ni10-HEA 兼具大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩和优异的力学性能，可用作需要多轴应力的磁电复合薄膜的多功能组元、替代霍尔探头的磁场强度检测器，以及由磁场驱动的非导电变体积阀。此外，它在宽温区内呈现大的内耗和 Elinvar 样效应（图 2c），可用作高阻尼材料和精密仪器。第二，近年来磁致伸缩材料的发展一直停滞。我们的工作表明，高熵合金的设计策略可为开发新颖磁致伸缩材料开辟新窗口。事实上，本高熵合金体系在很宽的成分空间内都能找到无磁滞体积磁致伸缩效应，例如在另一种 FCC 结构的 Fe₃₅Ni₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Co₁₀ 高熵合金（Ni35-HEA）中（支持信息图 S10）。Ni35-HEA 的体积磁致伸缩小于 Ni10-HEA，表明存在强的成分依赖性。深入理解本高熵体系中成分与磁致伸缩性能之间的关联，尚待未来进一步研究。最后，以往的实验和模拟研究通常将磁性高熵合金视为理想的线性铁磁态。我们的结果表明，高熵合金可能落入一种无序的非共线磁态，这或将推动对磁性高熵合金越来越丰富的研究。

结论3. Conclusions

Large nonhysteresis volume magnetostriction has been achieved in the Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ high-entropy alloy with a FCC phase of an inherent noncollinear magnetic configuration. The large volume magnetostriction over a wide temperature range can be attributed to the magnetic-field-induced reversible transition from low-spin small-volume state to high-spin large-volume state. The local lattice distortion and compositional fluctuations at nanoscale can contribute to the collapse of magnetic ordering, thus yielding nonhysteresis responses in external magnetic fields. Owing to the secondary phase hardening and the deformation-twinning effect of the heterogeneous matrix, this alloy also possesses excellent tensile properties, i.e., a combination of high strength and large tensile strain. In addition, the unique nanoscale strain fluctuation and the magnetic nanoheterogeneities also result in high damping capacity. The combination of such unusual properties makes the present Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ high-entropy alloy a promising candidate for multifunctional applications. The present findings may not only provide insight into designing unconventional and technologically important magnetostrictive materials, but also broaden the knowledge of magnetic high-entropy alloys.
在具有本征非共线磁构型 FCC 相的 Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ 高熵合金中，实现了大的无磁滞体积磁致伸缩。宽温区内的大体积磁致伸缩可归因于磁场诱导的、从低自旋小体积态到高自旋大体积态的可逆转变。纳米尺度的局部晶格畸变和成分涨落有助于磁有序的坍缩，从而在外磁场中产生无磁滞响应。得益于第二相硬化和异质基体的形变孪生效应，该合金还具有优异的拉伸性能，即高强度与大拉伸应变的组合。此外，独特的纳米尺度应变涨落和磁性纳米异质区还带来高阻尼能力。如此非常规性能的组合，使本 Fe₃₅Co₃₅Al₁₀Cr₁₀Ni₁₀ 高熵合金成为多功能应用的有力候选。本研究不仅可为设计非常规且具有重要技术价值的磁致伸缩材料提供启示，也拓宽了对磁性高熵合金的认识。
